# Satchida Procedural Art · style API cheat-sheet

English translation of the upstream interface cheat-sheet (section 2 of the original skill book).
Method names are code identifiers and unchanged; only the prose has been translated.
Shared helpers:

```python
import sys; sys.path.insert(0, "<this-skill>/lib")
from core import spline, curve, blob_pts, fbm1d, blur, polygon_mask
```

## Ink wash — `Painting`
- `paper()`, `ridge(base, peaks=[(x,h,w)])`, `peak(SX, SY, shoulders=...)`
- `wash(ridge, dens, decay, tex, edge, mist_y)`: ink-washed mountains, darker at the top and lighter below, with wet rim marks; occludes the layers behind it
- `cun()` texture strokes, `moss()` moss dots, `ridge_line()` pale ridge accent, `trails(ridge, summit, red_index)` paths converging on the summit, `mist(y, h)` bands of fog
- Brush: `stroke(pts, width, ink, dry, taper, red)`
- Motifs: `pine()`, `rock()`, `sun()`, `birds()`, `pagoda()`, `traveller()`, `dot()`
- Title and seal: `title_vertical()`, `seal()`

## Watercolour — `Watercolor`
- `paper()`: cold-press watercolour-paper texture
- Masks: `shape(pts, soft, ragged)` — soft 1–4 gives a crisp water edge, 15–40 a wet, feathered spread; `band(pts, width)` is a broad brush-shaped mask
- `glaze(mask, colour, strength, edge, gran, bloom, variation)`: a transparent glaze; layers deepen and mix colours like real pigment
- `gradient_wash(y0, y1, top, bottom)`: a wet-graded sky
- Strokes: `stroke()` a dry coloured brush, `line()` a fine drawn line, `lift(mask)` lifting out white, `splatter()` flicked specks
- `soften(mask)` watercolour-softens any mask's edge; `blobs([(cx, cy, rx, ry)])` merges a group of masses into one (tree crowns, shrubs, clouds); `mirror(mask, waterline_y)` a water reflection with broken ripples
- `Watercolor.petal_pts(x, y, angle, length, width)`: a petal or leaf outline; `hexc('#rrggbb')` converts a colour

## Paper collage — `Collage`
- `background(colour, texture)`
- `piece(pts or mask, colour, texture, torn, rim, shadow)`: glues down one piece of paper. `torn` is how torn the edge is, `rim` the white tear rim, `shadow` the paper's drop shadow; texture: paper / kraft / crayon / newsprint / notebook
- Shape masks: `circle_mask()`, `cloud_mask()`, `Collage.star_pts()`
- Crayon and light: `crayon(pts, width, colour)` crayon lines, `dot()`, `glow()`
- `face(cx, cy, r, mood='smile'|'sleep'|'dots')`: happy eyes, mouth, blush
- `text(s, x, y, size, colour, vertical)`

## Coloured pencil (Korean style) — `ColorPencil`
- `paper()`: fine paper tooth
- Masks: `mask_poly(pts)`, `mask_blob()`, `mask_ellipse()`
- `hatch(mask, colour, pressure, angle)`: short directional hatching fills colour. The wax only catches the paper's peaks; the higher `pressure` is, the deeper it presses into the valleys; edges fade automatically
- `shade(mask, colour, angle=different angle)`: cross-hatching for shadows
- `outline(pts, '#8a6a5a')`: pale brown pencil contour; `line(pts)` for open lines
- `sparkle(x, y, r)` white gel-pen highlight; `dots(mask, colour)` for strawberry seeds, sugar grains and the like
- Don't paint white objects white (invisible on white paper): use a warm milky white with pale violet-grey or pale blue-grey shadows

## Anime — `Anime`
- `sky(top, mid, horizon_col, horizon)`: gradient sky
- `cumulus(cx, base_y, w, h, light)`: cumulus clouds. Many irregular masses stacked back to front; each mass's shadow side is the crescent left by "the mass minus itself shifted toward the light", a hard-edged crescent
- `sun_flare(x, y)`: halo, god rays, lens flare; `sparkles()`; finish with `bloom()` glow and `vignette()`
- `field(top_y, colours)`: flat-banded fields; `pole()` utility poles, `wire(p0, p1, sag)` sagging wires
- Flats and line art: `fill(mask, colour)`, `gradient()`, `screen()`; `lines(pts, width, colour)` for clean linework; `poly()`, `circle()`
- Characters: two flat colours (lit and shadow tone) plus a dark outline

## Editorial (risograph) — `Editorial`
- Shapes: `circle()`, `ellipse()`, `rrect()` rounded rectangle, `blob()`, `poly()`
- `fill(mask, spot ink)`: prints a spot-colour ink layer with grain, uneven density and mis-registration, and **overprints** (overlaps multiply)
- `halftone(mask, spot, cell, angle, amount)`: halftone dots for shading
- `line(pts, ink, width, wobble, breaks)`: a wobbly hand-drawn ink line; `dashes()` dashed lines; `stars()` small cross stars
- Keep spot colours to 4–5 and deliberately let them overlap to mix a third colour

## Impasto oil — `OilPainting`
- Prepare two images first: a reference colour image `ref` (H×W×3, what the picture should look like) and a flow-direction field `ang` (H×W, the stroke angle at every pixel — the sky swirls, wheat sweeps sideways, trees run upward)
- `layer(ref, ang, length, width, density, mask)`: lays one layer of strokes. Big brushes for the ground, then medium, and last a detail brush only where it matters (`mask`)
- `stroke_at(x, y, angle, length, width, colour)`: one stroke at a chosen place (a crow, a signature, an accent)
- `render()` lights it automatically: flat-topped ridged paint, bristle grooves, thicker paint where the brush lands

## Ukiyo-e — `Ukiyoe`
- `block(mask, colour)`: prints one flat colour block, with wood grain and registration drift
- `bokashi(mask, colour, y_strong, y_fade)`: a graded wipe, strong at one edge and fading out at the other — for skies and seas
- `key(pts, width)`: the black key-block outline
- Motifs: `cloud_band(x0, x1, y, h, glow)` opaque cloud bands, `seigaiha(mask, colour, r)` overlapping-wave pattern
- Title cartouche and seal: `cartouche(x, y, w, h, 'title')`, `seal()`
- Shapes: `poly()`, `rect()`, `circle()`

## Pixel art — `PixelArt`
- Draw at low resolution (default 320×180, upscaled ×6). All coordinates are integer pixels
- Shapes: `rect()`, `poly()`, `circle()`, `line()` (Bresenham), `outline(mask)`, `put(x, y)`
- Dithering: `gradient(x0, y0, x1, y1, c0, c1, steps)` a banded gradient with dithered transitions between bands; `dither(mask, colour, alpha)` Bayer-matrix dithering; `glow(cx, cy, r, colour)` a dithered glow
- Else: `text('CAFE', x, y, colour, size)` a 3×5 pixel font, `rain()` rain streaks
- Deliver as PNG (lossless) so the pixels stay crisp

## Stop-motion clay — `Clay`
- Maintains a height map (in pixels) and a colour map at once; lit at the end with one studio-light pass. Parts rest on whatever is beneath them by default (`base=None`); far things first, and later parts sit on top
- `backdrop(colour)` the baseboard; `gradient(top, bottom, y0, y1)` builds a colour field you can pass to `pad`/`smear`
- Parts: `pad(mask, colour, height, round)` a pressed slab (small `round` is a hard-edged sheet, large a puffy pillow), `ball(x, y, r)` little balls, `snake(pts, r, taper=(start, end))` rolled snakes (lines, surf, mouths, stems), `text(s, x, y, size)` puffy clay letters
- Tool marks: `groove(pts, width, depth)` scored lines, `poke(x, y, r, depth)` poked holes; `smear(mask, colour field)` a big finger-smeared area (drags neighbouring colours together and leaves soft fingerprint ridges)
- `marble=('#colour', 0.25)` two colours not fully kneaded; surfaces carry little lumps and fingerprints automatically
- Masks: `circle()`, `ellipse()`, `poly()`, `blob()`, `rrect()`

## Cyanotype — `Cyanotype`
- The idea is "block UV" rather than "apply pigment": objects placed on the sheet block light by their opacity; transmissions multiply; finally it develops to Prussian blue
- `coat(x0, y0, x1, y1)` brushing on the emulsion: horizontal strokes, dry broken strokes at the ends of each pass, a few drips at the edges
- `place(mask, opacity, lift, texture)`: opacity about 0.85 for thin leaves, 1.0 for veins, about 0.3 for fluff; `lift` is the height off the paper (0 pressed flat and sharp, 1–3 a soft penumbra); `texture` is unevenness in the leaf flesh
- Batch-drawing masks: `shapes([outlines...])` draws many leaves at once, `lines([polylines...], width, taper)` many stems, veins and wisps; `Cyanotype.leaf_pts(x, y, angle, length, width, tip, blunt)` a leaf outline
- Text: `label()` white handwriting (written on a transparency and printed together), `pencil()` a pencil note written on the margin after developing

## Cross-stitch — `Stitch`
- Everything lives on the grid of fabric holes (`cell` pixels per cell). `cells(mask)` converts a pixel mask to cells, `at(gx, gy)` returns a cell centre
- Stitches: `cross(cell, colour)` cross-stitch, `half(cell, colour)` half stitch (skies, clouds, smoke), `back(pts, colour, step)` back-stitch (the path snaps to holes, one stitch spans about `step` cells), `running(pts)` running stitch (dashed), `satin(mask, colour, angle)` satin stitch (padded and glossy), `satin_text(s, x, y, size)`, `knot(x, y, colour)` French knot
- Charts: `chart(['..#..', '.###.'], gx, gy, {'#': colour})` stitch a character chart; `letters('HOME', gx, gy, colour, scale)` a 5×7 alphabet
- `label(x0, y0, x1, y1, 'EST. 2026')` a sewn-on woven label

## Vintage instrument panel — `Panel`
- Every part is lit by the same upper-left key light: edges facing the light are bright, edges away from it dark, raised parts cast a shadow to the lower right
- Materials: `surface(mask, material, colour, height)`; material is wood (teak), brushed (brushed aluminium), plastic, painted, fabric (grille cloth, `lurex=` metallic thread); `recess(mask, material, colour, depth)` a recessed window; `background()`, `paint()`, `glow()`, `drop_shadow()`
- Controls: `knob(x, y, r, angle)` a machined knob, `button()` a piano key (`pressed=True` pressed in), `toggle()` a toggle switch, `lamp()` a jewel lamp, `magic_eye()` the green magic-eye tuning tube, `meter()` a pointer meter, `screw()` screws, `grille()` a punched grille, `badge()` a chrome script nameplate
- Printing: `text(s, x, y, size, spacing=)` silkscreened type, `scale_arc()` an arc scale, `scale_linear()` a linear scale, `glass(mask)` glass reflection

## Sticker collage × receipt — `Sticker`
- Idea: draw each thing as flat vector art on its own little transparent sheet (`Art`), then decide its material: `stick()` makes it a die-cut vinyl sticker, `lay()` lays it down flat as paper
- `board(colour, kind='cream'|'kraft')`: a cream or light kraft board with paper grain, mottling, fibres and flecks
- `art(w, h)` returns `Art`: masks `circle/ring/ellipse/rrect/poly/blob/line/text` (mixed Latin and CJK, `stroke` to thicken); colour `fill(mask, colour, alpha, clip)`, `gradient()`, `radial()`; `crescent(mask, dx, dy)` a shading crescent; `speckle()` sprinkled flour or seeds; `cut()` punch holes; `texture('kraft')` kraft-paper grain
- `stick(art, cx, cy, rot, border=14, peel='tr'|'tl'|'br'|'bl', peel_size, lift, gloss)`: a die-cut sticker. The white border follows the rounded cut, with cut-edge thickness, laminate gloss and a diagonal sheen, a contact shadow plus a soft drop shadow; `peel` lifts a corner, showing the pale backing and its own shadow
- `lay(art, cx, cy, rot, curl, lift)`: paper lying flat (a receipt, a tag), ends curling slightly, the shadow longer where it lifts; returns a local-to-canvas mapping `T(x, y)` used to write or stamp on the paper
- `receipt(w, h)` returns `Receipt`: serrated tear edges, streaky grey thermal print (broken-pin column streaks, feed density, small dropouts); `centre()` centre-printed rows, `row(y, name, amount, was='old price')` with dotted leaders and a strike-through, `rule('dash'|'stars'|'double')`, `barcode()`, `crease(y)` a fold crease
- `tape(cx, cy, length, rot, colour, pattern='stripe'|'dot'|'grid'|'plain')` translucent washi tape with hand-torn ends; `tag_art(w, h, colour, grommet colour)` a price tag (`.hole` is the hole), `twine(points)` two-colour baker's twine
- `stamp(cx, cy, r, ring text, centre text, small text, colour, rot)` a round rubber stamp (translucent ink, uneven pressure, pooled ink at the letter edges, missed pinholes); `pen(points, colour, width)` a red ballpoint (ticks, a ring round the total, strike-throughs)
- Ready-made stickers: `lettering_art([(text, colour), ...], size)` bold lettering, `label_art()` a capsule label, `badge_art(r, colour, '-20%', top, bottom)` a scalloped sale badge

## Lab notebook × stickers — `Notebook`
- Scene: `desk()` a graphite blue-grey desk; `notebook(x0, y0, x1, y1, tabs, stack, ear, margin, header, grid, major)` a whole notebook (hard back cover, divider tabs, offset page edges, cyan graph paper, a red double rule, punch holes, a dog-ear); `binding()` the wire loops (lit as metal tubes, with a shadow)
- Pencil: `pencil(pts, width, pressure, jitter)` one hand-drawn stroke (spline-smoothed, hand wobble, light touch-down and lift-off); `pencils([...])`; `rule(x0, y0, x1, y1)` a ruled straight line; `hatch(mask, angle, spacing, pressure)` hatching; `checkbox(x, y, size, checked)`; `arrowhead(tip, direction)`; `axes(x0, y0, x1, y1, xlim, ylim, xticks, yticks, xlabels, ylabels)` returns `to_px(x, y)`
- Graphite only catches the paper's peaks: low pressure is grainy pale grey, high pressure fills the valleys; line width about 2–3.5 px
- Highlighter `highlight(x0, x1, y, h, tilt, alpha)`: multiply blend, sliced off at an angle at the start with pooled ink, fuzzy edges, lengthwise fibre streaks, a broken tail; two overlapping passes go darker
- Red pen: `pen(pts, width)`, `pen_loop(cx, cy, rx, ry, rot, turns=1.12)` an unclosed loop, `pen_arrow(pts, head)`, `pen_underline(x0, x1, y, double)`
- Text `text(s, x, y, size, colour, font, anchor, rot, spacing, weight, medium)`: font is sans / sans_bold / typewriter / cjk_sans (mixed Latin and CJK switches per character); medium is ink, print, typed (typewriter — each character differs slightly in density and baseline), pencil, opaque; `text_width()` measures width
- Paper bits: `sticky(cx, cy, w, h, rot, colour)` a sticky note (top flat, bottom curled with a shadow), returns a Frame — write on it with `f.pt(lx, ly)` and `rot=f.rot`; `sticker(cx, cy, w, h, rot, colour, shape='rrect'|'circle', lines=[...])` a die-cut sticker; `label_tape(s, x, y, size, rot)` label-maker tape; `stain(cx, cy, r)` a coffee-cup ring
- Props: `pencil_prop(x, y, angle, length, width, label, end='eraser'|'plain')` a hexagonal pencil
- Angles: `rot` is counter-clockwise positive (as in PIL); `angle` (pencil and arrow direction) is a screen angle, clockwise positive. Common colours: `INK SOFT LEAD RED HI MINT MINTD`

## Chalkboard — `Chalkboard`
- Layers: slate paint → chalk layer (premultiplied colour + alpha, removable by the eraser) → things in front of the board (wooden frame, chalk tray, sticks, eraser and its shadow) → room light and vignette
- Board: `slate()` cloudy uneven dark-green slate paint; `dust(amount, specks, haze)` settled chalk dust, thicker toward the tray; `frame(rail, top, ledge)` a mitred wooden frame plus tray, which sets the writing `area` and the tray height `tray_y`. The default size is known at construction, so dust and swipe marks can be drawn before the frame
- Eraser: `swipe(pts, width, strength, haze, smear, keep)` wipes along a path: removes chalk, drags a residue along the wipe direction, combs fine felt marks, dust pooled at the felt's ends; `smudge(cx, cy, w, h, rot)` a short rub; `with cb.erased(keep, angle, smear):` everything drawn inside the block becomes a half-erased ghost of the previous lesson
- Tips: `stroke(pts, colour, width, pressure, jitter)` / `strokes([...])`; `line()` a freehand straight line, `rule()` a ruled one; `box(x0, y0, x1, y1, gap=(xa, xb))` four edges overshooting, the top edge may leave a gap for a label; `loop(cx, cy, rx, ry, rot, turns)` an unclosed loop, `circle()`; `arrow(pts, head)` a two-stroke arrow that piles up at the tip; `underline(x0, x1, y, double)`; `wave(p0, p1, wavelength, amplitude)` a light-wave curve with an arrow; `dashed()` dashed lines drawn segment by segment; `dot()`; `hatch(mask, angle, spacing)` hatching; `propto(x, y, size)` a hand-drawn ∝, returns its width
- Side of the stick: `side(p0, p1, stick, pressure)` a chalk dragged on its side; `shade(mask, colour, angle, stick, pressure, overlap, weight)` side-tone fills, `weight` is a 0–1 full-canvas pressure map, so you can build gradients
- Chalk only catches the board tooth's peaks: pressure 0.2–0.4 is sparse grain (fills), 0.8–0.95 for writing and lines
- Text `text(s, x, y, size, colour, font, anchor, pressure, weight, rot, spacing, hand, grain)`: font is sans / sans_bold / cjk_sans, a missing glyph falls back to another set automatically; every character is rotated, offset and weighted a little differently (`hand=0` is typeset); `text_width()` measures width
- Props: `chalk_stick(x, cb.tray_y, length, colour, rot, worn='right'|'left'|None)` a stick of chalk, one end worn at an angle; `eraser(x, cb.tray_y + 2)` the felt eraser; `crumbs(x0, x1, n)` chalk crumbs
- Colours: `WHITE YELLOW PINK BLUE` first, `RED ORANGE GREEN VIOLET` for the spectrum and coloured chalks, plus `DUST`; all angles and rots are counter-clockwise positive

## Cyberpunk — `Cyberpunk`
- Idea: a horizontal pinhole camera looking down a street, world coordinates X right, Y up, Z forward, in metres. Everything is written into an HDR buffer (albedo, emission, z-buffer depth, reflection mirror rows); call order never decides occlusion; at `render` all lighting is computed at once: ambient + light spill, depth fog (fog colour is the city's scattered light), ground reflections, steam, rain, film
- Construction: `Cyberpunk(W, H, seed, focal=1150, vp=(x, y), eye=1.7, fog=125, wall=9.5, road=6, record=True)`; with `record=False`, `stage()` writes no snapshots and rendering is faster. Camera helpers: `project(X, Y, Z)`, `ground_row(Z)`, `near_z(side)`
- Distance: `sky()` low clouds lit from below by the street lamps; `skyline(z, x_range, heights, widths)` far tower silhouettes with pin-point windows and red aircraft lights; `light(x, y, r, colour, z)` a small point light
- Buildings: `building(side, z0, z1, height, wall, colour, lit, shops=(lit shops, shutters, dark shops, vending machines))` a perspective block, its front carrying a street-level facade texture (window grids, air-conditioner boxes, rain streaks, shops, sign lettering — the right-hand facade's letters mirror automatically)
- Neon: `blade_sign(side, z, Y0, Y1, 'text', colour, reach, border=colour, latin='BAR', dead=[indices], flicker=[indices], kind='neon'|'box', text_colour, level)` a vertical sign reaching out over the street, facing the camera; `sign(z, X0, X1, Y0, Y1, ..., vertical=False)` a horizontal sign or a standing lightbox; `neon_text(s, x, y, size, colour, z, vertical, dead, flicker)`, `neon_path(points, colour, z)` any tube you like
- Hologram: `hologram(z, X0, X1, Y0, Y1, 'MOON JELLY', 'SEA MOON', level, jelly=(x, y), sub_y)` a translucent projected ad (a moon jellyfish, a vertical mincho title, scanlines, tear slices, colour fringes, a projection edge) — additive light only, and it lights the fog and walls around it
- Street: `street(crossing=(z, width), puddles)` wet asphalt, kerbs, paving, a crossing, puddles; `puddle(X, Z, rx, rz)` a placed puddle (put it where a near sign reflects, and the whole sign mirrors into it); `grate(X, Z)` a drain grate
- Above: `walkway(z, Y, thick, deep, windows=None)` a footbridge gate (hang a `neon_text` on its face); `cables(n, z=(near, far), Y=(low, high))` wires; `light_trail(3-D control points, colour, pair, strobe, fade)` flying-car light trails (left-bound traffic: red tail lights, warm white head lights)
- Atmosphere: `steam(X, Z, height, width, drift, density)` grate steam; `mist()` thin fog lying only on the far street; `figure(X, Z, umbrella='clear'|'dark'|None)` a back view under an umbrella (rim-lit by the neon on both sides, no face); `rain(far, mid, near, slant, rings)` three layers of rain and splash rings; `glitch(y, h, shift, split)` one torn scanline — only in the finished frame, use it once
- Colour constants `MAGENTA CYAN AMBER RED WHITE`; `save('x.jpg')` defaults to quality 88, 4:4:4 chroma, so neon edges stay crisp

## Hand-drawn cartoon — `Cartoon`
- Idea: follow the order an animator draws one key frame — blue-pencil rough → ink → flats → cel shadows → crayon/screentone → effects → type. Every stroke lands in one "group" by kind: `rough` pencil, `ink` ink lines, `flat` flats, `shade` shadows/highlights/blush, `texture` crayon/screentone/hatching, `fx` effects, `type` lettering. `stage(name, show=[groups...], rough=amount)` shows only those groups, so a finished drawing can be replayed in drawing order; `with c.group(ink='ink_bg'):` re-routes one kind into another group, `with c.group('fx'):` re-routes everything
- Occlusion: things are opaque; `fill` carves away the ink lines and colours already behind it (hidden lines removed automatically), so draw far things first; `fx` and `type` are overlays composited last — their carving only applies when shown, so stage snapshots never show holes
- Ink: `line(pts, width, taper)` a brush line (thin landing, swollen middle, pointed lift-off, hand wobble and pressure drift); `outline(pts, width, weight, gaps)` a closed contour drawn in one stroke, both ends pointed and overlapping, heavier on the shaded lower right by `weight`, `gaps=n` leaves a few small breaks; `ink_fill()` solid ink masses, `dot()` ink dots. Every ink line first sketches itself in blue pencil (`rough=False` turns that off); `sketch()` draws pencil construction lines only
- Colour: `fill(pts|mask, colour, slop)` a flat fill, offset wholesale by `slop` px with wandering edges, returns a `Mask` usable as a clip; `shade(shape, colour, offset=(dx, dy), clip)` a hard-edged cel shadow (the shape minus itself shifted toward the light gives the shaded crescent); with `offset=None` it uses the shape as given; `glint(pts, width)` a white highlight pen; `shape(pts, colour, shade=(colour, dx, dy), shade2=...)` does flats, two shadows and an outline in one call; `darker(colour)` picks the shadow tone
- Texture: `crayon(shape, colour, pressure, angle)` crayon (catches only the tooth peaks, streaks along the stroke, ragged; `pressure` may be a full-canvas array for gradients); `tone(shape, colour, cell, angle, amount)` screentone; `hatch(shape, angle, spacing)` ink hatching (contact shadows); `blush()` blush
- Characters: `face(cx, cy, size, mood, rot)`, mood is happy (^ ^ eyes with an open tongue-out mouth), wow (round eyes and an o mouth), sleepy, smile, wink, with blush built in; `arm(shoulder, hand, bend, hand, thumb)` a rubber-hose arm with a white mitten glove that has a thumb (draw arms before the body)
- Effects (into the fx overlay by default; `fx=False` draws them as ordinary objects): `sparkle()` four-point sparkles, `speed_lines(p0, p1, angle)` speed lines, `steam()` steam, `puff()` scalloped smoke puffs, `burst()` an explosion star frame, `crumbs(..., avoid=mask)` flying crumbs, `pop_lines()` emphasis dashes
- Type (into the type overlay by default): `letter(s, x, y, size, fill, depth, depth_colour)` big title letters (rounded CJK with a comic Latin face, each letter slightly rotated and jumping, extruded shadow, top-left white edge and a heavy ink outline); `note()` handwritten notes; `arrow()` hand-drawn arrows; `badge(x, y, r, '1')` a numbered circle; `pencil_note()` a blue-pencil number in the corner; `text_width()` measures width
- Shapes: `Cartoon.rrect_pts(x0, y0, x1, y1, r or (tl, tr, br, bl))`, `ellipse_pts(cx, cy, rx, ry, rot, a0, a1)` (with a0/a1 it is an arc), `densify()` a polyline that keeps sharp corners, `place(local points, cx, cy, scale, rot)` to place an object drawn in local coordinates; `mask(pts)` a full-canvas mask you can multiply and subtract with numpy
- Boiling lines: `Cartoon(..., boil=2.0)` — every `stage()` re-draws the ink lines with a new smooth displacement field (1–2 px); colour and pencil rough stay still; `save()` uses the first draw
- All angles are counter-clockwise positive (as in PIL); colour constants `INK PAPER PENCIL WHITE RED YELLOW PINK ORANGE`

## Isometric 2.5D — `Isometric`
- Idea: a true isometric orthographic camera plus a small ray tracer. Every part is an analytic solid (convex polyhedra bounded by planes, cylinders and cones on any axis, ellipsoids clip-able by planes) intersected per pixel into a G-buffer (depth, normal, albedo, material); draw order never decides occlusion. The same solids cast a shadow map from the sun and a height map from directly above that records "column tops and bottoms"; lighting is computed at the end: sky ambient × AO + sun × N·L × soft shadows (PCSS, penumbra widening with occluder distance)
- Construction: `Isometric(W, H, seed, scale=px per cell, look=(x, y, z), at=(screen fractions x, y), sun=(x, y, z), ss=2, floor=-2.4, record=True)`. World x heads screen lower-left, y lower-right, z up, one cell = 1. `iso(x, y, z)` world → screen; `unproject(sx, sy, z)` screen → the world point on that height plane (use it to place floating things by screen position)
- Ground: `backdrop(top, bottom, glow, centre)` a pale gradient plus the unseen floor under the base (it catches the slab's shadow); `slab(x0, y0, x1, y1, z0, z1, layers)` a floating layered base; `tiles(levels, heights, colours, soil, gap, paved=(4,))` chamfered tiles (0 sea, 1 sand, 2 grass, 3 terrace, 4 paving); `sea(shallow, deep, foam, waves)` the sea (shore foam, a broken second wave line, a depth transition, short wave strokes, translucent side walls); `top_z(x, y)` reads the tile height; `road()` a road with a centre dash; `path(cells)` flagstones
- Solids: `box(x0, y0, z0, x1, y1, z1, colour, side, bevel)` a chamfered box; `obox(centre, size, yaw, pitch, roll)`; `prism(pts, z0, z1)`; `convex([(normal, d)], lo, hi)`; `cylinder(x, y, z0, z1, r, r1=None)` (with `r1` it is a cone / frustum); `tube(p0, p1, r)`; `ellipsoid(centre, radii, yaw, pitch, roll, clip=[(normal, d)])`, `sphere()`, `dome()`. All take `paint(P, n, tag)` (colour by world position: mortar joints, soil layers, tile courses, louvres, solar cells, card faces), `mat='matte'|'water'|'glass'|'soft'|'gloss'|'card'`, `cast=` (whether it casts a shadow), `ao=` (whether it enters the height map: floating things and thin wires turn it off)
- Buildings: `building(x0, y0, x1, y1, z, h, wall, roof='gable'|'hip'|'shed'|'flat', roof_colour, axis, windows=(rows, count on +x, count on +y), door=('+x', position), trim)`; `roof()`; `window(x, y, z, w, h, facing)` (white frame, cross mullions, angled sky light, a sill); `door()`; `sign(x, y, z, w, h, 'text', facing)`
- Nature and props: `tree(x, y, z, size, kind='round'|'pine'|'poplar'|'bush', flowers=)`, `rock()`, `cloud()` a flat-bottomed cloud, `windmill(x, y, z, h, yaw, spin)`, `windsock()`, `dish()` a satellite dish, `solar_panel()`, `fence(pts, z, h, closed)`, `pier()`, `boat()`, `balloon()`
- Infographics: `card(x, y, z, w, h, facing='+x', title, tag, value, unit, note, series, icon='wind'|'temp'|'rain'|'balloon'|'sun', anchor=(x, y, z), accent)` a data card standing in the air (type printed on the iso plane, with a thin leader and an anchor); `compass(x, y, z, r, north)` a compass lying on the ground; `text()`, `rule()`, `dot()` flat layout on the top layer
- Light: the sun comes from the upper left behind; the top faces are brightest, the +x faces (lower left) next, the +y faces (lower right) get only skylight; `sun_c / sky_c / hor_c / gnd_c`, `soft`, `pen` (min / max penumbra) are tunable. No neon glow, no bloom, no outlines

## Continuous line art — `LineArt`
- Idea: the whole picture is one path. Build each shape as points, then `chain()` strings them in drawing order into a single line; the line is rasterised once into a "time map" (every sub-pixel remembers how many pixels in, the pen first passed it), so any draw-on stage is exactly "time ≤ s"
- Ground: `backdrop('night'|'cream')` a deep-blue velvet ground (brighter in the middle, fine grain) or cream paper; sets the default line colour (gold / ink) and the accent (amber / vermilion)
- Shapes (static methods returning points): `smooth(control points)` a spline, `poly(points, r, sharp=(indices,))` a rounded polyline, `arc(cx, cy, rx, ry, a0, a1)`, `scallop(cx, cy, r, a0, a1, bumps, depth)` scalloped edges for canopies and shrubs, `place(points, origin, angle, scale)` local-coordinate placement, `curl(P, s, r, span, side, aspect)` a loop-the-loop inserted at pixel s of the path, `retrace(points)` out and back along the same line
- Path: `chain([(name, points), ...])` returns `(P, marks)`, gaps bridged by tangent-continuous cubics; `trim(P, s0, s1)` trims by length; `locate(P, (x, y))` the length at the nearest point; `at(P, s)` a point and direction; `length(P)`
- The line: `line(P, width, pressure=[(s0, s1, factor)], sheen, glow, hot)`: light touch-down, a long pointed lift-off, slow pressure drift, heavier in sharp turns, lifted and thinned where the line doubles back; on dark grounds it is a gold wire that brightens with angle and carries a faint tight glow
- Animation: `draw_to(s or a marks name)` moves the pen tip; while unfinished the tip carries a bright dot and the just-drawn stretch is brighter; `stage(name)`
- Accent: `accent(mask, colour, glow, warm)`, masks from `rect()`, `disc()`; on dark grounds it glows and warms nearby lines
- Text: `text(s, x, y, size, colour, font='display', spacing)` (a Didot-like serif; CJK switches to a thin Song face automatically), `text_width()`, `hairline()` thin decorative rules
- Colour constants `GOLD GOLD_HI GOLD_LO AMBER VERMILION NIGHT CREAM INK`; `save('x.jpg')` defaults to quality 88, 4:4:4

## Soft 3D — `Soft3D`
- Real 3D: `Soft3D(W, H, seed, eye, target, fov)` a pinhole camera, world coordinates X right, Y up, Z away from the lens; one ray per pixel, computed on the CPU with numpy
- Ball pit `ball_field(x0, x1, z0, z1, r, gap, colours, weights, mat)`: thousands of balls on a grid, rays found per grid cell by DDA, one sphere tested per cell — 100,000 balls and it is still fast; `tint_balls(x, z, radius, colours, chance)` re-colours locally; `floor(colour)` the floor seen in the gaps
- Subjects (signed distance fields, marched only inside their own bounding box): `text(s, at, size, depth, colour, mat, yaw, spacing, bob, roll, turn, dot)` puffy extruded letters (glyph → exact distance transform → extrude, round all over, then inflate; `dot=colour` swaps the i/j dots for a ball; returns {'heroes', 'dots', 'centre'}); `rbox()` a rounded box, `capsule()`, `cylinder()` a rounded cylinder, `torus()` a torus
- `part(heap, reach, pile, pile_top)`: the subject pushes up out of the ball sea; displaced balls fall into the nearest free pockets and heap at its feet (`pile` is a multiple of the displaced count, `pile_top` the heap's top height — keep it from hiding the letters' lower half); the ball sea around rises a little
- Loose balls: `ball(x, y, z, r, colour)` at a chosen place; `drop(x, z, r, colour, roll)` falls from above until it hits something, then rolls downhill into a pocket (roll=0 stops at the landing point, e.g. riding on the letters' shoulder)
- Lights: `sky(top, horizon, strength, haze, haze_start)` a sky dome (background gradient, ground bounce, far haze); `key(azimuth, elevation, strength, size)` a big softbox key, larger `size` = softer shadows; `rim()` a rim light; `softbox()` a light panel seen only in reflections. Azimuth is measured from the lens: 0 behind the camera, −90 left, +90 right, 180 behind the subject
- Materials `mat=`: candy (varnished candy, sharp panel highlights), satin (satin balls), jelly, matte, pearl; crevices and shadows deepen and saturate instead of greying out
- `lens(focus, aperture)` depth of field (`focus` takes text()'s return value or a point; best set together with the camera); `film(bloom, vignette, grain)`
- `save(path, stages_dir, ss=1.5)` supersamples the final image; `stage(name)` is a true preview render of the current scene — before `sky()` is called it is a grey clay render

## Shape morph — `Morph`
- Idea: everything is ONE closed outline; two outlines resampled to the same point count (default `Morph.N = 256`), given the same winding and an aligned start point, then blended point by point, is the morph; background and shape colours switch along
- Shape library `shape(name, cx, cy, size, rot, flip, stretch)`: `SHAPES` holds circle / square / triangle / star / heart / drop / cloud / snowflake / mountain / river / wave / sun / moon / leaf / bird / fish / pin / house; `union([('circle', x, y, r), ('line', points, width), ('poly', points), ('sub', primitive)], cx, cy, size)` builds your own shape from primitives and traces its outer outline automatically
- Geometry: `resample(P, n)` resamples evenly by arc length; `normalise(P)` clockwise from the top; `align(A, B)` matches winding and finds the best start with FFT cross-correlation; `chain([...])` aligns a whole series in turn; `ease(t, 'inout'|'sine'|'quad'|'in'|'out'|'back'|'expo'|'linear')`; `tween(A, B, s, at=, stretch=, direction=, spin=, scale=)` blends the outline about its centroid while the centroid travels an eased path, `stretch` is squash & stretch along the motion; `at(keys, T)` the whole keyframe chain at global time T (for real per-frame animation); `mix(c0, c1, t)` mixes colours in OKLab (blue to yellow never greys out)
- Background: `paper(colour)`; `panel(x0, y0, x1, y1, colour, ink=)` a flat colour panel, `ink` being the default colour for ghosts and lines drawn on that panel; `wipe(cx, cy, r, colour, ink, clip_box)` a frozen circular transition
- Shape layers: `fill(P, colour, alpha, clip=another outline, half=((x0, y0), (x1, y1)))`, `half` fills only the right of a line, for two-tone facets; `outline(P, colour, width, dash=(on, off))`; `stroke(points, colour, width)` a round-cap line (highlights, ripples, steam); `dot()`
- Stop-motion: `ghost(P, fill, fill_alpha, line_alpha, width)` onion-skin ghosts; a `None` line colour takes the panel's `ink`, switching automatically across panels; `points(P, every)` marks matched points; `links(A, B, every)` joins matched points; `path(points)` a smooth path through the centroids, `along(path, u)` a point and direction; `trail(path, dash=)` the motion trail; `chevron()` direction arrows; `streaks()` speed lines
- Timeline: `diamond(x, y, r, fill)` keyframe diamonds; `ease_curve(x0, y0, x1, y1, kind, ticks=frame times)` an ease curve with frame dots and dashed projections
- Text `text(s, x, y, size, colour, font='sans'|'display'|'mono'|'mono_bold'|'cjk'|'cjk_bold', anchor='l'|'m'|'r', spacing, alpha)`: y is the baseline, Latin and CJK switch fonts automatically, returns the width; `text_width()` measures width
- Coordinates are pixels, y down; angles are counter-clockwise positive

## Newspaper collage — `NewsCollage`
- Idea: everything is a paper `Sheet` that was "printed first, then torn or cut, then glued"; the old photos are not textures — they are rendered by `Photo` with height-field lighting, then screened into halftone
- Board: `board()` kraft paper (cloudy pulp, dark and light short fibres, bark flecks)
- Sheets: `sheet(w, h, colour)` returns a `Sheet` — `text()`, `block(..., minus=text mask)` reversed-out type, `ink()`, `rule()`; `tear('rtb', depth, rim)` hand-torn (a wandering tear line, a pale fibrous core, flying fibres), the other edges count as scissor cuts; `cut_rect()` a slightly crooked scissored quadrilateral; `cut(pts)`; `wrinkle()` paste wrinkles; `age()` yellowing and mildew
- Newspaper: `newsprint(w, h, columns, size, headline, picture, ad)` a whole fake page (pseudo word columns, masthead rules, a headline, a lede, subheads, column rules, boxed ads, halftone photos, show-through from the back), then `tear()`
- Old photos: build objects on `Photo(w, h)`: `disc()`, `dome(..., rot, cut)` (bells, spheres), `ring()` (bezel), `rod()`, `poly()` (hands), `gear(r, teeth, spokes, pinion)`, `spiral(r0, r1, turns, flare)` (mainspring, balance spring), `screw()`; `text()` / `lines()` lettering and scales printed on the surface; `glare()` a glass reflection. z is the base height, `height` the part's own thickness, albedo / metal / gloss its material
- Screening to halftone: `print_photo(photo, cell, angle, ink)` develops and prints as a halftone, returns a Sheet; `s.cutout(margin, centre)` scissors it out along the silhouette; `s.split(p0, p1)` one snip splits it in two
- Pasting: `paste(sheet, cx, cy, rot, lift)` returns a Frame — `f.pt(lx, ly)` converts local to canvas coordinates; `disc(cx, cy, r, colour)` a scissor-cut disc of coloured paper
- Lettering: `ransom('OPEN IT UP', x, y, size, faces=[...], styles=[...])` ransom-note letters, returns [(Frame, letter)]; `clipping(letter, size, face, paper, block, fg, extra)` one clipped letter, `extra` is 'text' (with neighbours) or 'tint' (halftone ground); `label(text, x, y, size, face, fg, bg, block)` a cut strip of printed type
- Else: `tape(cx, cy, length, rot)` masking tape; `stamp(cx, cy, [(text, size)], shape='box'|'round', ring=ring text)` a rubber stamp; `pen(pts, dash=(on, off), dot)` an inked leader line
- Fonts: news / news_bold / news_italic / serif_bold / slab / clarendon / didone / poster / futura / grotesk / typewriter(_bold) / cjk_song / cjk_hei / cjk_round / cjk_kai; missing glyphs fall back to a CJK hei face automatically
- Colour constants `INK BLACK RED MUSTARD KRAFT NEWS PAPER TAPE`; rot is counter-clockwise positive

## Aurora glassmorphism — `Aurora`
- Idea: a bright "screen of light" (a diffuse gradient) with a few real frosted-glass panes floating above it. What you see through the glass is a gaussian blur of the **actual** pixels beneath (backdrop, orbs, cards and text behind all count), veiled with milky white; the interface is printed on the glass. Earlier panes sit behind; a later card blurs an earlier one, text and all
- Backdrop: `backdrop(base, blobs=[(x, y, rx, ry, colour, strength)], ribbons=[(points, width, colour, strength)], warp, grain)` the mesh gradient: colour pools weighted and mixed in OKLab, coordinates warped into irregular shapes, ribbons along splines, fine grain built in; `sparkles(n)` scattered motes; `orb(cx, cy, r, (lit, mid, shade))` glossy balls: an iridescent ramp, a Fresnel rim picking up the backdrop, highlights, a coloured shadow on the backdrop
- Glass: `glass(x0, y0, x1, y1, radius, blur, tint, sat, shadow, elevation, bevel, rim, sheen)` returns a `Card` (x0 y0 x1 y1 w h cx cy r). `blur` is the frost (about 20–30); `tint` the milky veil (about 0.24, thicker toward the light); `sat` the saturation of what shows through (about 1.5); `bevel` the rim lens, refracting outward with a different offset per channel and very faint dispersion; `rim` the highlight stroke on the light-facing edge; the shadow falls only outside the card and takes its colour from the backdrop. `pill()` is a small glass capsule (buttons, reminders, highlighted rows — glass on glass), `cloud(cx, cy, w)` a glass cloud
- Shapes (signed-distance fields, 1 px analytic anti-aliasing): `rrect()`, `circle(ring=width)`, `line(points, smooth)`, all accepting `grad=ramp, angle=`; `divider()` an etched hairline
- Controls: `toggle(x, y, on)`, `slider(x0, x1, y, value)`, `knob()`, `ring(cx, cy, r, width, frac)` (clockwise from 12 o'clock, conic gradient, round caps, glow, an end knob), `check(cx, cy, r, 'done'|'now'|'todo')`, `bars(x0, y0, x1, y1, values, labels=, highlight=)`, `spark(x0, y0, x1, y1, values, dots=)` (returns the points), `sun()`, `app_icon(x, y, size, grad, glyph)`
- Text `text(s, x, y, size, fill, weight='light'|'regular'|'medium'|'semibold'|'bold', anchor, alpha, spacing, grad)`: Latin uses SF (optical size follows the font size), CJK uses PingFang, script runs split automatically; in `anchor`, the second letter is s = baseline, m = middle, t = top, b = bottom; `text_width()` measures width
- Colours: `INK` deep-indigo text; gradient ramps `WARM` (apricot → pink → violet), `COOL` (sky → periwinkle → violet), `MINT`; `ramp(t, stops)`, `mix()` interpolate in OKLab. One gradient means one thing
- `save('x.jpg')` defaults to quality 88, 4:4:4 chroma

## Bauhaus geometric — `Bauhaus`
- Idea: the picture is a screen-printed press sheet, one screen per colour, pulled light to dark; everything on one screen shares that screen's registration error (a translation plus a tiny rotation), so where colour meets black you see a sliver of paper or a dark overlap
- Paper: `paper(colour, margin, module)` cream uncoated stock (cloudy pulp, short fibres, flecks, tooth, a faint cockle); it sets the design area and the grid module; `at(col, row)` converts grid cells to pixels; `grid()` prints the construction grid from a pale grey screen
- Masks (full canvas, anti-aliased, can add and subtract): `circle()`, `ring()`, `sector(cx, cy, r, a0, a1)` half and quarter circles, `arc()`, `rect(x0, y0, x1, y1, rot)`, `bar(x0, y0, x1, y1, width, cap='butt'|'square'|'round')` heavy bars and thin lines, `poly()`, `triangle(x0, x1, base_y)` (equilateral by default)
- Printing: `pull(mask, ink, knock, edge, density, opacity)` pulls one screen: the ink film streaks along the squeegee and thins toward the stroke's end, thin film exposes tooth pits, dust in the screen leaves pinholes, the screen edge is mesh-ragged and gathers a little ink; inks are semi-opaque and deepen where they overprint. `knock` is a knock-out mask; `edge=0` keeps thin lines clean
- Text: `text(s, x, y, size, ink, font, anchor, spacing, rot, weight)` printed directly; `text_mask()` returns a mask for knock-outs; font is geo (Futura Bold), geo_book, geo_cond (condensed extra-bold), cjk (heavy Hei), cjk_book, switching per character; `text_width()` measures width
- Press marks: `marks(targets, crop, bar, inks, overprints)` register crosses, corner crop marks, a colour bar. Once registered, each screen prints its own copy on its first pull, so the crosses stack as offset multi-colour crosses
- After printing: `pencil(s, x, y, size)` a pencil edition number in the margin, graphite catching only the tooth peaks
- Colour constants `YELLOW RED BLUE BLACK`, paper `PAPER`, grid `TINT`; angles are counter-clockwise positive with 0° at 3 o'clock; `save('x.jpg')` defaults to quality 88, 4:4:4 chroma

## Retro synthwave — `Synthwave`
- Idea:
  - a horizontal pinhole camera looks at an infinite plane (X right, Y up, Z forward, metres); the colour buffer is painted far to near;
  - emissive things are also written into an emission buffer; later opaque objects block the light behind them;
  - finally a four-radius bloom, over-exposed channels spilling to white, and the finished frame "played off a tape"
- Construction: `Synthwave(W, H, seed, horizon=610, vp_x=1060, focal=1100, eye=3.5, record=True)`; camera helpers `project(X, Y, Z)`, `ground_row(Z)`
- Sky: `sky(stops, ground)` a dusk gradient, with the unlit ground laid below the horizon first; `stars(n, bright)` sparser near the horizon, the brightest with four-point spikes; `streaks([(y, x0, x1, thickness)])` thin streak clouds lit along their bottom edge
- `sun(x, y, r, bands=8, band_top, gaps=(0.2, 0.7), level, gain)`: a yellow → orange → pink gradient sun with slits that thicken downward — real gaps, clipped at the horizon
- Distance:
  - `mountains(x0, x1, z0, z1, height, cell, taper, edge=(MAGENTA, CYAN))`: a jittered grid height field in world space, dark flat-shaded triangles, edge lines grading from magenta at the foot to cyan at the ridge;
  - `skyline(X0, X1, Z, heights, widths, tall=(X, height), windows)` a distant little city, window lights, red aircraft lights
- Ground:
  - `floor(colour, spacing, width, fog, scroll, x_range)`: the neon grid, analytic line width per pixel, fading to its average in the distance against moiré, with pink ground fog and a sun light band;
  - `sea(coast, bar, spread, sky, glitter, stretch)`: the sea on the X < coast side, its reflection torn into bars;
  - `road(x0, x1, dash)`: a glossy road with neon edges and a centre dash;
  - `neon_line(X, colour, width, dash)`: any vertical neon line on the ground (the coastline)
- Objects:
  - `palm(X, Z, height, lean, fronds)`: palm silhouettes — ringed trunks, fronds at varied elevation angles, drooping leaflets, coconuts, a sunlit rim;
  - `car(X, Z)`: a sports car from behind, its whole tail-light bar and the red reflection on the road
- Lettering:
  - `chrome_text(s, x, y, size, depth, glints=(letter indices...))`: chrome display letters, a dark outline, stepped extrusion, a lit pale edge, sky mirrored above and ground below with one sharp horizon, lit bevels on the upper left and four-point glints;
  - `glint(x, y, length)`: a single four-point star glint;
  - `neon_script(s, x, y, size, colour, rot)`: neon script;
  - `caption(s, x, y, size, colour, rules)` letter-spaced small type with rules on both sides
- Tape:
  - `osd(s, x, y, px, anchor)`: a 5×7 block-letter VCR on-screen display (PLAY ▶, the date);
  - `vhs(bleed, shift_px, jitter, noise, scan, tracking, head)`: applies to the finished frame only — YIQ chroma blur shifted right, ghost ringing, per-line jitter, a tracking noise band, head-switching noise at the bottom, white dropout lines, scanlines, a lifted black level; stage snapshots stay clean
- Colour constants `MAGENTA CYAN VIOLET ORANGE YELLOW PINK RED WHITE`; fonts come from `synth_font('chrome'|'script'|'caption', size)` looking for system fonts (Avenir Next Heavy Italic / Brush Script / a heavy hei face), overridable with `INKPAINT_FONT_CHROME` etc.; `save('x.jpg')` defaults to quality 88, 4:4:4 chroma

## Perler beads — `Perler`
- Idea: the whole table is a height map and every thing is a solid (beads are short tubes with holes, the pegboard carries pegs, paper has thickness); the camera leans back a little: every screen pixel walks its own column to the nearest surface, so each bead shows a sliver of side wall, the gaps show the backs of the next row, and inside each hole you see the inner wall and the peg. One big light box upper-left: local point lights, soft shadows marched through the height map, sky AO; the light box is visible in specular reflections (crisp arcs on glossy rims, a waxy sheen once melted); shading in linear space, `ss`× supersampling
- Construction: `Perler(W, H, seed, pitch=22, tilt=0.32, ss=2, light=(x, y, z))`; `pitch` is the bead spacing (px), `tilt` the camera lean
- Table and paper: `desk(colour, kind='wood'|'mat')`; `chart(rows, key, cx, cy, cell, angle, title, subtitle, number, names, done)` the printed chart (grid, symbols, row and column numbers, a heavy line every 5 cells, a legend with counts), whose `.done.update('RD')` ticks placed colours off with pencil; `tape(text, cx, cy, angle, size, sub, badge='1')` a masking-tape label; `sheet(draw, cx, cy, angle, thick, translucency, drape, cut, curl, bump)` any thin sheet
- Pegboard and beads: `pegboard(cx, cy, cols, rows, shape='square'|'circle'|'hexagon', colour='clear'|'white'|colour, angle)` returns a `Board` (pegs on grid points, `xy(row, col)` gives a peg's position); `place(board, rows, key, at=(row, col), only='RDP', keep=lambda r, c: ...)` inserts beads from a character chart, batched by colour or region; `key` values use `BEADS` colour names (including `pearl`, `clear`, `glow`) or '#rrggbb'
- Ironing: `ironing_paper(cx, cy, w, h, angle, cut=((x, y), (nx, ny)), curl, wrinkle)` the translucent ironing paper laid over the beads, `cut` half-peeled (curling), `wrinkle` heat wrinkles; `iron(board, melt, where)`: 0.4 half-melted, 0.7 finished, 0.8 fully flat; `lift_off(board)` takes the finished piece off the board
- Props: `tray(cx, cy, [[colour, ...], ...], cell, angle, fill)` sorting trays (a heap of beads per cell); `spill(cx, cy, colours, n, spread, standing)` spilled beads; `bead(x, y, colour, pose='stand'|'side', angle, tilt)`; `tweezers(tip, angle, length, lift, rise, holding='sky', grip)` tweezers held in the air, a bead in their tip, with their own shadow
- Layering and animation: `on_top(*objs)` lifts objects to the top, `remove(obj)` takes them away; `stage(name)` only stores a snapshot, and `save(path, stages_dir)` renders the stages at 1×
- Colour constant `BEADS`; angles are counter-clockwise positive; `save('x.jpg')` defaults to quality 88, 4:4:4 chroma

## Petroglyph — `Petroglyph`
- Idea: a petroglyph is not painted on; the rock's dark skin (desert varnish) is hammered off. Everything is height (px) and colour fields, lit at the end by one low sun upper-left: normal shading, shadows marched along the light (each pit's upper-left wall dark, lower-right bright), occlusion in the hollows
- Construction: `Petroglyph(W, H, seed, light=(-0.62, -0.5, 0.6))`
- Rock:
  - `rock(colour, relief, bedding, grain)`: a sandstone height field (broad swells, intermittent bedding, sand grain, shallow pits) and the stone colour under the varnish;
  - `ledge(y, drop=34)`: a kinked rock ledge, the face below it stepped back and its varnish thinner;
  - `varnish(strength, streaks, top, scour, patches)`: desert varnish — a near-uniform dark film + crisp blue-black runoff streaks + pale streaks, thicker on top, scoured thinner near the ground;
  - `fracture(pts, width, depth, seep, branches)`: cracks — zigzag narrow slots, rounded shoulders, branches, dark varnish seeping down
- Drafting: `draft()` returns a `Draft` (`stroke(pts, w, end_w)`, `fill(pts)`, `dot`, `ring`, `add(mask)`); the stock glyphs all return a Draft and can be merged with `into=`:
  - `bighorn(x, y, size, facing, kind='ram'|'ewe'|'lamb', gait, rot)` a bighorn sheep (twisted-perspective horns);
  - `hunter(x, y, size, facing, weapon='bow'|'atlatl')` a hunter drawing a bow / raising an atlatl;
  - `sun_spiral(x, y, r, turns, rays)`, `crescent(x, y, r, thick, rot)`, `concentric(x, y, r, rings)` the waterhole;
  - `hand(x, y, size, rot, outline, spread)`, `hoofprints(pts, size, every)` cloven prints, `meander(pts, width, smooth)` any line
- Pecking and abrading:
  - `peck(glyph, density, size, depth, age, scatter)`: hundreds to thousands of irregular pits hammer the glyph out, breaking the varnish and crushing the grains pale;
  - `abrade(pts, width, depth, age)`: a ground V-groove (smoother and shinier than pecking, with along-the-line scratches);
  - `abrade_tally(x, y, counts, gap, length, width, row_gap, slant, age, strike_every)` rows of day-count tallies
- Age: `age` 0 freshly pecked (bright, crisp), 0.3 a few centuries, 0.6–0.8 almost re-covered by varnish; newer work pecked over older reads naturally brighter and deeper
- Weathering: `spall(pts, depth, impact, age)` spalls (small shell-arc lips, stepped shadow, conchoidal ripples, fresh orange stone — carrying away what was on them); `lichen(x, y, r, kind='green'|'orange'|'grey', n)` crustose lichen
- Order: rock → ledge → varnish → fracture → old glyphs (high age) → new glyphs → spall → lichen
- Colour constants `STONE SCAR CRUSH VARNISH_FE VARNISH_MN LICHEN`; `stage(name)` composites a snapshot immediately; `save('x.jpg')` defaults to quality 88, 4:4:4 chroma

## Egyptian tomb painting — `TombPainting`
- Idea: first "declare" everything on the wall, then paint it over and over in the painters' own order: `wall()` straw-tempered mud under gypsum plaster → `grid(box, cell, base)` a red-ochre snapped proportion grid (one cell is one proportion unit of the figure, 18 cells from sole to hairline), `guide(p0, p1)` register lines → `sketch()` a red underdrawing → `ground(colour)` the ground wash brushed round the figures → `colour([pigments...])` one pigment at a time, `ink()` outlines last → `colour(layer='text')`, `ink(layer='text')` the writing → `age()`. Every layer stays in memory and re-appears layer by layer when it flakes
- Parts: `part(pts or [polygons...], pigment, paths=, ink=, width=, z=, alpha=, clip=, layer=)`; pigment names in `PIGMENTS` (skin the men's red ochre, skin_f the women's yellow ochre, linen, white, yellow orpiment, blue Egyptian blue, lblue, green, dkgreen, lgreen, red, brown, ochre, black); laid over a per-pixel z map, so painting in batches by pigment never stacks wrong; outline hidden under a later layer is automatically not drawn
- Figures: `figure(x, ground line y, height, pose, facing, sex='m'|'f', wig='short'|'long'|'woman'|'cap', dress='kilt'|'long'|'loin'|'dress', collar)`; `POSES` holds stand / walk / staff / plough / reap / sow / hoe / glean / carry / hold / scoop / squat, any keyword overridable (hip, lean, knees kf/kb, ankles af/ab, wrists wf/wb, elbow bends bf/bb, hand shapes hf/hb, `turn`, mirrored feet `mb`); returns the canvas coordinates and z of hands, wrists, shoulders, ears (`hand_f`, `z_hand_b`...), `S((x, y))` converts proportion units to canvas coordinates for hanging props
- Props: `staff()`, `sickle()`, `hoe(fig)`, `rod()`, `basket(full=)`, `sack(lean=)`, `heap()`, `measure(tilt=)`, `grain_stream()`, `scoop()`, `papyrus()`, `palette()`; things held in the hand take z `fig['z_hand_x'] - 0.01` (passing behind the fist)
- Animals and plants: `ox(x, ground line, length, facing, colour, patches)` a long-horned ox (the far-side legs offset a step, all four legs visible), returns `poll` (where the yoke sits); `wheat(x0, x1, ground line, top, stubble=[(xa, xb)], grab=[(fist x, fist y, (xa, xb))])` the field, stubble and grabbed stalks; `sheaf()`, `tree(waterskin=(dx, length))`, `papyrus_clump()`
- Borders: `block_border()` a block border, `kheker()` the kheker frieze, `band(x0, x1, y, [(pigment, height), ('rule', 2), ...])` register bands, `river()` the Nile
- Text: `column(x0, y0, x1, y1, facing, n, seed)` a column of pseudo-hieroglyphs laid out in quadrats (tall narrow ones side by side, flat ones stacked); `glyph(name, x, y, size, facing)` one sign (23 in `SIGNS`, plus hoe, stroke, heel, coil); `numerals(12, x, y, size)` hieroglyphic numbers
- Age: `age(losses=[(x, y, rx, ry)], zones=[(x, y, r)], cracks, flake, soot, fade, salt, tide, wear_amt)`: clustered flaking (blue and crack edges first), flaking down to the plaster where the red grid and sketch return, worn pigment, plaster lost to the mud (a broken pale rim, steps, shadows), cracks, Egyptian blue greying to green, soot at the top, a damp tide line and salt at the wall foot, fly specks, a lamp vignette
- `stage(name)`, `save(path, stages_dir)`; `save('x.jpg')` defaults to quality 88, 4:4:4 chroma

## Roman mosaic — `Mosaic`
- Idea: the craftsman first paints an invisible "cartoon" (a flat-and-gradient colour field), then lays stones one by one: each stone takes the average colour of the cartoon under its foot and picks the closest box in `STONES` (hue compared before lightness, with a little jitter — at colour-step boundaries it looks like the craftsman mixing two stones). The laying direction (andamento) is the drawing: every laying mode runs one row along an iso-line of some scalar field (a distance field or a coordinate) via marching squares, cut into a whole number of stones, so each is slightly longer or shorter; on curves each stone becomes a little wedge automatically, and at sharp corners the row breaks and a new stone starts; stones near a figure's spine stop on the distance field's ridge, the two sides meeting at the centre line. A later stone mostly covered by an earlier one is rejected; slightly overlapped, it is "cut" and leaves a grout gap (earlier stones win). Rendering is an `ss`× supersampled height field: slightly domed polished tops with worn edges (each stone's own signed distance does the bevel), recessed sandy grout, the mortar bed lower again; one oblique light upper-left, diffuse + specular by material, each stone slightly tilted so equal stones catch the light differently; stones cast shadows into the joints, the joints have AO
- Construction: `Mosaic(W, H, seed, tile=13, gap=1.8, ss=2, light, bed, grout, stones=None)`; `tile` is the background stone size, `gap` the grout width (px); figures use smaller stones (size about 6–8)
- Cartoon and drafting: `paint(mask, colour)`; `shade(mask, c0, c1, p0, p1, gamma)` a linear gradient (dark back, pale belly); `sinopia(mask=... or pts=...)` a red-ochre sketch on the wet bed, visible wherever nothing covers it (the animation's first frame)
- Masks: `poly_mask(pts)`, `ellipse(cx, cy, rx, ry, rot)`, `rect()`, `band(pts, w0, w1)` a tapering heavy line (tentacles, stern posts, wave crests); `everything()` what is laid, `free(region)` what is still open
- Laying (in call order, earlier wins — lay what must stay whole first):
  - `point(x, y, colour, size, 'round'|'square'|'tri', angle)`: one stone (an eye, a word-divider dot);
  - `line(pts, colour=None, size, across)`: a row along a path (letter strokes, fin lines, a mast, rigging, net lines); with `colour=None` it takes the cartoon's colour — a tentacle's mid-ridge;
  - `outline(mask, 'black', rows, size)`: the first dark contour rows inside a figure;
  - `fill(mask, size, stones, wrap=True)`: rows inward from the outline (opus vermiculatum); `wrap` also rings already-laid eyes and gill lines; `figure()` = outline + fill;
  - `halo(region, rows, size, stones, around)`: one or two rings hugging a figure's outer contour in the background;
  - `rows(region, size, angle, wobble, field, stones)`: background tiling (opus tessellatum); with `field` (H×W) it follows that field's iso-lines — the sea uses a wavy field;
  - `frame(x0, y0, x1, y1, width, size, stones)`: border rows parallel to a rectangle's four sides, mitred corners; make the width a whole multiple of the row pitch and black-and-white edging aligns with the rows;
  - `tuck(region, size, small)`: the remaining holes filled with cut-down stones, big holes first
- Inscriptions: `letters(text, x, y, cap height, colour)` Roman capitals (common letters A–Y, U written as V, no J K W Z), one row of stones per stroke, `·` a triangular word-divider; `tabula(x0, y0, x1, y1, [(text, cap height, colour)], ear, frame=('black', 'red'))` a tabula ansata with dovetail ears: the text, black frame, red frame, white ground laid horizontally, red ears
- Age (done last): `lacuna(mask)` a patch of stones lost, showing the old mortar bed and the square sockets; `crack(pts)` a settling crack (a thin dark line crossing stones and joints, with a depression); `patina()` grout dirt in patches and local loss of sheen
- Stones: `STONES` name → (colour, material), material stone (limestone — matte, mottled, pitted) / marble (veined, faintly glossy) / glass (smalti — saturated, bright, with bubbles) / terracotta; `stones=[...]` limits what this step may use (the sky only white / bone / cream, the sea only blue glass)
- Helpers: `edt(mask)` a jump-flood Euclidean distance field, `iso_lines(f, step)` all iso-lines at once, `maxfilt()`; `STONES MATERIALS LETTERS SINOPIA`
- Animation: `stage(name)` only records progress, `save(path, stages_dir)` renders the stages and the final frame at `stage_ss` (default 1×); `save('x.jpg')` defaults to quality 88, 4:4:4 chroma

## Stained glass — `StainedGlass`
- Idea: the glazier's process — the cartoon is cut into pieces → glass is chosen and cut → grisaille trace, stipple and silver stain → lead came soldered → saddle bars → daylight seen from inside. All colour is in the glass; there is only one paint, a brown-black; lead lands on every boundary between two pieces
- Construction: `StainedGlass(W, H, seed, record=True, ambient=0.05)`; `stage(name, cartoon=False)` (`cartoon=True` gives the cutline drawing on paper); `save(path, stages_dir)`
- Stone wall: `wall(colour, mortar, course, block)` an ashlar wall (each block a different shade, angled tooling, worn arrises, recessed mortar); `lancet(cx, top, bottom, width, arch=1, splay, sill, hood)` opens a pointed-arch light and returns a `Light`, with splayed reveals (radiant wedge joints in the arch), a splayed sill and a hood mould; `string_course(y, h)` a string course
- `Light`: `pt(lx, ly)` / `P(points)` converts local coordinates (origin at the arch tip, y down) to canvas coordinates; `inner(b)` the window shape inset by b; `band(b0, b1)` a ring along the window edge (borders, pale strips); `outline(b, step)` points along the edge (for border pieces); `sdf()`; `half(ly, b)` the half-width at a height
- Glass: `glass(mask, colour or colour list, cell, streak, seeds, thick, vary, flashed, aspect, angle, around, spokes, sites, streak_dir)`. The colour is how it looks with light through it; with `cell` it is cut into a Voronoi mosaic, each piece taking one colour from the list at its own shade, thickness and streak; `sites` cuts at given points (borders, strips); `aspect` / `angle` stretch the pieces; `around` cuts radiating about a point; `flashed` is the strong banding of flashed red. Later glass is cut out of earlier glass; slivers too small or too narrow merge into same-colour neighbours automatically. `cut(points)` adds one cutline
- Masks: `circle()`, `ellipse()`, `poly()`, `blob()`, `branch(points, start width, end width)` a tapering branch
- Painting:
  - `trace(points, width, taper, opacity, clip)` opaque trace lines (features, veins, feathers, twigs), `clip` confines them to a mask;
  - `matt(mask, strength, soft, stipple)` a translucent matt wash with stipple grain; `shade(mask, offset, strength, clip)` a crescent matt on the side away from the light;
  - `scratch(points, width)` / `scratch_mask(mask)` a wooden stick scratches the paint off and lets the light through;
  - `diaper(mask, spacing, motif='cross'|'dot'|'ring'|'flake'|'quatrefoil', jitter)` ground patterns, scratched out of a thin matt by default — with `jitter` scattered it becomes snow;
  - `stain(mask, strength)` silver stain: white glass to lemon yellow and amber, blue glass to green;
  - `inscribe(text, x, y, size, font='lombardic', scratched)` lettering (Luminari / Herculanum, overridable with `INKPAINT_FONT_LOMBARDIC`)
- Leading and iron: `lead(width=6, rim=10, solder=True)` leads every boundary (lit as an H-section, solder blobs brighter and rounder, a wide rim came round the window edge); `crack(points, mend=True)` cracked pieces and a mending strap lead, before `lead()`; `saddle_bar(light, y, width)` a horizontal bar (y in canvas coordinates) wired to the leads at both ends, after `lead()`
- Light: `weather(grime, pits)` grime on the leads and lower glass, corrosion pits on some panes; `daylight(strength, spill, halation, sky)` turns on the interior view: a coloured spill over the wall, the reveals glowing near the glass, an upside-down blurred projection on the sill, the halo of bright glass eating a little of the lead (blue most visibly). Before `daylight()` the wall uses fixed neutral light, so the stone wall does not change between stage snapshots
- Colour constants `RUBY BLUE DEEP_BLUE SKY GREEN LEAF SPRING OLIVE GOLD AMBER ORANGE MURREY WHITE PALE BROWN FLESH`; `save('x.jpg')` defaults to quality 88, 4:4:4 chroma

## Illuminated manuscript — `Illuminated`
- Idea:
  - paint in workshop order: ruling → copying (rubrics and initials left blank) → rubrication → pictures → gilding → colour → pen work;
  - the letters are written with a simulated broad-edged quill: the nib is a short segment held at about 40°, a stroke is the area it sweeps, so weight follows direction; the diamond heads and hairline joins come from the pen, not a font;
  - burnished gold is a mirror on a raised gesso cushion: every pixel reflects the camera ray along its own normal (the cushion's dome, punchwork, gold seams, polishing marks) to look up a room with a big window upper-left;
  - shell gold is matte granules;
  - gold first, colour after — later paint and ink cover the gold.
- Book and skin:
  - `desk()` a walnut desk;
  - `codex(x0, y0, x1, y1, cover, squares, stack, dip)` an open book (leather boards, a page-block book edge, pages dipping darker at the spine), returns the two page rectangles;
  - `flaw(cx, cy, rx, ry, rot)` a natural hole in the vellum;
  - `rule(x0, x1, ys, prick_x)` prickings, ruling lines at x-height, border verticals running through;
  - `show_through(x0, x1, ys, xh, opacity)` the writing on the back showing through.
- Text:
  - `column(text, x0, x1, y, xh, leading, indent=[(rows, left x)], align, filler)` sets a column, returns a Block:
    - markers `*rubric*` (left for the rubricator), `^word` (an initial dotted red), `¶` a paragraph mark, `|` the end of a paragraph;
    - automatic long s / round s, hyphenation at line breaks, line-end fillers;
    - `7` is written as the Tironian et (⁊), `ꝑ` is per.
  - `scribe(blk, dip)` copies the black text: the quill re-dips every so often, iron-gall ink going from black to brown, ink pooling at stroke edges;
  - `rubricate(blk, blue_first)` vermilion rubrics, red-dotted initials, red and blue alternating paragraph marks, a red sawtooth with a blue dot at line ends;
  - `write(s, x, y, xh, kind, rot, load)` writes one line, returns the line's end x;
  - `arc_text(s, cx, cy, r, at, xh, kind)` writes along an arc;
  - `text_width()` measures width;
  - kind is 'iron' / 'red' / 'blue' / 'white' / 'diagram'.
- Gold and pigment:
  - `gild(mask, cushion)` burnished gold on a raised red bole;
  - `punch(pts, r, depth)` punchwork;
  - `shell_gold(mask)` shell gold;
  - `paint(mask, colour, mix=(colour2, 0..1 field), granular)` tempera with pooled edges, granular azurite;
  - `wash(mask, colour, opacity)` a transparent tint;
  - `pen(paths, width, kind, dash)` round-pen lines;
  - `outline(mask, width)` an ink edge around a shape.
- Masks: `mask(polys, lines, dots, holes)`, `circle()`, `ring()`, `ellipse()`, `star()`.
- Ornament objects (call each one's `.draw()`, `.gild()`, `.paint()`, `.pen()` in workshop order):
  - `initial('Q'|'O', x0, y0, size, body)` a historiated initial: a Lombardic letter with white-line tracery, punchworked gold ground, a night sky in the counter;
  - `border(path, width, avoid, bounds, seed, every, reach)` a gold / ultramarine / rose bar border growing ivy sprays recursively (gold leaves, flowers, gold beads, tendrils), avoiding the `avoid` rectangles;
  - `versal('O', x, y, size, colour, flourish, reach)` a two-line-high split Lombardic letter with a red flourish;
  - `astrolabe(cx, cy, R, lat, rete, rule, star)` an astrolabe; `.pen(labels=[(text, (x, y), (pointed-to x, y))])` adds red annotations;
  - `spheres(cx, cy, R, angles, label_at)` the roundel of the spheres.
- Finish and output:
  - `age(foxing, soil, flakes, halo)` six hundred years: iron-gall halos, foxing, page-turning thumb soil, gold flaked to the red bole, azurite losses;
  - `stage(name)`, `save(path, stages_dir)`.
- Colour constants `VELLUM INK RED AZURE ROSE GREEN WHITE SILVER LEAD OCHRE BOLE`; angles counter-clockwise positive; `save('x.jpg')` defaults to quality 88, 4:4:4 chroma.

## Leonardo codex — `Codex`
- Paper: `sheet(tone, mount, margin, laid, chain, lost_corner='br', fold=x, stains=[(x, y, r)], foxing, edges, age)`: laid rag paper on a mount (laid lines, chain lines, fibres, tooth), with browning ragged edges, a lost corner, yellowed clouds, crisp foxing, a water stain with a tide line, show-through from the verso, optional old folds, handling soil where it was turned; `show_through(x, y, width, lines)` the writing on the back showing through, mirrored left-right (so it reads the right way round)
- Iron-gall ink and quill: `quill(pts, width, ink)` / `quills([...])`, width following the broad nib's direction, heavy landing and pointed lift-off; the pen's ink runs out: it pales as it goes, re-dipping darkens again, occasionally pooling a bead at the landing; `contour(pts, searching=2)` lightly searches two or three times before committing (reworked lines); `ruled(p0, p1)` a ruled line; `dotted(pts, dot_dash=True)` dashed / dot-dash lines (axes); `arrow(pts)`
- Hatching: `hatch(mask, shade, angle=55, spacing, levels=(...), cross=0.76, cross_angle=-38, length, origin, medium='ink'|'chalk')`. 55° is the left-hander's '\\'; shade 0 light to 1 dark, each extra level interleaves between the previous two, past `cross` a '/' crossing layer is added; laid band by band. Tone fields: `between(a0, a1, b0, b1)` (0→1 from one line to another — membranes, round shafts), `radial_shade(cx, cy, r)`; masks: `poly_mask(pts)`, `disc_mask(cx, cy, r)`, `Codex.arc(cx, cy, r, a0, a1)` takes points on an arc
- Red chalk and pencil: `sanguine(pts, width, pressure)` / `sanguines([...])` catching only the tooth, `rub(mask, amount)` rubbed open with a finger; `leadpoint(pts)` a pale grey pencil underdrawing
- Stylus and compass: `incise(pts)` colourless incisions (one side lit, one dark in raking light), `compass(cx, cy, r, a0, a1)` scores an arc and leaves a pinhole at the centre, `prick(x, y)`
- Stock subjects: `cane(p0, p1, w0, w1, bend, lashings)` a cane or wooden spar drawn as two lines with lashings, returns the outline to knock hatching out of; `feather(base, angle, length, width, medium, side, overlapped)` a feather (returns outline, rachis, vane edge); `bird(x, y, size, phase)` a small flying bird from above, phase from π/2 (wings highest) to −π/2 (lowest)
- Mirror writing: `write(text or lines, x, y, width, size, mirror=True, max_lines)`, with `mirror` x is the right margin, each line written right to left with a ragged left; `prose(n)` generates Italian pseudo-text; `label('a', x, y)` letter labels (`mirror=False` writes them normally, e.g. a collector's folio number)
- Accidents: `blot(x, y, r)` an ink blot with a dark tide line and small splashes, `spatter(x, y, n)`, `strike(x0, y0, x1, y1)` a struck-out idea
- Machine parts (pen rendering): `Camera(cx, cy, scale, azim, elev)` an orthographic camera; parts `gear(r, teeth, depth, h, hub)`, `lantern(r, h, staves)` a lantern pinion, `crown(r, h, teeth)` a crown wheel, `crank(arm, r_axle, handle_len, handle_r)`, `pulley(r, h)`, `cylinder()`, `box()`, `extrude()`; `.place(origin, axis, spin)` to place, `.named('wheel')` to name a surface group, `merge(...)` to merge into one mesh; `solid(mesh, cam, hatch=dict(...), hatch_groups={'wheel.face': dict(...) or None})`: a z-buffer drops hidden lines, ink behind is erased first, hatching follows how far each face turns from the light, only silhouettes / creases / opening edges are inked and joined into long strokes; an exploded view just pulls the parts apart along their axes and adds dot-dash lines
- Colour constants `INK_DARK INK_LIGHT SANGUINE LEADPOINT PAPER MOUNT`; fonts found automatically: Apple Chancery / URW Chancery (Linux) / Segoe Script (Windows), overridable with `INKPAINT_FONT_CHANCERY`; `save('x.jpg')` defaults to quality 88, 4:4:4 chroma

## Silhouette — `Silhouette`
- Idea: the silhouette is black paper cut with small scissors, pasted on an ivory card, touched with a few strokes of thin shell gold, and hung on the wall in an oval gilt frame behind convex glass. The wallpaper, gilt frame and glass are built from real materials; one window upper-left lights the whole wall
- Wall: `wallpaper(ground, ink, tint, period, repeat, length)` a block-printed stripe wallpaper (brushed distemper ground, two print plates, misregistration, dry specks, pin-registration dots, each sheet's pattern slightly mis-aligned, sheet joins); `picture_rail(y, h)` the picture rail; `chair_rail(y, h, dado)` the chair rail and the dado below
- Frame: `frame(cx, cy, rx, ry, width, pearls, twist, eglomise, card, hook, cord, spread)` returns a `Frame`. A height map is built from a moulding profile (a sight-edge bead, a cove, a ring of pearls, a large torus, a flat fillet, an outer round; `twist` makes the torus a rope twist); the gilt surface mirrors the room (window bright, room dim, floor black), so bands of light and dark ring the frame; there are gold-foil square seams, the crests worn to the red bole, grime in the hollows; a contact shadow and a soft shadow on the wall. `hook=True` hangs two silk cords from a brass hook on the picture rail (a V, running in behind the frame); the card's edge yellows with mildew; `eglomise` is a gold coil on a black ground on the back of the glass
- Cut paper: `sheet(f, at, scale, flip, warp)` returns a `Sheet`, coordinates in design units (px = at + scale × point); `warp` is a function that can rescale the whole body (a bigger head, a shorter torso):
  - glued flat: `shape(pts)` the main silhouette (with the barest shadow, its edge resampled into small snip facets), `disc(x, y, r)`; `hole(pts)` cut away, the card showing through (an eye, between the legs, a bow's gap)
  - cut separately, slightly lifted, a longer shadow: `snip(pts, w0, w1)` tapering strips (lashes, whiskers, hair strands, ribbons — width may be an array); `line(pts, w)` even strips (cables, masts); `fringe(pts, n, length, angle, width, side, bend)` a row of fine strands along a curve (plume barbs, chest fur); `scallop(pts, r, outset)` a row of bulging curls (ringlets, a gathered crown, furled sails); `ringlet(x, y, length, width, turns, sway)` a spiral curl, returns its centreline for the shell gold; `lace(pts, depth, step)` lace with a row of piercings
- `paste(sheet)` pastes the cut paper to the card: 4× supersampled then downsampled, so 1 px strands survive; the black paper carries fibres and sheen, cut edges toward the light carrying a thin bright line
- `bronze(sheet, strokes, width, tip, bright)` shell gold: each stroke tapers, each with its own brightness, granular, staying only on the black paper
- Lettering: `caption(f, text, x, y, size, style='roundhand'|'italic')` an iron-gall hand-written caption (`sil_font` finds Snell Roundhand / Baskerville Italic, overridable with `INKPAINT_FONT_ROUNDHAND`, `INKPAINT_FONT_ITALIC`); `pen(f, pts)` ink lines
- `glaze()` fits the glass: convex glass softness and a window reflection; `smooth(pts, per, closed)` an open or closed Catmull-Rom curve
- `stage(name)`, `save(path, stages_dir)`; `save('x.jpg')` defaults to quality 88, 4:4:4 chroma

## Letterpress poster — `Letterpress`
- Idea: one forme per ink; the things to print in that colour are "locked" into the forme and `press(ink)` prints one pass. Every pass lands differently: each forme carries its own translation and tiny rotation (`register(ink, dx, dy, rot)`), so the red and black never quite register. Red first, then black
- Paper: `paper(colour, warm, tone)`: cheap poster stock already yellowed (cloudy pulp, short fibres, flecks, tooth, a faint cockle)
- Formes: `lock(mask, ink, film)` locks in any mask; `clear(mask, ink, keep)` cuts away (woodcut highlights, occlusion); `with lp.layer('key'):` locks things into part of a forme and `press(ink, only=['key'])` prints just that part (the stage GIF shows outline first, then hatching); `press(ink, density)`: edges ragged with the tooth, a darker rim of pooled ink, large solids starving and showing tooth dots, dust leaving round misses; black over red goes deeper
- Wood type: `type_line(s, x0, x1, base, cap, face, ink, align='justify', stretch, track, shade=(dx, dy, other forme colour, gap), inline=(inset, width), wear, grain, limits)`: by default a line is justified to the measure (letters squeezed or stretched, plus tracking); each letter is a block of wood: grain streaks, one end printing light, dents and soft spots, nicked corners, the odd along-the-grain split; `shade` is a drop shade printed on the other forme, `inline` a white line scored through the stems. face is clarendon / antique / gothic / fatface / cjk (Song and Hei); `type_width()` measures width
- Metal type and ornaments: `text(s, x, y, size, face='roman'|'roman_bold'|'italic'|..., ink, anchor, spacing, rot, stretch)`, `text_mask()`, `text_width()`; `rule(x0, x1, y, kind='single'|'double'|'thick_thin'|'thin_thick'|'dotted', ink, weight, joints)` brass rules (long rules made of pieces, joints showing hairline gaps and a 1 px offset); `leaders()` leader dots; `star()`; `ornament(character, ...)` a dingbat face (a star is drawn when the font is missing); `border()` a thick-thin double frame with square corners
- Shapes (full-page float masks, can add, subtract, multiply): `shape(control points)` a smooth closed curve, `poly()` straight-edged polygons, `tube(points, widths=[...])` a variable-width tube (legs, noses, tails, ropes), `ellipse(cx, cy, rx, ry, rot, a0, a1)`, `rect()`, `outline_pts()`
- Woodcut: `key(mask, width, heavy)` contour lines cut just inside a shape, heavier on the side away from the light; `hatch(mask, angle or centre+aspect, spacing, tone, lo, gain, wobble)` hatching whose line width follows tone, tapering out below `lo` and merging into solids above; `form_tone(mask, soft)` lights the mask as a rounded volume to get the tone; `ramp(p0, p1)` a linear tone ramp; `cast(mask, dx, dy, onto)` cast shadows; `line(pts, width, taper)` a double-tapered cut line (wrinkles, ropes); `solid()`; `stipple(mask, density, size, tone)` stipple; `tint(mask, ink=RED, loose, grow)` a separately cut spot block, its edge wandering and a little fatter than the outline
- After printing: `fold('v'|'h', pos, depth, crack)` creases (ink cracked, grime settled, the two sides lit differently); `tone_edges()` yellowed edges; `foxing(n)` foxing; `stain()` a water stain; `tack(x, y, r)` tack holes and rust; `nicks(n, size)` edge nicks; `strip(x0, y0, x1, y1, rot, colour, draw=fn)` a separately printed date strip pasted on (fn receives a little Letterpress to set type on, paste marks around it)
- Colour constants `RED BLACK PAPER`; `save('x.jpg')` defaults to quality 88, 4:4:4 chroma

## Soviet constructivism — `Constructivism`
- Idea: one sheet, two plates. Everything red is on the red plate, everything black (solids, type, every halftone dot of every photo) on the black plate. To draw is to "print a mask onto a plate" or "knock out of plates, exposing paper". At composite time each plate carries a registration error as a whole (translation plus tiny rotation), so red and black meet with paper showing on one side and a dark overlap on the other. Inks are transparent filters that multiply; black over red is a warm black
- Construction: `Constructivism(W, H, seed, misreg=2.4, red=RED, black=BLACK)`
- Paper: `paper(colour, aged, yellow)`: cheap cream poster stock (cloudy pulp, fibres, flecks, a faint cockle, yellowed edges)
- Masks (full canvas, anti-aliased, can add and subtract): `circle()`, `ring()`, `arc(cx, cy, r, a0, a1, width)`, `sector()`, `rect(x0, y0, x1, y1, rot)`, `bar(x0, y0, x1, y1, width)` a diagonal bar or thin line, `wedge(apex, angle, half, length)`, `poly()`; `along(p, angle, d, side)`: from p walk angle for d, then offset to the side — for slanted type and labels
- Printing:
  - `ink(mask, 'red'|'black', knock=, density)`: print a plate, `knock` the letters knocked out of that colour;
  - `knock(mask, plates)`: knock out of these plates;
  - `tint(mask, plate, level, cell, angle)`: a flat screen;
  - `waves(cx, cy, r0, r1, step, width, a0, a1, plates, grow)`: concentric wave fronts within an angle range, `grow` negative to thin as they spread
- Type: `text(s, x, y, size, plate, font_style='grotesk'|'din'|'narrow'|'futura', anchor, spacing, rot, wobble, scale_x, knock)`; `text_mask()` for reversed-out type; `text_width()` measures width. grotesk is Helvetica Neue Condensed Black (falling back to Impact / DejaVu Sans Condensed Bold), din is DIN Condensed (falling back to Arial Narrow / PT Sans Narrow); fonts missing Cyrillic glyphs are skipped automatically; `wobble` gives big letters a hand-drawn jitter
- Photos `Photo(w, h, eye, target, fov, ss, key, fill, haze, shift, ...)`: a small perspective z-buffer rasteriser, units metres, Z up:
  - `material(albedo, gloss, shine, metal, tex, interior)` returns a material id; `tex` is a procedural texture computed in world coordinates (wood grain, dial scales, coil windings);
  - `cylinder()`, `lathe(base, axis, [(t, r), ...])` a solid of revolution, `sweep(path, radii)` a variable-radius tube (goosenecks, horns, flexes), `box()`, `hyperboloid(z0, z1, r0, r1, n, twist, mid)` one segment of a lattice tower;
  - `project()` converts world points to photo pixels, so other poster elements can line up with what is in the photo (a wedge meeting a horn bell);
