  - `render()` is called once: only pixels with geometry are shaded, buffers released afterwards
- Collage: `montage(photo, cx, cy, rot, scale, plate, cut, close, backdrop, cell, angle, contrast, soften)`: scale, rotate, place, then screen everything at 45° in poster coordinates (all photos share one screen).
  - `cut=` scissor it out along the silhouette with a margin: it covers what was printed below and shows a paper-coloured rim and the backdrop's glints;
  - `close=` bridges the narrow gaps scissors cannot enter;
  - without `cut` it is a cut-out photo, its halftone overprinting straight onto the red;
  - `soften` is the pre-screen blur (in grid units) — use 0.12 for thin structures like lattice towers
- Ageing: `age(folds=(vertical, horizontal), foxing, stain, pins, crack, grime)`: creases cracking the ink and wearing, foxing, water stains with tide lines, worn corners, rusty pin holes
- Order: paper → red-plate solids (circles, wedges, blocks) → black wave lines and diagonal bars (`knock` out of the red beneath) → photos → type and labels → age
- Colour constants `RED BLACK PAPER AGED`; angles counter-clockwise positive with 0° at 3 o'clock; `stage(name)` composites a snapshot immediately; `save('x.jpg')` defaults to quality 88, 4:4:4 chroma

## Art Deco — `ArtDeco`
- Idea: the painter draws no lines. Every shape is a knife-cut frisket sprayed with an airbrush, so edges are crisp and every inside is one smooth fade; form comes from tone alone: a tower is a flat silhouette sprayed dark on one side and light on the other, a cylinder gets one highlight band on the shoulder and one reflected light below
- Airbrush: `spray(mask, colour or a colour field, density, grain, blend='paint'|'screen'|'add')` one spray pass; thin film carries droplet grain (fixed noise × d(1−d)), solid film does not; `fade(mask, stops, p0, p1)` a linear fade, `glow(cx, cy, r, colour)` a radial glow; friskets `rect()`, `poly()`, `disc()` (all anti-aliased); `ramp(t, stops)` a colour ramp
- Sky: `sky(stops, top, bottom)` a long gradient; `burst(cx, cy, rays, contrast, reach, colour, dark, glow, phase, taper, bottom)` alternating light and dark rays (bright wedges slightly wider near the centre, contrast decaying with distance, cut off at `bottom`); `moon(cx, cy, r)` an off-centre radial-gradient sphere, halo, soft-edged maria; `searchlight(x, y, angle, length, spread)` a searchlight beam
- City: `tower(x, base, [(width, height), ...], body, rim, light=±1, flutes, windows, slits, far, spire, spire_w, mast, ledge, top_windows)` a stepped set-back tower, returns the top of its topmost level; `far` 0..1 pushes it into the haze (paler, bluer, flatter); `crown(x, y, w, steps)` a fan-stepped crown (radiating windows), returns the apex y; `clock(cx, cy, r, hour, minute)` a clock face
- River: `water(horizon, stops, reflect)` sprays the river gradient first, then mirrors what was already drawn above the horizon into it, broken into horizontal ripple dashes; brighter things reflect more strongly
- 3D train: `camera(f, cx, horizon, height)` a horizontal pinhole camera; `express(nose=(X, Z), vp_x, deck, cars, loco, car, gap, nose_len, rake, light, moon)` renders the streamlined express on its arched viaduct (distance fields + numpy sphere marching, only edge pixels 3×3 supersampled), returning layered results `r`; layered in the painter's order: `frisket(r)` frisket flats → `model(r)` tonal modelling (a half-Lambert ramp, an airbrushed highlight band on the shoulder, moon rim light, under-belly reflected light, far haze) → `lights(r, beam, beam_len, bloom, reflect)` windows (sleeping-car blinds half drawn), headlamp, beam, glow, the lamp lighting the rails, an optional river reflection; `project(X, Y, Z)` world to pixels
- Lettering: `lettering(s, x, base, cap, fill, track, shadow, anchor)` the built-in Deco display alphabet A–Z (heavy verticals, hairline horizontals, a high waistline, elliptical bowls), filled with a metallic gradient (one hard horizon line) and optional block shadow; `deco_width()` measures width; `caption(s, x, y, size, colour, track, anchor)` letter-spaced geometric sans small type (returns width); `rule()` gold rules; `border(inset, gap, width, step)` a stepped-corner double gold border
- Printing: `finish(paper, tooth, warm, vignette)` paper grain, slight flecks, a warm cast, a light vignette
- Colour constants `NAVY MIDNIGHT TEAL JADE GOLD BRASS IVORY CREAM MAROON CRIMSON WARM`; coordinates are pixels with y down, 3-D world units metres with y up; `save('x.jpg')` defaults to quality 88, 4:4:4 chroma

## Golden-age comic — `ComicCover`
- Idea: first "make" the cover the way the shop did, then "print" it the way the press did. The inker brushes key lines onto the black plate; the colourist assigns each area a mix of Ben-Day tint percentages (0 / 20 / 40 / 70 / 100 %) of the three process inks on the colour guide — the whole cover has only a few dozen colours; at print time each plate is screened at its own angle and lands a few pixels out of register, and the newsprint drinks the ink so dots go ragged, small dots drop and solids starve; transparent inks multiply. Neither the dots nor the mis-registration are hand-drawn
- Construction: `ComicCover(W, H, seed, screen=11, misreg=4, slop=1.6, light=(-0.6, -0.8))`: `screen` is the dot pitch (px), `misreg` each plate's offset, `slop` the hand-cut flats' wander; `register(plate, dx, dy, rot)` sets a plate's landing by hand
- Paper: `paper(colour, tone, fibres, flecks)` yellowed newsprint (cloudy pulp, dark and light short fibres, bark flecks, ink-repelling pits)
- Shapes (full-page anti-aliased masks, add, subtract, multiply): `mask(pts, smooth)`, `circle()`; static methods `ellipse_pts()`, `smooth()` (closed spline), `tube_pts(path, width list)` (limbs, ropes, scarves, ears), `burst_pts()` (stars, burst frames), `edge_normals(pts)` (contour points and outward normals, for `feather`); `grow()` / `shrink()`
- Colour guide: `flat(shape, colour)`, colour being four tint percentages (blue, red, yellow, black); `graded(shape, [colours...], p0, p1, edges=[...])` bands cut along hand-cut wavy edges; `rings()` concentric colour rings; `knock()` knocked white
- Black plate: `part(pts, colour, width, heavy)` is the standard way to place a foreground object: erase the ink and colour behind it, lay the flat, ring it with an outline heavier on the shaded side; `brush(pts, width, taper, swell)` a brush line pointed at both ends and swollen in the middle; `pen()` a fine pen; `outline()` / `edge()` closed / open contours; `ink()` solid black; `erase()`; `mask_outline(mask, width, heavy)` outlines any mask (a union of circles, lettering); `feather(P, N, length, width, spacing, clip, along, toward, curl)` feathering growing out of a shadow edge; `hatch()` parallel hatching; `speed()` speed lines; `dot()`
- Lettering: `letter(text, x, y, size, face, colour)` hand lettering, each letter slightly rotated and jumping (face: letter comic hand / logo narrow bold / bold / slab / heavy); `text_mask()`; `balloon(text, x, y, size, tail, kind='speech'|'yell'|'thought')` a speech balloon / a yell burst / a thought cloud; `caption(x0, y0, x1, y1, text, colour)` a narration box; `sfx(text, x, y, size, colour, rot, shade)` sound effects; `logo(text, quad, vp, depth, colours, shade, shadow, glints)` a perspective block logo: four corners set the perspective, extruded `depth` px toward the vanishing point, the front banded light-to-dark, with extrusion faces, a drop shadow, a heavy black frame and glint streaks
- Ageing (added after printing, each confined to its own area): `brown_edges()` yellowed edges; `spine(ticks, staples)` spine creases, white stress cracks, two rusted staples; `corner('br', radius)` ink worn off a corner, a crease; `crease(p0, p1)` ink cracked white along a fold; `foxing(n)` foxing
- Stages: `stage(name, plates='CMYK')` records the plates, printing only the listed ones at save time: 'K' for the black key lines, then 'YK', 'YMK', 'CMYK' — the printer's progressive proofs
- Colour constants (guide fractions): `WHITE CREAM PALE YELLOW GOLD ORANGE DEEP_ORANGE RED CRIMSON PINK SKIN TAN BROWN DARK_BROWN SKY LIGHT_BLUE BLUE NAVY VIOLET MAUVE LILAC GREEN LIME GREY LIGHT_GREY SILVER`; ink colour `INK`; `save('x.jpg')` defaults to quality 88, 4:4:4 chroma

## Pop silkscreen — `PopSilkscreen`
- Idea: rebuild the 1960s "Factory" method. One photo becomes a black photographic screen; one canvas is taped into a grid, each panel first hand-painted in loud flat acrylic (ground, then face, eyes, nose, collar...) with the shapes traced loosely off the photo and never quite registering; then the same black screen is pulled over each panel, each with its own placement, angle and ink load
- Construction: `PopSilkscreen(W, H, seed, light=(-0.55, -0.8))`; `canvas(gesso, thread=3.8, slub=0.18)`: cotton duck primed with gesso (a plain-weave height field, slubs, even lighting)
- The grid: `grid(cols, rows, gap, margin, origin=(0.5, 0.53), unit=0.285)` returns a list of `Panel`s. Each panel is drawn in "units": `origin` is the unit origin inside the panel (fractions of width, height), `unit` each unit's share of the panel height
- Masks on a `Panel` (a local window with `pad` px of margin around it, so bleed and misregistration fit):
  - `shape(pts)`: a closed spline
  - `stroke(pts, w0, w1, profile=)`: a tapering stroke, widths in units; `profile(t)` a custom width
  - `ellipse(u, v, ru, rv, rot)`, `dots([(u, v, r)])`
  - `soft(mask, sigma)`: softened to a tone for screening; `blank()`
  - Coordinates: `at(u, v)` units → local px, `xy(u, v)` units → canvas px
  - Module functions: `turn(pts, deg, about)` rotate counter-clockwise on screen (for tilted heads), `mirror(pts)` mirror left-right, `closed_spline()`
- Hand-painted acrylic:
  - `ground(p, colour, direction, bleed)`: a panel's ground colour, brushed up to the tape, bleeding under it in a few places
  - `paint(p, mask, colour, direction, wobble, drift, opacity, dry, streak)`: one hand-painted flat. `wobble` is the hand's edge jitter (px), `drift` how far the shape drifted (px); slight light and dark along the stroke, dry drag marks and a lit ridge of paint at the edge; confined to its own panel
- The black screen `key(p, solid, tone, ink, shift, rot, density, flood, starve, pull, nicks, ghost, cell, angle)` returns the coverage printed:
  - `solid` is the solid-black shadow masses; `tone` is the mid-tones, printed as a coarse 45° halftone (cell about 7 px); the photographic threshold's grainy edge and dust on the film are added automatically; `stencil()` returns the photographic mask alone
  - `shift`, `rot`: this panel's screen offset (px) and rotation (degrees)
  - `density`: the ink load — 0.92 starved, 1.0 normal, 1.15 flooded; below about 0.9 the face will not print
  - `flood`: too much ink clogs the screen — shapes swell, fine gaps fill, ink squidges out
  - `starve`: a dry patch on the mesh; `pull`: squeegee direction (90 top to bottom, 0 left to right); `nicks`: the fine pale lines a nicked blade leaves
  - `ghost=(dx, dy, strength)`: the screen placed twice, leaving a grey after-image
- `stage(name)`, `save(path, stages_dir)`; `save('x.jpg')` defaults to quality 88, 4:4:4 chroma
- Colour constants (60s acrylic clashes): `HOT_PINK PINK BLUSH RED ORANGE TANGERINE LEMON YELLOW CREAM LIME GREEN MINT TURQUOISE SKY BLUE ULTRA VIOLET LILAC WHITE`, primer `GESSO`, black ink `INK`

## ASCII art (line printer) — `LinePrinter`
- Idea: the scene is never drawn on the paper; it is painted into a hidden "role map" (6×10 samples per character cell): each region carries a role (which character set) and a tone (how much ink), later over earlier; `compose()` then picks each cell's character: line cells by the line's real-pixel direction and position in the cell (| / \ _ - . ' X +), silhouette turns the same way, fill cells pick from the role's character ladder by tone (error diffusion within a role), the darkest cells overstruck. Finally a drum line printer strikes the page line by line
- Construction: `LinePrinter(W, H, seed, cols=132, top=40, cpi=10, lpi=6, form_in=14.875, ss=2, weight='bold', ppi=None, page_in=11.0)`; `top` is the tear-line position, `ppi=None` stretches the paper to fill the frame — lower it and the whole sheet lies on the desk with the desk showing either side; `page_in` is the page depth (11 in = 66 rows, 8.5 in = 51 rows; with ppi it can put both tear lines in frame), `page_rows` the rows per page; `rows` is the visible rows; coordinates are always (col, row), floats allowed; `xy(col, row)` / `centre(col, row)` convert to pixels
- Paper: `form(white, green, desk, band=3, first_green, holes)`: 14 7/8-inch greenbar continuous paper, a pale green band every 3 rows, feed holes down both sides (the desk and the paper's shadow visible through them, the odd stretched broken hole), micro-perforations, a horizontal tear line and crease every `page_rows` rows; when the paper is narrower than the frame, the desk and the paper's shadow show either side. To leave a margin, `erase(rect(0, page_rows − 2, cols, rows))` before `compose()`
- Roles: `role(name, ramp, edge='outer'|'all'|None, outline='straight'|'round'|dict, dither, noise, group, inner_max)`; `ramp` light to dark, e.g. `['', '.', ':', '%', '@', '#', '#@']` where '#@' overstrikes @ onto #; `group` suppresses outlines between the same role; `inner_max` outlines between same-role patches only in the bright parts
- Shapes (sub-cell masks): `circle(col, row, radius in cols)` (auto-rounded to the cell aspect), `ellipse()`, `poly(points)`, `shape(control points)` a smooth closed curve, `rect()`, `blob()`
- Tones: `sphere_tone(col, row, r, light, amb)` a sphere (smoke puffs), `cylinder_tone(c0, c1, light, amb)` a vertical cylinder (the rocket body), `ramp_tone(p0, p1, t0, t1)` a linear ramp
- Drawing: `fill(mask, role, tone)`; `erase(mask)` back to white paper; `stroke(points, width, glyphs=None|'round'|a single character, smooth)` lines (a single character like '=' forces it — platforms and beams use it); `billow([(col, row, r), ...], role, light, amb, shadow, glow=(col, row, r, strength), lift=tone field)` a cloud: each puff lit as a sphere, casting a contact shadow on the puffs behind it, `glow` lit from inside by flame, `lift` darkening the cloud base
- Text: `text(s, col, row, strike=2)` typed as-is (strike=2 overstrikes for bold; `opaque` lets spaces punch out what is beneath), `text_v()` vertical; `plot(col, row, w, h, xlim, ylim, [(xs, ys, character), ...], xticks, yticks)` a printer plot (I vertical, - horizontal, + ticks), returns a data-to-cell converter (to ring points on top of it)
- Printing: `compose()` builds the character grid (`listing(path)` saves it as plain text); `print_rows(r0, r1)` strikes line by line (all overstrikes of one row finish before the paper feeds): the same character always sits a little high or low (the drum's phase), each column's hammer strength differs (one or two weak columns), the overstrike pass lands offset by fractions of a pixel, the ribbon fades over the job and wears along its width, occasional low-ribbon rows printing faint heads, worn slugs missing the same nick every time, ink soaking into the paper, hammers pressing a faint dent
- Annotation: `pen(points, colour, width)` a ballpoint (ink pooling at landing and lift-off, paler where fast, broken on the tooth), `ring(x, y, rx, ry)` a casual unclosed ring, `tick(x, y, size)` a tick, `pen_text(s, x, y, size)` handwriting; these use pixel coordinates
- Layering and animation: `fill`/`stroke`/`text` the whole picture first, then `compose()`, then several `print_rows` passes each followed by `stage(name)` — the animation of a page being printed; `save('x.jpg')` defaults to quality 88, 4:4:4 chroma
- Colour constant `INK`; `save(path, stages_dir)` outputs the stages at 1×

## Low poly (1999 3D) — `LowPoly`
- Idea: a software rasteriser written only in numpy, drawing the way a 1999 console did: a small framebuffer (default 384×216), vertices snapped to whole pixels, back-face culling, the whole scene's triangles in one painter's-sorted list (the ordering table) drawn far to near, near-plane clipping; one colour per triangle (flat shading), textures affinely interpolated with no perspective correction, per-vertex fog; then 15-bit colour through a 4×4 ordered dither and a nearest-neighbour blow-up. Call order never decides occlusion
- Construction: `LowPoly(W, H, scale=5, seed, record=True, zbuffer=False, near=0.6, far=260)`; world coordinates X right, Y up, Z forward, in metres; `zbuffer=True` is N64-style per-pixel depth
- Camera: `camera(eye, target or yaw=, pitch=, fov, roll)`; `aim(world point, at=(fx, fy))` turns the camera so that point lands at a given screen fraction (for composition); `project(p)`, `ray(sx, sy)`
- Sky and light: `sky(zenith, horizon, glow, bands)` a gradient by elevation, a warm halo round the sun, optional thin streak clouds; `sun(screen=(fx, fy) or direction=)` draws the sun and sets the light direction; `light(lift=degrees)` raises the shading light separately; `ambient(sky, ground)` hemisphere ambient; `fog(near, far)` per-vertex fog, defaulting to the horizon colour
- Terrain: `path([(x, z), ...])` a centre line, `along(path, s, u, y)` a point on it, `heading(path, s)` its direction; `canyon(path, step, height, width, wobble, strata, buried)` a canyon lofted along the line (a river bed, sand banks, scree, two cliff levels and steps, the canyon rim, mesas; walls carry strata by height, flats topped with sand or scrub); `river(path, half, ripple, spec)` a river of uneven facets; `mesa(x, z, r, h, base)` a mesa; `arch(path, s, span, rise, base)` a natural stone arch; `boulder(x, y, z, r)`; `tree(x, y, z, height)` a crossed-quad juniper (texture alpha hard-cut)
- Objects: `ring(centre, facing, radius, tube)` a faceted gold hoop; `aircraft(pos, heading, roll, pitch, size, body, trim, canopy, spinner)` a small stunt plane of about 230 triangles plus a half-transparent propeller disc; `torus()`; `mesh(V, F, colour, uv, tex, lit, spec, shine, blend, alpha, two_sided, fog, bias, cutout, glow)` any mesh: blend is 'opaque' | 'half' | 'add' | 'sub' | 'quarter' (the era's four translucent modes), `bias` is the sort bias (negative draws later), `glow` pulls faces toward full brightness (pickups)
- Effects: `shadow(x, z, y, radius)` a round blob shadow straight below ('sub' blend); `sparkle(p, size)` an additive four-point star; `flare()` a screen-space lens flare (not drawn when the sun is occluded)
- Textures: `texture('strata'|'ledge'|'sand'|'water'|'rock'|'juniper'|'spark')` 32–64 px procedural textures, sampled nearest-neighbour
- HUD (framebuffer pixel coordinates): `text(s, x, y, scale, fill=colour or a top-down ramp list, italic, anchor)` a 5×7 bitmap font (bold, italic, a 1 px black outline plus a drop shadow); `counter(label, value, x, y, icon, anchor)` a small label with a big italic number; `icon('ring'|'plane'|'clock')`; `gauge(x, y, value, number, unit)` a stepped speed bar; `panel()` a translucent frame; `minimap(x0, y0, x1, y1, path, s0, s1, rings, done, plane, heading, finish, title)` a course minimap rotated nose-up
- Animation: `stage(name)`; `stage(name, wire=True)` a hidden-line wireframe; `stage(name, fog=False, textures=False)` a flat-shaded preview (each face takes its centre texel)
- Colour constants `GOLD WHITE BLACK HUD_YELLOW HUD_ORANGE HUD_RED HUD_GREEN HUD_CYAN`; `save('x.jpg')` defaults to quality 88, 4:4:4 chroma

## Stencil graffiti — `Stencil`
- Idea: a real wall, real card stock stencils, a spray can. The wall is albedo plus a height map lit by a low sun; a stencil is a cut sheet of card — the parts the cuts isolate (the middle of an O, an arm from a body, spokes from a wheel) would fall off, so they are held by bridges, and the bridges read as hairlines of bare wall in the paint; spraying is the can sweeping back and forth, each sweep laying one gaussian cone, the paint film the sum of the strokes; paint is made of droplets — thick places join into solid colour, thin places are scattered dots, so every soft edge is stippled, never blurred; paint will not enter small pits, lies on the relief with a satin sheen, and runs off heavy lower edges as drips
- Wall: `wall(colour, planks, ties, ground, damp, crack)` board-marked concrete: colour bands and wood grain pressed in by each plank (wandering around knots), butt-joint seams, broken paste fins squeezed out of the gaps, form-tie holes (rusted rods / mortar plugs / empty), bug holes, rain and rust streaks, a damp tide line with efflorescence, a hairline crack; `pavement(colour)` an asphalt pavement at the wall's foot (aggregate, patched tar, gum stains, oil, dust and weeds)
- Old marks: `buff(x0, y0, x1, y1, colour, roller, ragged)` a council roller buff (vertical roller tracks, stepped top and bottom edges, dried-out ends, overlaps slightly darker, what is beneath showing through a little); `freehand(..., wash=0.7)` an old signature half pressure-washed away
- Stencils: `card(x0, y0, x1, y1, origin, scale, tape, material='manila'|'board')` returns a `Card`, shapes in design units (px = origin + scale × point), returning local masks you can add and subtract with numpy:
  - `poly(pts, smooth, facet)` (`facet` is the knife's per-segment jitter along curves), `capsule(p0, p1, r0, r1)`, `ellipse(c, rx, ry, rot)`, `ring(c, outer, inner)`, `stroke(pts, w0, w1)` a variable-width band
  - `text(s, x, y, size, font='stencil'|'slab'|'block', anchor, spacing, bridge)` stencil letters: every counter automatically bridged above and below
  - `cut(mask)` cut open; `keep(mask)` keep the card (reversed letters, details); `bridge(p0, p1, width)` place a bridge by hand; `bridges(width)` find every island that would fall and add the shortest bridges (two, in opposite directions, for large islands); `islands()` how many px of card would still fall (0 means cut)
- Tone cards: `shade_side(mask, light, depth, soft, wobble, seed)` a crescent `depth` px wide on the side away from the light — the grey and black cards of multi-tone stencils cut with it; `wobble` makes the edge read hand-cut
- Spraying: `spray(card, colour, coats, cone, angle, misreg, at, rot, lift, mist, drips, drip_len, wet, linger, ghost)`. `cone` is the spray width (px); `misreg` the random placement error (about 2 for multi-colour); `at` / `rot` move and turn the same small card to spray again (swallows, leaves, bow tails); `lift` how much the card lifts (soft edges and creep); `mist` overspray fog; `drips` how many drips, `wet` the film thickness where drips start
- Freehand: `freehand(pts, colour, width, coats, speed, spits, drips, mist, wash)` a skinny cap: width and density follow hand speed (`speed` one factor per control point, slow = heavy), a blob at touch-down, drips at pauses, a few large spits beside the line
- `stage(name, card=c)` snapshots with the card still taped to the wall (tape, paint accumulated on the card, cut edges lit on one side, the card's shadow) — for the GIF; `save(path, stages_dir)` defaults to quality 88, 4:4:4 chroma
- Colour constants `BLACK WHITE GREY RED CONCRETE`; `HIDE` each colour's hiding power (white worst), `GLOSS` gloss; fonts from `stencil_font(style, size)` finding DIN Condensed Bold / Impact, Rockwell, Arial Black, overridable with `INKPAINT_FONT_STENCIL` etc.

## Embroidered patches — `Patch`
- Idea: stitched the way an embroidery machine does: every shape is cut into parallel rows at its own angle (scan lines), each row into stitches, so a shape's edge is a row of stitch ends. Every thread is lit as a cylinder that dives into the cloth at both needle holes (the holes are little pits), and the highlight is anisotropic (Kajiya-Kay) — the same colour sewn at two angles catches the light differently, which is exactly what reads as "embroidered" rather than "printed". A badge starts as a cut twill blank laid on the denim (with thickness and a shadow), then stitches, then an edge, then whip-stitching it on
- Denim: `denim(colour, weft, warp_px, pick_px, fade)` a 3/1 right-hand twill (warp denser than weft, wales about 60°, pale weft dots, slubs, worn highlights), rendered 2×; `seam(pts, thread, rows, stitch, gap, fold)` a felled seam with double topstitching, the fold worn pale
- Blanks: `blank(pts, colour)` returns a `Blank` (`.pts` outline, `.mask` mask); outlines: `circle_pts(cx, cy, r)`, `arch_pts(cx, top, w, h, corner)` an arch badge, `rocker_pts(cx, cy, r0, r1, span, centre)` an arc rocker; masks: `circle()`, `ring()`, `poly(pts, smooth)`, `text_mask()`, combined with `*`, `np.maximum`, `1 - m`
- Fill: `fill(mask, colour, angle, kind='tatami'|'satin', pitch, length, stagger, maxlen)`: tatami for large grounds (short stitches about 15 px, rows staggered by 0.3 stitch); satin one long float per row (letters, snow caps, flames, long thin shapes), split into interleaved segmented satin above `maxlen`; `angle` is the stitch direction (degrees counter-clockwise, 0 = left-right)
- Along a path: `column(pts, colour, width, closed)` a satin column with stitches perpendicular to the path (ring dividers, logs, tent poles); `run(pts, colour, stitch, gap, width, clip=)` running stitch / sashiko dashes / topstitching; `knot(x, y, colour, r)` a French knot (star points, sparks); `star(x, y, r, colour)` a small four-point satin star
- Edges: `merrow(blank, colour, width)` a merrowed edge: a rolled cord of slanted wraps over the cloth edge — for circles, ellipses, arc rockers; `satin_border(blank, colour, width)` a hot-cut satin border for other shapes
- Lettering: `text(s, x, y, size, colour)`, `arc_text(s, cx, cy, r, size, colour, centre=90, bottom=False)` satin letters with each letter stitched at an angle; `bottom=True` reads along the lower arc, letter heads inward
- Hand sewing: `whip(blank, colour, frm, to)` whip stitches a fraction of the outline's length, returning the last stitch's position; `thread(pts, colour, lift)` loose threads (twisted, the shadow stretching the higher they lift); `needle(eye, tip)` a steel needle lying on the cloth (draw the thread first: it shows through the needle's eye)
- `stage(name)`, `save(path, stages_dir)`; `save('x.jpg')` defaults to quality 88, 4:4:4 chroma

## Blueprint — `Blueprint`
- Idea: the drawing is inked first on tracing linen with a few fixed ruling-pen weights (outlines, hatching, dimensions, single-stroke lettering are all "ink"), then contact-printed like a real blueprint: ink blocks the arc lamp so the coating under every line stays un-blue and washes out white, everything else becomes Prussian blue. Exposure = transmission × lamp intensity; development follows a threshold S-curve — heavy lines print clean white, hairlines pale blue, the ground saturates; light creeps under line edges (sharp where the glass pressed the linen flat, soft where contact was poor) with a faint halo; the lamp is stronger mid-frame, the coating carries machine streaks, mottle and wash runs from the drying line; colour is Beer-Lambert Prussian blue on paper with the fibres showing through. Then the sheet's life: folded, faded, handled, stained, pinned, and finally a site engineer's red wax-pencil marks
- Construction: `Blueprint(W, H, seed, ss=3, tracing=(x0, y0, x1, y1))`; `expose(time, contact)`: exposure time (0.8 under-exposed and pale, 1.3 over) and how uneven the frame's contact is
- Pens: `line(pts, pen, closed)`, pen is `'heavy' 'thick' 'medium' 'thin' 'fine'` or a pixel width, with a little pooled ink at line starts; `dashed(pts, pen, dash)` dashed; `centerline(pts)` dot-dash; `circle`, `arc`, `box`; `fill(pts)` solid fills (thin-metal sections, arrows, black scale-bar cells); `dot`; `erase(pts)` scraped off with a blade; `pencil(pts)` pencil lines left on the linen (printing as very faint lines); `Blueprint.arc_pts(...)` takes arc points (angles counter-clockwise, paper y up)
- Hatching: `hatch(polys, angle, spacing, pen, holes, kind)`, kind is `'lines'` stone/general, `'cross'` metal, `'glass'` three short lines for glass, `'rock'` rock fissures, `'stipple'` concrete dots, `'solid'` solid; `ground(pts)` a natural ground line with tick hatches; `breakline(p0, p1)` a break line
- Text: `text(s, x, y, size, anchor, pen, slant, spacing, rot, guide, clear)` a built-in single-stroke capital font (lettering-guide style: A–Z, 0–9, Ø ± ° × ℄ ✓ → etc.), `size` is the cap height, `anchor` like `'ls' 'mm' 'rs'` (s = baseline), `slant` about 0.22 for italics, `guide=True` leaves the pencil guide lines, `clear=True` scrapes the hatching from behind the text first, `\n` breaks lines; `text_width(s, size)`; `cjk(s, x, y, size, anchor, condense)` a condensed long-Fangsong Chinese face (finds STFangsong / FangSong, falling back to a CJK font, overridable with `INKPAINT_FONT_FANGSONG`)
- Drafting symbols: `dim(p0, p1, offset, text, size, arrows, scale, fmt, text_off, shift, clear)` dimensions (extension lines, arrows, numbers written along the dimension line, short dimensions putting the arrows outside automatically; `offset=0` skips the extension lines so you can chain dimensions yourself); `level(x, y, text, side, length, mark_at)` level marks; `leader(pts, text, end='arrow'|'dot'|None)` leaders; `cut_mark(p0, p1, label, view)` section marks; `bubble(x, y, r, top, bottom)` detail bubbles; `view_title(x, y, title, sub, size, bubble)` view titles (a heavy-thin double underline + the scale); `north(x, y, r)` a north point; `scale_bar(x, y, px_per_unit, units, label)` graphic scales; `border(x0, y0, x1, y1, zones)` the border and zone letters; `table(x0, y0, widths, heights)` title / revision blocks, returning cell coordinates `[row][col]`; `revision(x, y, mark)` revision triangles
- The sheet's life (applies only to the printed blueprint): `fold(xs, ys, wear)` folded to filing size (the coating cracks white along creases, one side light one side dark, worn at crossings); `fade(x0, y0, x1, y1, amount)` the outside panel faded; `grime(...)` handling grime; `stain(x, y, r)` an alkaline water stain (blue to rust-brown, two tide lines); `tack(x, y)` pin holes and rust rings; `tear(x, y, direction, length)` a small tear where a crease meets the paper edge
- Red wax pencil: `red(pts, width)`, `cloud(pts, bump)` revision clouds, `red_text(s, x, y, size, rot)` handwritten notes; red is the only colour in the picture that is not part of the print
- `stage(name)`, `save(path, stages_dir)`; `save('x.jpg')` defaults to quality 88, 4:4:4 chroma

## Children's crayon — `Crayon`
- Idea: rough paper is a tooth height field equalised to a uniform [0, 1], so "1 − threshold" is exactly the fraction of paper the wax can reach; wax only catches the tooth's peaks: wax lays where `tooth + 0.35 × existing wax > 1 − contact × pressure`; light strokes leave a scatter of white specks, heavy strokes press into the valleys, and later colours catch more easily on earlier wax (build-up). The crayon is dragged along its stroke, so the skipped pits stretch into short streaks along it. The worn tip is a faceted face: one stroke = K (about width / 3) parallel sub-lines of varying strength, with white streaks inside the stroke. Colours mix semi-opaquely and multiplicatively: `new = lerp(base, colour × base^0.6, coverage)` — blue over yellow goes green, black outlines survive the colour, the same colour deepens with repeats. Finally tooth and wax are lit together as one height field by a low upper-left light, with a waxy sheen on thick wax
- Construction: `Crayon(W, H, seed, paper='#f7f3e9')`; `paper(tone, grain, fibres, scan)` rough paper (tooth, pulp fibres, uneven scanning light); colour constant `c.C` (a 24-crayon box: red orange yellow yellowgreen green darkgreen sky blue navy violet purple pink magenta brown tan peach apricot black grey white ...)
- Child-drawn shapes (return points): `circle(cx, cy, rx, ry, rot, lumpy)`, `rect(x0, y0, x1, y1, skew)` (each corner skewed a little), `poly(pts)`, `wobble(pts, amp)`; masks: `mask(pts)`, `stroke_mask(pts, width)`, `grow(mask, px)`, `drawn()` (where the paper is already drawn)
- Filling:
  - `scribble(mask, colour, angle, width, density, pressure, reach, mess, angle_jitter, avoid, halo, fade)`: back-and-forth scribbles. `reach` is arm's length; large areas are filled patch by patch, each patch at its own angle (None = one patch); low `density` leaves triangular white gaps between passes; `mess` is the overshoot / shortfall at the outline; `avoid` + `halo` colour around things, leaving a white rim (careful turns at the avoided edges, never entering gaps narrower than 0.7 × stroke width); `fade=(y full, y none)` thins toward the bottom (the bored-out sky);
  - `loops(mask, colour, r, width, density)`: loop scribbles (tree crowns, suns, balls, spots);
  - `follow(pts, colour, width, passes, crayon)`: fills along a band (each rainbow stripe, roads);
  - `burnish(mask, strength)`: pressed hard: existing wax is pushed into the pits, white specks close, colour deepens and shines
- Lines and dots: `line(pts, colour, width, pressure, closed, avoid)` a crayon line, heavy landing, light lift-off; `closed=True` the tail runs past the start and misses it by a few px; `avoid` stops the line behind things; `dab(x, y, r, colour)` a solid dot pressed into the paper (eyes, apples, watermelon seeds)
- Writing: `write(s, x, y, size, colour or colour list, width, tilt, jumble, spacing, anchor)`: thin-font glyphs are thinned to centrelines and traced with a round crayon; each letter tilted ±tilt°, size and baseline jumping by `jumble`, colours cycling through a list. The font is found from STHeiti Light / Hiragino Sans GB / Noto Sans CJK Light / Microsoft YaHei Light, overridable with `INKPAINT_FONT_CRAYON`
- Things on the paper: `crumbs(x0, y0, x1, y1, colours, n)` wax crumbs rubbed off (raised, with little shadows); `stick(x, y, angle, colour, length=360, radius=22, tip='worn'|'new'|'broken', wrap, peel, label)` a real crayon (matte wax, printed wrapper, a fake-text label, a snapped end, peeled paper), drawn at composite time with a soft shadow
- Order: outline first (each thing's inner mask collected into an OBJ), then fill the big areas avoiding `U(OBJ, drawn())`, then colour the things on the white paper; words and the signature go on before the sky / grass
- `stage(name)`, `save(path, stages_dir)`; `save('x.jpg')` defaults to quality 88, 4:4:4 chroma; the low-level `deposit(cover, box, colour)` lays wax for any contact field against the tooth

## Green-screen CRT terminal — `CRTTerminal`
- Idea: rebuild a character terminal; nothing is "drawn" on the screen:
  - everything on screen is a character in character memory: each cell carries a brightness attribute (dim / normal / bold), reverse video, underline; lines can be double width or double height;
  - the font ROM is a 5×7 glyph in a 7×10 cell, lowercase with two descender rows; a graphics set accompanies it: box drawing ─│┌┐└┘├┤┬┴┼, five scan-line bars ⎺⎻─⎼⎽ for curves (`SCAN`), eighth blocks ▁▂▃▄▅▆▇█ (`EIGHTHS`), ░▒▓, ° ·, ▲▼◆, arrows;
  - ASCII art is painted into a hidden "material + tone" map (7×10 samples per cell), and `compose()` picks each cell's character: interior cells by the material's ladder and tone with error diffusion within a group; cells the silhouette crosses by the edge's direction and position ( _ . - ' / \ ( ) | ); stroke cells by stroke direction. On screen "dense = bright": dark things must be sparse and dim;
  - the beam paints ten scan lines per text row: dots stretched half a dot, video-amplifier smear, the spot swelling with current (bold fills the gaps between scan lines);
  - P1 phosphor saturates to white; after that, afterglow, screen burn, barrel distortion, edge falloff, halation in the glass and the room's reflection
- Construction: `CRTTerminal(W, H, seed, cols=120, rows=36, phosphor='green'|'amber'|'white', opening=(88, 60, 1832, 958), radius=62, raster=(0.955, 0.93), curve=0.065)`; `opening` is the bezel opening, `raster` the undistorted raster's share of it, `curve` the barrel strength; `cw`, `ch` are a cell's pixel size
- Character memory: `write(col, row, s, attr)`, attr is `'dim'`, `'normal'` or `'bold'`, plus `'+rev'` reverse and `'+ul'` underline; `big(col, row, s)` double-height double-width, occupying rows row and row+1 (those rows only take big text, each letter two columns); `wide()` double width; `box(c0, r0, c1, r1, attr, title)`, `hline()`, `vline()`; `cursor(col, row, 'block'|'ul')`; `clear()`; `listing(path)` saves plain text
- ASCII art (the hidden map):
  - `material(name, ramp, attrs, outline, group, edge, dither)`: `ramp` dark to light, e.g. `' .:+*%#@'`; `attrs` one brightness per step — brighten with this first, not with fancier characters; `outline` is `'cloud'` (slopes always ( )), `'round'`, `'straight'` or `None` (tone only, no outline); `group` suppresses outlines between the same material; `edge` fixes the outline glyph's brightness
  - Shapes: `circle(col, row, r)` (r in columns, auto-rounded to the cell aspect), `ellipse()`, `poly()`, `shape()` (smooth closed), `rect()`; tones: `sphere_tone()`, `radial_tone()`, `ramp_tone()`
  - Drawing: `fill(mask, material, tone)`, `erase(mask)`, `stroke(points, material, width, glyph)`; `puffs([(col, row, r), ...], material, light, amb, base, flat, base_dark)` a cloud, `base` in integer rows, flat bottoms struck as one _
  - `compose(region)` picks characters, `reveal(r0, r1)` puts them on screen in stretches; `put(col, row, s, attr)`, `sprite(col, row, rows list, attr, attrs, swap)` hand-placed little pieces (stars, rain, houses, lighthouses, lit windows) — spaces inside a sprite's rows are opaque
- Charts: `plot(col, row, w, h, xs, ys, xlim, ylim, attr)` one scan-line bar per column, five heights per row, returns `at(x, y)` converting data to cell coordinates for labels; `bars(col, row, h, values, vmax, attr)` eighth-block bars
- The tube: `trail(col, row, s, level)` afterglow (about 0.32 for the previous frame, 0.10 for the one before); `burn(col, row, s, depth)` screen burn — best recognised in the gaps of reverse-video bars; `tear(row, dx, lines)` sync tearing; `power(on)` with the set off, only the glass and reflections remain; `brightness` the raster's base glow
- Stage GIF: power off → power-on raster → the command typed → the header → the art streaming in row by row → the curve → bars and axes → the conclusion; `stage(name)`, `save(path, stages_dir)`; `save('x.jpg')` defaults to quality 88, 4:4:4 chroma
- Constants `LEVELS PHOSPHORS SCAN EIGHTHS PUTTY ROM_CHARS`

## Thermal camera — `Thermal`
- Idea: a thermal camera sees neither colour nor light, only how hot each surface is — so no RGB is painted. Three steps: paint a temperature field (°C, at 2× sensor resolution); pass it through a modelled uncooled camera (lens blur, box sampling onto sensor pixels, noise and column stripes, a centre-cool vignette, detail enhancement, automatic gain); finally map it through a false-colour palette and overlay a crisp OSD. Coordinates are canvas pixels, temperature in °C
- Construction: `Thermal(W, H, sensor=(640, 360), ss=2, seed, ambient=21)`; the smaller the sensor the blurrier — the examples use 480×270
- Shapes → masks: `poly(pts)`, `shape(pts)` a closed spline, `rect(x0, y0, x1, y1, r)`, `ellipse(cx, cy, rx, ry, rot)`, `blob()`, `line(pts, width)`; combine with `*`, `1 - m`, `np.clip(a + b, 0, 1)`
- Temperature fields: `ramp(p0, t0, p1, t1, ease)` linear, `radial(cx, cy, rx, ry, t0, t1, power)`, `texture(scale, seed)` noise; `room(top, bottom, y_top, y_bottom)` the background air layered warm over cool
- Objects: `surface(mask, temp, emissivity, reflected, grain, soft, fuzz, limb, limb_px)`: `temp` can be a number or a ramp / radial field; for emissivity < 1, polished metal's reading is pulled toward the reflected temperature (room temperature by default); `grain` material unevenness (°C); `fuzz` fuzzy edges; `limb` rounded bodies cooling toward their outline. `warm(mask, dT)` adds or removes heat; `seam(pts, dT, width)` door gaps, tile joints, kinked lines
- Heat leaking outward:
  - `soak(mask, temp, reach, strength, onto)`: heat halo soaking into the surrounding surfaces (soak before drawing the object itself; `onto` limits the receiving surface)
  - `plume(path, temp, w0, w1, opacity, cool, eddy, wisp, puffs, seed, fade_out)`: steam / vapour / cold air along a path, widening, cooling and breaking up as it goes; `temp` below room temperature is cold air (the path heads downward); `rise(x, y, height, sway, lean)` generates a rising path
  - `pool(x, y, temp, reach, spread, tongues, squash, width)` cold (or warm) tongues spreading over the floor, `spread` the direction range on the ground (0 = to the right, 90 = toward the lens)
  - `prints(steps, temp, size, squash, spread)` residual paw prints, steps = [(x, y, heading°, scale, age 0 fresh .. 1 gone)], older = fainter and more spread; `paw()` a single paw mask
  - `reflect(region, axis_y, strength, blur_px, fade)` glazed tiles mirroring the hotter things above
- Camera: `camera(palette, span, agc, plateau, curve, netd, fpn, optics, dde, dde_clip, narcissus, pixel, saturate)`
  - palette: iron / rainbow / white_hot / black_hot / arctic; `span=None` sets the range automatically from the picture
  - `agc`: the share of plateau-limited histogram equalisation; `curve=[(°C, 0..1), ...]` a hand-tuned tone curve replacing the linear span — use it when the room, the cat and the stove should each own a stretch of colour
  - `netd`, `fpn`: temporal noise and column stripes (°C); `optics` lens blur; `dde` detail enhancement (amplifying only small temperature differences); `narcissus` the centre-cool vignette; `pixel` how much of the sensor pixel grid remains
- OSD (recorded when called, read off that stage's sensor image at save time, so numbers and colours always agree):
  - `statusbar(rec, date, clock, battery, emissivity, mode, height, reflected)`
  - `scalebar(x0, y0, x1, y1, ticks, height)`: wider than tall is horizontal; ticks placed by the gain curve's non-linear positions, crowded ones skipped automatically; with `height` a translucent bar is added at the bottom
  - `spot(x, y, label, name, side, r)`, `box(x0, y0, x1, y1, label, name)`, `hottest(region)`, `coldest(region)`, `profile(p0, p1, inset=(x, y, w, h), label, name)` a line profile + inset, `results(x, y, width)` a readings table, `reticle()`, `text()`
- `level(v)` where a temperature lands on the palette; `stage(name)` stores only the temperature field and the OSD count, `save(path, stages_dir)` renders every snapshot with the final gain so colours do not jump between stages; `save('x.jpg')` defaults to quality 88, 4:4:4 chroma

## Delft tiles — `DelftTile`
- Idea: paint a tin-glazed tile wall in workshop order — blank tiles on a grid that is never quite true; a pricked paper pattern pounces charcoal dots (the pounce); the trek (outline) traces the pounce with a fine long brush; then loose cobalt washes with a broad brush (the raw glaze drinks fast, washes cannot blend, so they leave brush bands, bristle streaks, dried darker edges, darkening overlaps and misses against the outline); before firing the pigment is dull grey-black and the glaze a matte chalky white; in the kiln the glaze brightens and the pigment becomes cobalt by per-channel Beer-Lambert absorption, bleeding into the glaze with fine grain. There is one transparent pigment, so paint front to back and reserve the foreground with `occlude()`. Each tile is painted and fired on its own: lines step one or two px at the joints and each tile has its own white and its own blue; quarter ornaments are painted per tile and only become whole where four single tiles meet. The wall is a height field: each tile slightly domed and tilted, chamfers lit, window reflections broken into bent shards
- Construction: `DelftTile(W, H, seed, tile=128, joint=3.6, origin=(0, -36), ss=3, glaze=(cool white, warm white), mortar, light)`; `tile` is the tile pitch (about 13 cm per tile), `origin` aligns the picture to the grid; `tableau(i0, k0, i1, k1)` marks the tiles forming one picture (i0 ≤ i < i1) and returns the outer frame in pixels; `rect(i, k)`, `centre(i, k)`, `visible()`, `in_tableau(i, k)`
- Layers: `layer(name)`, each with a trek (line) and a wash copy; `stage(name, hide=(...))` can hide layer names ('single'), kinds ('wash'), particular copies ('picture.wash'), the pounce ('pounce') or everything ('*') — to replay the workshop
- Brushes:
  - `trek(points, width, dens, taper, wobble, smooth, pounce, bead)` the outline: a small ink bead at the landing, a pointed lift-off, downward strokes heavier, a slightly shaking hand, pounce points recorded as it goes; `line(p0, p1, bend)`, `closed(points)`, `dot(x, y, r)`;
  - `wash(shape, tone, angle, pool, ragged, slip, band, streak, grad, holes)` the wash: shape is a polygon, a list of polygons or a full mask; tone about 0.1 pale to 0.5 deep; `angle` the stroke direction; `grad=((x0, y0), (x1, y1), f0, f1)` a gradient along a line; `holes=[polygons]` reserves;
  - `hatch(shape, angle, spacing, width, dens, wave)` hatching (shaded sides, sails, thatch, hulls);
  - `letters(text, x, y, size, font='serif', spacing)` hand lettering
- Reserving: `occlude(shape, grow)` keeps the outline of what is drawn so later lines and washes avoid it; `clear_occlusion()`
- Motifs (Delft stock, coordinates in canvas px): `windmill(x, ground y, hub height, sails, squash, cloth)` a smock mill with a gallery (`squash < 1` turns the sails toward the front left); `sailboat(stern x, waterline y, length, facing, belly, cargo, skipper, pennant, leeboard)` a flat-bottomed sailing barge (a gaff mainsail, a jib, leeboards, a masthead pennant, cargo sacks, the skipper), returns the hull outline; `house(x0, x1, ground y, wall height, gable='step'|'bell'|'neck'|'spout', gable_h, floors, chimney, smoke=(dx, dy), vane=-1, shade='right')` a canal house (cross windows, a door, a hoisting beam, brick joints, smoke, a weathervane); `tree(x, ground y, h, w, lean, clumps)`; `bushes(x0, x1, y, h, gap)` distant trees on the horizon; `tulip(x, ground y, h, lean)`; `cloud(cx, cy, w, h, lean)`; `birds([(x, y), ...])`; `water(x0, x1, y0, y1, far, near, avoid)` ripples denser far and sparser near; `reflection(x, waterline y, w, depth)`; `grass()`; `smoke()`; point helpers `arc()`, `ellipse_pts()`
- Single tiles: `field(tiles=None, corners='spin'|'lelie'|'dot', medallion=True, motifs)` paints every non-picture tile with four quarter corner ornaments, a double-ring medallion and a little motif ('tulip' 'ship' 'mill' 'boat' 'fish' 'bird' 'house' 'pot', never repeating against the left and upper neighbours); `corner()`, `medallion(i, k)`, `vignette(i, k, kind)` are usable alone
- Border: `border(rect, band, inset, tone, leaf, reserve=[scroll shapes])` a blue border round the picture: vines, leaves and berries reserved, corner roses, `reserve` leaving room for lettering scrolls
- Process: `fire()` the kiln (grey-black pigment to cobalt, the glaze brightening, pigment bleeding with grain, the pounce burned off, each tile's cobalt a little different); `set_wall()` lime-mortar joints; `age(craze, chips, specks, nails, cracks=[points])` crazing, iron specks and pinholes, chips, the nail holes of corner-cutting templates, cracks within a single tile; `light(window=(x, y, w, h), strength=0.5, panes=(2, 3), soft=8)` the window's reflection on the glaze, x, y where the reflection falls when the wall is perfectly flat; layered over the glaze, the picture showing through; only visible where things were painted (clouds, the border), nearly invisible on plain white glaze
- `stage(name, hide)`, `save(path, stages_dir)`; `save('x.jpg')` defaults to quality 88, 4:4:4 chroma
- Constants `COBALT_K` (the fired cobalt's absorption), `RAW_K` (raw pigment), `GLAZE_COOL GLAZE_WARM RAW_GLAZE MORTAR BED BODY`

## Cave painting — `CavePainting`
- Idea: pigment is laid ON, not pecked (the opposite of the petroglyph): a pale limestone height field plus layered pigment coverage fields (multiplied into the albedo, the rock's mottling showing through), lit at the end only by the campfire and the stone lamp on the floor: each a point light with half-Lambert shading + inverse-square falloff + per-pixel shadows marched along the light (raking light makes every bulge read as volume), a specular sheen on wet flowstone, a little cool ambient in the hollows; then flame glow, bloom, smoke and a tone curve. Lighting is cached and recomputed only when the height field changes
- Construction: `CavePainting(W, H, seed)`
- Cave wall: `wall(colour, relief, scallops, grain)` limestone (broad swells, ridges, solution scallops, grain, pits, ochre and grey mottling, calcite crust on the convex parts); `boss(x, y, rx, ry, height, rot)` a bulge (paint the bison's belly and the horse's flank on one); `fold(pts, height, soft, side)` a turn in the rock; `flowstone(x0, x1, top, length)` a drapery (orange banding, wet sheen); `crack(pts, width, depth, branches)`; `floor(y, tilt)` the mud floor tilted toward the viewer; `floor_h(x, y)` the ground height
- Fire and props: `fire(x, y, size)` a campfire (a stone ring, ash, radiating logs, flame tongues, sparks, a smoke column + the main light); `lamp(x, y, size)` a red-sandstone fat lamp (bowl, handle, oil surface, a small wick flame + the second light); `stone(pts, colour, height)` slabs and stones; `heap(x, y, r, pigment)` a heap of ground pigment; `stick(p0, p1, width, colour, hollow, tip)` a charcoal stick / a hollow bone tube (`hollow=True`, `tip` the pigment at the mouth)
- Drafting (unit coordinates, facing 1 right / −1 left, size the body length): `bison(x, y, size, facing, head_drop)`, `horse(..., gait='run'|'gallop'|'stand')`, `deer(..., kind='stag'|'hind', gait='leap'|'stand')`, `hunter(x, sole y, height, facing, pose='throw'|'run')` (`anchors['grip']`, `['spear_dir']` for hanging the spear), `spear(p0, p1, width)`, `hand(x, y, size, rot, side, spread, fold=(3, 4))` (wrist as origin, fingers up; `fold` the bent fingers), `shape(...)` custom; returns a `Figure`: parts body / legs (below the body outline only) / tail / horns / antlers / mane / ears, regions head / hump / belly / back / front; `fig.sel('head', 'mane')` takes parts, `fig.anchors['eye']` etc.
- Colour (all accept a Figure or `fig.sel(...)`):
  - `sketch(fig, width, passes, strength)` charcoal searching lines (each pass along a slightly wandering contour);
  - `blow(t, pigment, density, edge, halo, speckle, rim, gradient, relief, clip, exclude)` blown paint: soft edges, halo, mist speckle, unevenness; `rim` denser near the outline, `gradient` darker along the back, `relief` denser on rock facing away from the fire (borrowing the relief), `clip` stays inside, `exclude` leaves pale bellies;
  - `daub(t, pigment)` padded daubs; `paint(fig, pigment, kind)` shapes and fills hunters and spears; `line(pts, width, pigment, kind='charcoal'|'finger'|'brush')`;
  - `outline(fig, pigment, width, top, gaps, kind='charcoal'|'blow')` the finishing edge: a band inside the contour, heavier along the back and top, thinner at the belly, with breaks;
  - `dots(pts, r, pigment)` palm dots; `stencil(hand, pigment, spread, puffs)` a blown negative hand; `handprint(hand, pigment)` a positive hand
- Pigments: from `PIGMENTS` — red hematite red ochre / darkred / orange / yellow goethite yellow ochre / brown / black charcoal black / manganese manganese black / white kaolin, or '#rrggbb'
- Age: `calcite(x, y, rx, ry, amount)` a milky calcite veil (over the painting); `flake(n, size, min_load)` scattered paint loss; `claws(x, y, angle, n, length, spread)` cave-bear claw marks (fresh pale grooves cutting through the paint); `soot(x, y, amount, drift, spread)` the campfire's smoke column and sooted ceiling
- Order: wall → boss / fold / flowstone / crack → floor → fire / lamp / props → sketch → red, yellow (blow / daub) → black (blow / outline / line) → hunters, hands, dots → calcite / flake / claws / soot
- `stage(name)` composites a snapshot immediately, `save(path, stages_dir)`; `save('x.jpg')` defaults to quality 88, 4:4:4 chroma

## 1-bit paint program — `MacPaint`
- Idea: not "pixel-art illustration" — the whole screen of a 1984-style black-and-white paint program. The 640×360 framebuffer has exactly two values, blown up ×3 nearest-neighbour to 1920×1080. Everything in the document bitmap `doc` (1 = black) follows the original tools' rules: 8×8 patterns tiled against the document origin (two fills of one pattern butt seamlessly and never bend with the shape); lines are square-pen Bresenham; shapes = pattern fill + a frame drawn inside the outline at the current width; the bucket is 4-connected flood fill; text with anti-aliasing off, styles as bitmap operations on the glyph mask. The interface is drawn only at render time, so every `stage()` can change tools, patterns, cursors and menus
- Construction: `MacPaint(seed, title='untitled', menus=(...), screen=(640, 360), scale=3, ink, paper, doc=None, scroll=(0, 0))`; the document origin is the top-left of the window's content area, 554×246 by default
- Patterns: `PATTERNS` holds 41 (white / black / gray 50% / light 25% / lighter 12.5% / mist / faint / dark / darker, dots, polka, hlines, vlines, diag, rdiag, thick_diag, grid, xhatch, bricks, scales, weave, basket, diamonds, shingles, waves, zigzag, stars, sprigs, checker, gingham, hstripes, vstripes, wood, grass, pebbles, circles ...), the pattern palette shows the `PALETTE` 38; `paint(mask, pattern, mode='opaque'|'or'|'erase'|'invert')`; `bands(mask, [patterns...], y0, y1)` a "gradient" of pattern bands with staggered, interleaved seams
- Shape tools (all return masks): `rect(x0, y0, x1, y1, fill, line, radius)`, `oval(cx, cy, rx, ry, fill, line)`, `shape(points, fill, line, smooth)` freeform / polygon; `line(p0, p1, width)`, `pencil(points, mode)`, `brush(points, size, shape='round'|'square'|'slash'|'backslash'|'bar'|'vbar'|'dot', pat)`, `spray(points, radius, density, pat, clip)`, `bucket(x, y, pattern)`, `dotted(points, gap, size)`; masks: `mask_rect / mask_oval / mask_poly / mask_path`, `border(mask, w)` an inner frame, `pen(mask, w)` square-pen thickening
- Editing: `copy(rect, to, flip, mask)` select + drag copy (`flip` mirrors left-right, `mask` acts as a lasso — the background does not follow), `invert(rect or mask)` XOR, `erase(rect or mask)`
- Text: `text(s, x, y, size, font='system'|core font style, style=('bold', 'italic', 'underline', 'outline', 'shadow'), anchor='l'|'m'|'r', opaque)`; 'system' is the built-in bold bitmap face (9 px capitals, 2 px stems, Latin letters, digits and a few symbols only), other fonts go through `core.load_font` with anti-aliasing off; outline / shadow are hollow glyphs
- Interface state (applies at the next `stage()`): `use(tool, pattern, line)` inverts the active tool (20 names in `MacPaint.TOOLS`), sets the current pattern and ticks the line width; `cursor(kind, x, y)` (arrow / cross / ibeam / bucket / spray / brush / pencil, kind=None hides it); `select(rect=...)` or `select(lasso=mask)` marching ants; `menu(name, items, checked, hilite, styled)` a pull-down menu, `styled=True` writing each item in its own style, `menu(None)` closes it; `zoom(x, y, w, h, at, fat)` the fat-bits window, `zoom(None)` closes it; `retitle(title)` renames the saved document
- `render()` returns the 640×360 0/1 screen, `image()` the upscaled PIL image; `stage(name)`, `save(path, stages_dir)` (JPG defaults to quality 88, saved as greyscale when purely black-and-white)
- GIFs: frames are pure black-and-white; scale to 720 wide with BOX and a fixed greyscale palette (not the default LANCZOS + median cut)

## Greek black-figure vase — `BlackFigure`
- Idea: the pot is thrown first — a profile r(y) plus two round handles — and everything is painted on the "unrolled surface" (the circumference × height arc); the same unrolled surface wraps onto the curved pot and lies flat as the rollout, and the two can never disagree. Workshop order: bands painted on the wheel → slip silhouettes → incision → added red and white → a three-stage firing (only now does the slip become glossy black) → ageing → lit like a museum photograph
- Construction: `BlackFigure(W, H, seed)`; `museum(wall, plinth, spot, plinth_x)` a dark wall, a spotlight, a pale plinth; `pot = throw(cx, foot, height, rmax=None, shape='neck_amphora', eye=0.42)`: `foot` is the screen y where the pot's foot lands on the plinth
- Coordinates on the pot: `y` is px down from the rim; inside a frieze, `x` is arc-length px at that band's radius, 0 at the left handle, the front (the visible half) 0..C/2 and the back C/2..C; `pot.TW` is the unrolled width (at the largest radius), `pot.radius(y)`
- Wheel work and ornament: `pot.paint_handles()`; `pot.band(y0, y1)` wheel-painted black bands (dead level, a small lap where the brush lifted); `pot.palmettes(y0, y1, n)` a palmette and lotus chain; `pot.tongues(y0, y1, n, red_every=2)` shoulder tongues; `pot.meander(y0, y1, n)` a key-fret band; `pot.rays(y0, y1, n)` foot rays
- Figures: `z = pot.zone(y0, y1)`, `z.C` the band's circumference; `z.quadriga(x, hw, white_horse, red_manes, bends)` a four-horse chariot (x = the nearest horse's girth, hw = the horses' height); `z.runner(x, height, phase, beard, back_arm)`; `z.column(x, height, red)` a Doric turning post; `z.judge(x, height, facing=-1, holds='wreath'|'rod')`; `z.tripod(x, height)` the prize tripod; `z.prize_table(x, height)` the table with the little amphora and the wreath; `z.bird(x, y, size, facing, flap)`; `z.letters(text, x, y, size, vertical, retro)` archaic Attic letters (fine-pen slip, may be written retrograde)
- New figures by hand: build a `blackfigure._Fig()`, call `slip(poly)` / `cut(pts, w)` / `red(poly)` / `white(poly)` / `reserve(poly)` / `line(pts, w)` far to near in local units (feet at (0, 0), facing +x, y down), then `z.emit(fig, X, G, scale, facing)`; widths are px (the incising tool and fine pen do not scale with the figure)
- Three passes: `pot.paint()` brushes the slip silhouettes (only newly declared parts); `pot.incise()` cuts the lines; `pot.add_colour()` adds red and white. The last two replay in declaration order: a nearer black silhouette covers a farther one's incisions and added colour, then cuts its own outline — four overlapping horses still read as four
- `pot.fire()` fires; `pot.age(mend=(u, v, rx, ry), misfire=(u, v, r), chips=7, roots=14)`: u, v are unrolled px (the front's centre at u = TW/4)
- Panel: `panel(box, title, subtitle, english, greek)`; `rollout(pot, bands, x, y, scale, half=0|1, caption, ticks)` unrolls the bands to their own circumferences into vertically centred strips (half 0 = front, 1 = back); `legend(x, y, [(label, 'clay'|'black'|'incision'|'red'|'white')])`
- `stage(name)` / `save(path, stages_dir)`; every stage re-lights and re-renders the pot as it stands (geometry computed once)

## Graphite pencil — `Graphite`
- Idea: graphite is a "mass field" m; the seen value is Dmax·(1 − e^−m), building up layer over layer but never reaching black; graphite is a cool slate, not black, and hard pressure polishes it lighter (`b`) with a faint silvery sheen in raking light. The pencil deposits only where its tip touches the paper: light pressure grazes the tooth's peaks (white specks in the grey), soft leads and hard pressure fill the valleys. Every stroke has a landing, a swell and a lift-off, and less and less is drawn toward the sheet's edges
- Construction: `Graphite(W, H, seed, ss=2)`; `paper(tone, tooth=1.6, felt, mottle)` drawing paper (a tooth height field, a slight relief under raking light, a light fall-off brighter upper-left)
- Pencil grades `GRADES`: '4H' 'H' 'HB' '2B' '4B' '6B' '8B', deciding each stroke's graphite mass, how deep it reaches into the tooth and its width; harder = lighter, thinner, whiter paper
- Masks and tone fields: `poly(pts)`, `ellipse(cx, cy, rx, ry, rot)`, `ring(cx, cy, r0, r1)`, `band(pts, width)` a heavy line (tubes, shafts), `lines_mask(lines, width)` a batch of fine lines, `rect()`; `linear(p0, p1)` a 0-to-1 ramp, `radial(cx, cy, r)`; `circle_pts(cx, cy, rx, ry, a0, a1, rot)` points on an elliptical arc (degrees, screen-clockwise)
- The finished zone: `focus(cx, cy, rx, ry, soft, rough)`: finished inside a ragged ellipse, tone outside fading by finish^0.6, hatching and side-shading dropped whole strokes at a time, lines paling, the edges left as bare drawing; any method with `finish=False` ignores it
- Construction lines: `guide(p0, p1, grade='2H', overshoot)` long guide lines overshooting at both ends (the horizon, perspective lines, plumb lines); `guide_ellipse(cx, cy, rx, ry, rot, loops=2)` a loose ellipse looped two or three times with its axes
- Lines: `line(pts, grade, pressure, closed, searching, lost, weight, fade=0.35)`: pressure drifting along the line; `lost` breaks the lit-side edges; `weight` is a field (e.g. the shaded side) — where it is high the line is darker and heavier; `searching` searches lightly a few times first; `strokes(lines, grade, pressure, width, taper, clip, fade)` a batch of free strokes (spokes, chain links, basket weaves)
- Hatching: `hatch(mask, tone, angle, grade, spacing, length, layers=(0, 0, 58, -38), pressure, zigzag, edge)`: tone 0 paper white .. 1 darkest (a number or a field); `layers` are each layer's angle offset — repeating one angle interleaves between that layer's lines, layer k only draws where tone > k/layer count, pressing harder the darker it gets; hatched patch by patch (each patch sharing a slightly drifting seam), a wrist arc bending every stroke the same way, heavy landing and flicked lift-off; `zigzag` continuous back-and-forth without lifting. `contour_hatch(lines, mask, tone, grade, pressure)` strokes that follow the form (round a tyre, along a pipe, along a loaf)
- Side of the lead: `shade(mask, tone, angle, grade='4B', width=9, pressure, passes=3)` the blunt soft lead's side, broad soft strokes overlapping in three passes, catching only the tooth peaks — the base tone before smudging
- Blending stump: `smudge(mask, amount, radius, direction, length, pickup, contain=True)` pushes graphite into the tooth's valleys and evens it sideways (drag along one direction if you like), grain disappearing; with `contain` it evens only inside the mask and never wipes blank paper in from outside
- Erasers: `lift(mask, amount)` a kneaded eraser pressing out soft lights; `erase(pts, width, amount, soft)` a hard eraser cutting crisp white lines along its edge (rims, tube highlights, lit corners, glass reflections), leaving a slight ghost
- Margins: `write(s, x, y, size, grade, pressure, font='hand')` pencil handwriting; `value_scale(x, y, w, h, grades)` tried swatches (one grade per cell, labelled below); `fingerprint(x, y, r, rot, amount)` a graphite fingerprint; `smear(pts, width, amount)` the heel of a hand dragged through
- `stage(name)`, `save(path, stages_dir)`; `save('x.jpg')` defaults to quality 88, 4:4:4 chroma; colour constants `PAPER GRAPHITE SHEEN`

## X-ray — `XRay`
- Idea: never draw what an object looks like; build "how much of what material lies along the beam". Every object adds μ(material, energy) × density × path length to two attenuation maps (dual energy: low about 60 keV, high about 120 keV); there is no front-to-back occlusion, overlaps simply add; Beer's law I = e^(−A) then shoots the beam through, the result passes through the detector (scatter haze, focal-spot blur, per-row gain streaks and module seams every 64 rows, photon noise), and finally into a display (film: denser = brighter; or security pseudo-colour: Zeff sorts organic orange / inorganic green / metal blue / opaque black). Outlines glow for a physical reason: a hollow shell's side walls are crossed lengthwise at the outline, the longest path
- Construction: `XRay(W, H, px_cm=17, seed)`; all lengths are canvas pixels, including depth along the beam; `px_cm` sets the physical scale. The material table `MATERIALS`: organic, water, rubber, pvc, pcb, magnesium, mica, glass, aluminium, bone, plaster, ferrite, zinc, steel, brass, copper, tin, lead
- Outlines: `rrect(cx, cy, w, h, r, rot)`, `ellipse(cx, cy, rx, ry, rot)`, `poly(pts, cx, cy, rot, scale)` (local coordinates carried to the canvas), `closed(ctrl)` a closed spline, `path(ctrl)` an open spline (points may carry a third depth z), `offset(P, d)` a side shift
- Objects:
  - `slab(outline, material, depth, edge, holes, density, tag, texture)`: solid, edges rounded by `edge` radius (default depth/2, pebble-like; 0 is a sawn edge); `holes` punched through; `density` may be negative (milled slots on a key)
  - `shell(outline, material, depth, wall, edge)`: a hollow shell (a case, a hairdryer body, a camera body), glowing at the outline and pale inside
  - `rod(P, material, r, wall, ribs, flat)`: a round wire / rod along a path, `r` may taper point to point; `wall` makes it a tube (bright both walls, dark between); with z in P, the segments diving along the beam read brighter; `ribs=(amplitude, period)` threads, a double helix, stranded twist; `flat` a flat strap (may be a function of arc length). `tube(P, m, r, wall)` the same
  - `coil(p0, p1, R, turns, material, r_wire)` a spring / heating element (side-on: turn points bright, the near and far halves zigzag); `edgeon(p0, p1, thick, material)` a disc seen edge-on (fans, grilles, filters); `disc(cx, cy, r, material, depth, hole, rim, dome)` a disc face-on (a coin with a rim, washers, a lens with dome>0 thicker in the middle, a lens barrel face-on all wall); `ball()` a ball bearing
  - `zipper(P, material, tooth, pitch, depth, tape)`, `screw(p0, p1, r, head, pitch)`, `cable(P, r, cores, core_r)`
  - `fabric(outline, depth, layers, sheet, density, weave, scale, angle, creases)`: folded clothes, fold edges turning over as soft bright rims; `weave('plain' | 'twill' | 'knit', scale, angle, amount)` can also be given alone to `slab(texture=)`
  - `belt(y0, y1, lacing=x)`: the conveyor belt (lengthwise cord lines), steel side rails and bolts, a steel hook joint; its attenuation is recorded as the air calibration and subtracted automatically in the pseudo-colour view
- Detector and displays: `scan(kv, ma, flux=(low, high photon counts), scatter, scatter_px, focal, streak, module)`; `film(a0, amax, mix, edge=(fine, heavy), glow, vignette, stops)`; `detect()` → (A'_lo, A'_hi), `show_film(Lo, Hi)`, `show_material(Lo, Hi)`, `zeff(Lo, Hi)` usable separately
- OSD: `status(left_lines, right_lines)` the top bar; `footer(left, right)` the bottom bar ((text, colour), amber / green / red getting a backing block automatically); `flag(x0, y0, x1, y1, n, label, note, side)` numbered alert boxes; `panel(x0, y0, x1, y1, title)`; `inset(src, dst, 'material' | 'film', title)` an inset of any region; `legend(x, y, w)`; `scale(x0, y0, x1, y1, [(label, material, thickness px)], title)` an attenuation scale (ticks computed on the same display curve); `text()`, `line(pts, dash=)`
- Readings: tag an object `tag='name'`, then write `{name.z:.1f}` (Zeff), `{name.ml:.0f}` (volume in water-equivalent ml), `{name.cm2}` in OSD text; measured at save time off that stage's detector image (background subtracted in a ring outside the object), so the numbers always agree with the picture; `x.readings` keeps the last readings
- `stage(name)` stores the two attenuation maps as float16 plus the OSD count; `save(path, stages_dir)` renders each stage with the same noise, so only what was newly added changes between stages; `save('x.jpg')` defaults to quality 88, 4:4:4 chroma

## 1930s rubber hose — `RubberHose`
- Idea: the way a 1930s studio made one frame: the background artist paints the background once on board in grey gouache (flats + dry-brush streaks, airbrushed pools of light, contact shadows, a thin grey outline); the animator roughs the actors in pencil (two passes slightly offset, with construction circles, cross lines and action lines); the inker traces them onto cels in heavy, even black with slight pressure drift; the painter fills a few flat greys on the back (white, light, mid, dark, black — no shading on cels); stacked over the background, shot on black-and-white film, printed and projected many times. Cels are opaque and painted back to front, later covering earlier; background parts a character must pass "behind" (the near hills outside the window, the window bar, curtains, a blanket) are stored as clip masks
- Construction: `RubberHose(W, H, seed, ss=2, ink_w=5.5)` (2× supersampled inside); grey constants `WHITE LIGHT PALE MID DARK BLACK INK`
- Background (painted on board): `wash(pts, value, value2, axis, tex, line, dark)` flat / gradient gouache + a thin grey contour; `blob(circles, value)` a union of circles (clouds, canopies), only the outer edge outlined; `line(pts, width, dark)`; `airbrush(cx, cy, rx, ry, amount)` pools of light (positive brightens, negative darkens); `light(pts, amount, soft)` soft-edged light patches (sunlight on the floor, the shadow of a window bar); `pattern(pts, draw_fn, dark)` little motifs (wallpaper, polka dots; dark < 0 lightens)
- Room: `wall(y1)` gradient wallpaper + stripes + sprigged flowers + a skirting board; `floor(y0, board, vp)` a perspective floor, seams, wood grain; `window(x0, y0, x1, y1, bars='sash'|'cross', horizon, valley)` the morning outside (sky, clouds, far hills, trees, the near hills; the near hills and the window bar go into the 'window' clip), returns geometry; `curtain(...)`, `valance(...)` curtains and a valance (also subtracted from the clip); `nightstand(x0, x1, top, bottom, floor_y)` returns the top's y; `bed(x0, x1, head_top, sheet_y, front_y, floor_y)` the bed from the foot (an arched headboard, ball finials, a quilted blanket, a hanging edge; the blanket goes into the 'bed' clip); `rug(cx, cy, rx, ry)` a braided rug; `sampler(x0, y0, x1, y1, lines)` a framed motto on the wall
- Clips: `clip(name, add, sub, full)` builds a mask; character methods take `clip=`, or `using(name)` ... `using(None)` puts subsequent cels into a clip
- Cel basics: `shape(pts, fill, ow)` (ink only outside the fill; `fill=None` outline only); `union(subs, fill, ow)` pieces joined with no line between (`('poly', pts)` / `('cap', pts, r)`); `hose(pts, width)` constant-width hose with no elbows or knees; `stroke(pts, width, taper)`; `text(s, x, y, size, fill, ow, rot)`; `shadow(cx, cy, rx, ry, amount)` the grey shadow under a character's feet; `guide(pts)` construction lines that exist only in the pencil stage
- Rubber-hose vocabulary: `hose_path(p0, p1, bend, bend2)` C- or S-shaped limbs; `glove(x, y, angle, size, pose='open'|'point'|'fist'|'palm', flip)` a four-fingered white glove (three stitch lines, a flared cuff; (x, y) is the wrist, `angle` the finger direction); `shoe(x, y, size, facing, tilt)` big round shoes (a white highlight, a grey sole); `pie_eye(cx, cy, rx, ry, look, wedge, lid)` a white eye ring + a black pupil with a pie-cut wedge, `lid` a half-closed lid; `grin(F, x0, x1, y_top, y_bot)` a wide grin with a tongue; `star()`; `Frame(x, y, s, rot, flip)` local coordinates
- Characters: `alarm_clock(cx, cy, r, tilt, look, time=(7, 0), arms=[...], legs=[...])` a twin-bell alarm clock (a black case, a face on the white dial, hands, two bells and swinging strikers; arms / legs give wrist / ankle positions with bend, pose, facing, tilt), returns a local Frame; `rh.bells` the two bells' positions; `sun(cx, cy, r, arms, ray_style='wavy'|'spiky', clip)` a yawning, half-awake sun stretching; `pillow(cx, cy, w, h, arm, clip)` a snoring pillow in a striped night-cap holding up a glove; `slipper(x, y, size, facing, tilt)` a dancing slipper
- Effects: `vibration(cx, cy, r, a0, a1, n)` concentric vibration arcs; `speed_lines([pts])`; `lettering(s, path, size)` bouncy heavy serif letters along a curve (white with black rims, odd and even letters tilted oppositely, staggered up and down); `zzz(x, y, size)`; `slab_font(size)` finds heavy serif fonts like Superclarendon / Rockwell (overridable with `INKPAINT_FONT_SLAB`)
- Order and snapshots: background → `stage('layout', view='layout')` (the background's pencil layout on paper) → `stage('background', view='background')` → draw characters (pencil at this point) → `stage('pencil')` → `ink()` → `stage('ink')` → `paint()` → `stage('paint')` → effects → `film(grain, soft, halation, tone='silver'|'sepia'|'neutral', vignette, weave, gate)` → `age(scratches, dust, specks, fibres, hair, stains, flicker)` → `save(path, stages_dir)`; `save('x.jpg')` defaults to quality 88, 4:4:4 chroma

## Linocut — `Linocut`
- Idea: subtraction. The whole block rolls up with ink and prints as one solid black; every white in the picture is lino a gouge took away. Each block is a relief map at ss× resolution (255 = uncut and inking, 0 = cut away): the black block starts full, a colour block starts cleared and only "keeps" the shapes that print its colour; the black block's outer rim is never cut, printing as the slightly rippling black frame of a hand-cut block. Every cut is a double-tapered polygon with a crumbly edge; tone is never grey, only the density and width of cuts (white lines cut in black, or black lines left standing in white). Printing is simulated as a real hand rub: the brayer film uneven with stop-lines, a wooden baren's circular pressure; where ink × pressure cannot beat the paper's tooth the print goes pale (big solids "salt"), ink squeezes to shape edges so edges stay solid; the colour block prints first, the black over it a few px out of register; the paper is pressed into the block, cut areas and the margin rising slightly
- Construction: `Linocut(W, H, block=(x0, y0, x1, y1), seed, paper, ink, ss=2, rim=7)`; `colour_block(colour, offset=(dx, dy), mottle)` adds a spot block (the colour written as it prints on this paper), returns a `Block`; tools take `on=` to choose which block (the black block by default)
- Masks: `poly(pts, smooth)`, `disc(cx, cy, r)`, `rect()` give 1× float masks (numpy add and subtract fine); region arguments may also be polygon points directly; `mask_of(polys)` turns a set of cut polygons into a mask
- One cut: `stroke(pts, width, tool, taper, end)` returns the cut polygon; `gouge(pts, width, tool='v'|'u'|'knife'|'brush', cut=True, on, region)`: v is a V-gouge (dips to a point, widens, lifts to a point), u a U-gouge (round head, crumbly tail), knife an even-width knife, brush a width running linearly from `width` to `end` (branches, left-standing lines); `cut=False` leaves one standing (a black line in white); `jab(x, y, size)` a U-gouge poke
- Clearing: `clear(shape, chatter, angle)` clears an area with a big U-gouge, leaving chatter ridges along the cutting direction (more near the edges); `leave(shape)` keeps an area; `hatch(region, angle, spacing, width, dash, gap, tool, cut, density, bend)` rows of parallel cuts: `density(x, y)` decides whether a cut survives and how wide (this is the tone), `bend(x, y)` bends the rows (drifting snow, contours, cloth folds); `flow(region, field, n, length, width)` long cuts along a field field(x, y) → angle (wind, water, fur); `speckle(region, n, size, stars)` falling snow: U-gouge pokes plus a few three-cut six-point flakes, cut only where ink remains; `rings(cx, cy, radii, width, arc, gap, cut, squash)` broken concentric rings (a moon halo, tree rings); `outline(shape, width, cut=True)` cuts a white rim outside a shape so a black thing can separate from a black ground (`cut=False` leaves a black rim, a white thing crossing white ground)
- Motifs: `moon(cx, cy, r, rings)`; `pine(x, y, h, w, tiers, snow, halo, nicks, region)` a snow-covered spruce; `bare_tree(x, y, h, lean, spread, width, depth, halo, snow, seed)` a bare tree (returns a mask); `cottage(x, base, fw, wall, roof, side, rise, windows, side_windows, door=(u, w, h, open), chimney=(position, width, height), lit=colour block, attic)` a snow-covered cottage, returns `{'apex', 'windows', 'door', 'chimney'}`; `smoke(x, y, rise, drift, width)` a string of curling smoke puffs; `footprints(path, step, size, side, start, stop, paws)` footprints / paw prints
- Printing: `pull(blocks)` prints a PIL image; `sign(edition, title, name, y)` pencil in the margin: edition left, title centre, signature right; `chop(x, y, size)` a blind embossed chop
- `stage(name, blocks=None)` snapshots (`blocks=[]` is blank paper, `blocks=[L.key]` prints the black block only); `save(path, stages_dir)` defaults to quality 88, 4:4:4 chroma
- Order: like the painter's algorithm, later cuts cover: clear the big whites first (hills, snow fields, the moon) → sky cuts → distance → houses → snow field → near figures → falling snow → the colour block → pencil

## VHS home video — `VHSCamcorder`
- Idea: not a retro filter laid over a picture (that is how Synthwave does its tape look), but the three real steps a home-video frame went through, every blemish coming from one of them:
  1. The room: colour goes into the albedo buffer; lamp shades, candle flames, the sky outside the window go into the emission buffer; at composite time albedo is multiplied by the room's light (warm ambient + point lights + painted light patches) and emission added; things too close to the lens are drawn on a defocus layer
  2. The camcorder: hand-held tilt and zoom, white balance stuck on 'outdoor' under tungsten light, a soft highlight knee, bloom, vertical CCD smear, lag tails, lens softness, vignette, gain noise in the shadows; the camera's character generator mixes the OSD into the signal here, so the OSD goes onto tape and blurs with it
  3. The tape: down to 480 lines × 760 samples, split into luma Y and chroma I/Q; luma low-passed with a playback sharpness halo (one side bright, one dark), chroma low-passed about 6× wider and averaged over two lines, delayed rightward (reds bleed right); playback adds streaky luma noise, lateral chroma noise, per-line timebase jitter, flagging at the top, a tracking noise band, head-switching noise, dropout lines, a lifted black level, finally upscaled to the output size
- Construction: `VHSCamcorder(W, H, seed, lines=480, samples=760, ss=2, keep_stages=True)`; with `keep_stages=False`, `stage()` writes no snapshots (faster)
- Shapes → masks: `poly(pts)`, `shape(pts)` a closed spline, `rect(x0, y0, x1, y1, r)`, `ellipse(cx, cy, rx, ry, rot)`, `line(pts, width)`, `text(s, x, y, size, style, anchor, rot)`; colour fields `lin(p0, c0, p1, c1)`, `rad(cx, cy, rx, ry, c0, c1)`
- Paint and light:
  - `paint(mask, colour, alpha, form, form_r, light)`: `colour` may be a colour field; `form > 0` rounds the shape (edges dark, the side facing the light bright)
  - `emit(mask, colour, level)` emission (unaffected by room light); `glow(mask, colour, level)` a light patch falling on a surface (multiplied by albedo); `shadow(mask, strength, soft, dx, dy)`; `rim(mask, toward, colour, level, width)` a rim on the side toward the light
  - `ambient(colour, level)`, `light(x, y, radius, colour, power)` a point light with 1 / (1 + (d/r)²) falloff
  - `with v.layer(defocus=4.5): ...` everything drawn inside is blurred (foreground too close to the lens)
- A 1970s living room: `wallpaper(mask, tile, contrast)` ring wallpaper with sheet seams, `paneling(mask, grooves)` walnut panelling, `ceiling()` a sprayed ceiling, `shag()` shag carpet, `window(x0, y0, x1, y1)` a dusk window (a silhouette, a lit window across the street), `rod()` + `curtain(x0, x1, y0, y1, folds)` flowered drapes, `console_tv()` a console TV cabinet, `rabbit_ears()`, `floor_lamp(x, y_floor, y_shade)` (with its own light and the scallops on the wall), `sofa(x0, x1, y_back, y_seat, y_front, y_floor, colour)`
