- The party: `streamer(p0, p1, sag, colour, twists)` twisted crepe streamers, `pennants(p0, p1, sag, letters, colours, size)` lettered pennants (pale flags automatically get dark letters), `balloon(x, y, r, colour, string_to)`, `coffee_table(quad, thick, legs, gloss)`, `cake(cx, cy, rx, ry, h, candles, sprinkles, lean)` (candles = [(angle, colour, 'lit' or a 0–1 smoke amount)], returns flame positions, one `light` each), `smoke()`, `present()`, `cup()`, `plates()`, `blower()`, `confetti()`, `teddy()`; `child_back(hx, hy, s, lean, glow_from)` the child from behind (a party hat, a hair whorl, ears, elbows), drawn in the defocus layer
- The three-stage switches (apply to every stage after they are called):
  - `camera(tilt, zoom, white_balance, exposure, knee, bloom, smear, lag, soft, vignette, gain_noise)`: `white_balance` is RGB gain, default (1.08, 0.94, 0.70) = 'outdoor under tungsten'; `smear` only affects point lights far over full well; `lag = (dx, dy, strength)` is the trail behind highlights
  - `record(luma, chroma, chroma_v, delay, sharpen, sharp_r, saturation)`: luma / chroma bandwidth (sample-space sigma), the chroma delay, the sharpening halo
  - `wear(noise, chroma_noise, jitter, flagging, tracking=(centre fraction, height fraction), head_switch, dropouts, black, lines)`
- OSD (bitmap letters with black rims, mixed into the signal and onto the tape): `rec(x, y)` a red dot + REC, `battery(x, y, level, of)`, `counter(s, x, y)`, `datestamp(time, date, x, y)`, `zoom_bar(x, y, pos, width)`, `osd_text(s, x, y, px, colour, anchor)`; the font holds capitals, digits and `: . - / >` only
- `stage(name)` stores a full render of the current state; `save(path, stages_dir)`; `save('x.jpg')` defaults to quality 88, 4:4:4 chroma

## Flat vector — `FlatVector`
- Idea: a vector app's artboard, every thing a layer. Edges crisp, no outlines; seven flat hues, every other colour a `tone` or `mix` of them; one light for the whole picture, so everything splits into a lit face and a shade face; each lifted layer first casts a short, soft night-blue shadow on what is there (the "vector paper" depth cue), and a faint fibre and a fixed fine grain are added last (the same grain on stage snapshots, so GIF diffs stay small)
- Construction: `FlatVector(W, H, seed, light=(-0.7, -0.7))`, `light` the screen direction toward the light
- Shapes: all return a `Mask`, combinable with `|` union, `&` intersection, `-` difference, `moved(dx, dy)`, `scaled(k)`, computed only over their bounding box. There are `circle`, `ellipse(cx, cy, rx, ry, rot)`, `rect(x0, y0, x1, y1, round)`, `poly(pts, round=a radius or per-corner radii)`, `capsule(p0, p1, r0, r1)`, `star(cx, cy, r_out, r_in, n)`, `teardrop(x, bottom, r, h, lean)` flame / drop shapes, `profile(pts, bottom)` a land shape (spline top filled down); `half(m, p0, p1)` takes the right side of a directed line
- Colour:
  - `backdrop(colour)`;
  - `fill(m, colour, alpha, lift, texture, shadow)`: with `lift > 0` the inter-layer shadow is cast first (down 0.45 × lift, blurred 0.9 × lift, tinted night-blue-violet), then the colour;
  - `face(m, p0, p1, colour)`: the right side of the line becomes the shade colour;
  - `glow(cx, cy, rx, ry, colour, rings, alpha)`: stacked flat translucent rings;
  - `drop(m, lift, strength)`: the shadow only, no fill;
  - `tone(c, k)`: k < 0 toward night-blue-black, k > 0 toward cream; `mix(a, b, t)`
- Sky: `moon(cx, cy, r, rays, ray_colour, ray_len, halo, craters)`, alternating long and short triangular rays round the moon plus two stepped halos; `stars(n, box, avoid=[(x, y, r)])`; `sparkle(x, y, r)` a four-point star; `shooting_star(head, tail, r)`; `cloud(cx, cy, w, colour, belly)` a flat-bottomed cloud
- Terrain:
  - `mountain(peak, left, right, colour, snow, ridge, lift, shade)`: a kinked ridge splitting lit and shade faces, a jagged snow cap; returns `dict(body, shade, snow)`;
  - `land(pts, colour, lift, shadow)`: one layer of hills or sand;
  - `pine(x, base, h, colour, tiers, width)` stacked triangles split down the middle into lit and shade; `round_tree(x, base, r, colour, fruit)`;
  - `dashes(box, colours, n, length, thick, horizon)`: short dark and pale dashes on the sand, longer nearer; `pebble(x, y, r, colour)`, `tuft(x, y, h)`
- Story props:
  - `trail(pts, colour, width, dash, gap)`: a dashed path thicker downward, returns a mask — `& peak['snow']` to recolour it;
  - `flag(x, y, h)`, `tent(x, base, w, h, colour, depth, inside)`: a lit front face, a shaded side and roof, a glowing doorway, a lifted flap, guy ropes and pegs;
  - `campfire(x, base, s)`; `hiker(x, base, h, facing, lamp)`: a faceless little silhouette with a pack, a walking stick and a head-torch beam
- Palette constants `NIGHT PLUM BERRY EMBER SAND PINE CREAM`; `stage(name)`, `save(path, stages_dir)`; `save('x.jpg')` defaults to quality 88, 4:4:4 chroma

## Storybook animation — `Storybook`
- Idea: an animator's shot, not a painting. Everything is a function of time t (seconds): the same scene gives either a real per-frame animation (`frame(t)`, `gif()`) or one "journey poster" (`journey()`: the world fully painted, the character multiply-exposed at key poses with a pencil motion arc). Everything in the scene is pre-painted into its own clipped layer with a reveal map: at what moment in that layer's own drawing process each pixel was reached by brush or pen — ink lines reveal along arc length (the pen is moving), watercolour spreads from a point or sweeps across (with a slightly darker wet front), small props pop in scaled (0 → 1.1 → 1). A layer's progress comes from a time window `when=(t0, t1)`, or `follow=lead`: paint up to `lead` px ahead of the character's furthest reached x, so hillsides, flowers and banks are drawn exactly as the character passes. The character is rendered every frame from implicit shapes in its own local coordinates: a volume-preserving affine squash & stretch along the axis (k along, 1/√k across) then rolled; `rest()` puts the lowest point exactly on the ground; lighting is fixed in the world (the highlight does not rotate as it rolls); the higher off the ground, the smaller and fainter the contact shadow
- Construction: `Storybook(W, H, seed, actor_size=44)`: all coordinates are given in 1920×1080 design coordinates, output at any size (the 720×405 GIF and the 1920×1080 poster are two instances of the same scene)
- Choreography: `act(t0, t1, fn)`, fn(u, t) → a pose dict or None (hidden). Pose keys: `x, y` (the acorn's centre), `rot` (degrees, counter-clockwise), `k` (>1 stretched, <1 squashed), `axis` (the stretch direction, degrees), `scale`, `eyes` (0 closed .. 1 open), `lid` ('blink' / 'sleep' / 'happy' closed-eye shapes), `look=(dx, dy)`, `brow` ('determined' / 'up' / 'worried'), `mouth` ('smile' / 'grin' / 'o' / 'flat'), `blush`, `shadow=(gx, gy, width, density)`, `clip` (a ground y; below it not drawn — digging into soil), `only='cap'` (draw only the cap: on top of the seedling), `lead=False` (does not push follow layers), `speed=False` / `speed_min` (automatic speed lines). Helpers: `rest(gx, gy, rot, k, axis, normal)` stands the character on a ground point; `anchor(px, py, local, rot, scale)` puts a local point (the stem tip by default) on a point (hanging from a twig); `ease(u, kind)` (in / out / inout / sine / back with overshoot / elastic), `spring(u, cycles, damp)` a damped wobble, `arc(p0, p1, height, u)` a parabolic hop; `pose(t)`, `lead_x(t)`, `when_at(x, lead, dur)` (a time window starting when the character reaches x)
- Scene: `paper()`; `wash(pts, colour, reveal='x'|'-x'|'radial'|'pop', origin, dashes)`; `ink(pts, width)` / `inks(paths)` fine navy ink lines (pressure lift-off, slight hand wobble); `ground(pts, ticks=True)` a horizon with "v" grass ticks; `land(top, colour, left_edge, right_edge, dashes)` a hillside (a spline top line, a ragged cliff edge, crayon ticks); `hill(top, colour, outline)` far hills (only a thin grey line at the top); `tree(x, base, top, canopy, branch)` an autumn oak; `sun(cx, cy, r)` (a disc popping in, its rays drawn one by one); `cloud(cx, cy, w)` an impasto white cloud with a lavender shadow; `water(x0, x1, y)` a stream; `shape(polys, fill, stroke, pop)` little props with fine ink edges; `flower(x, y, h, kind)`, `tuft`, `cattail`, `leaf_on_ground`, `mound` (a soil mound drawn over the character, able to bury it). Common timing arguments: `when`, `follow`, `pre=(t0, t1, upto)` (a stretch of a follow layer left of the character painted by time), `xa / xb` (follow's start and end x), `z`
- Props: `sprite(polys, fill, veins=)` pre-paints a prop; `prop(sprite, t0, t1, fn)`, fn → dict(x, y, rot, sx, sy, alpha, ghost), affined on every frame (a falling oak leaf uses sx to fake flipping, lying flat with sy=0.5 once in the water); `oak_leaf(length, width, lobes, base, angle)` a module function giving an oak-leaf outline
- Effects: `fx(kind, t0, x, y, ...)`: 'impact' impact lines, 'dust' white dust puffs, 'ripple' water rings, 'specks' soil grains, 'sparkle' four-point stars, 'confetti' confetti; speed lines are added automatically by speed
- Handwriting: `write(text, x, y, size, when, underline=((x0, x1), y), weight)`: the text mask is thinned to a one-pixel skeleton by Zhang–Suen, then walked like a hand (starting at the leftmost endpoint after de-italics, going straight through junctions where possible, lifting and jumping at the end), each ink pixel timed by its nearest skeleton point
- Output: `frame(t)`; `gif(path, fps=15, t_end, hold)`: rendered frame by frame (one float frame kept at a time), sharing one weighted palette + a fixed 4×4 Bayer, unchanged pixels written as a magenta transparency index; `journey(times, t_world, arc, labels, prop_arcs, world_fx, final, props)` the journey poster; `stage(name, t)` stores a key frame at time t, `save(path, stages_dir)`; `save('x.jpg')` defaults to quality 88, 4:4:4 chroma
- Palette constants `PAPER INK PENCIL GRASS MINT LAVENDER SUN WATER NUT CAP CONFETTI`

## Ballpoint doodle — `Ballpoint`
- Idea: ballpoint ink is an oil paste laid by a rolling ball; colour stacks by optical density (Beer's law), so a line drawn twice, a crossing or a blob goes darker and more saturated (blue deepening to blue-violet, never dead black). Each stroke: the first few px the ball is dry (thin, pale, catching only the tooth peaks) → pressure drifting ±12 % with the width following, hand wobble → occasional skips of 2–9 px (the ink breaks, the groove remains) → at stops and sharp turns, sometimes a gloop at the landing → a 90–160° flicked hook at lift-off. Every stroke presses a groove into the paper; under an upper-left light the groove's upper-left wall is dark and lower-right bright. Coloured pencil is wax catching only the tooth it touches: light pressure grainy and pale, heavy pressure filling the valleys; hatched in quick slanted patches, overshooting, changing direction, light on big areas and pressed hard on small ones
- Construction: `Ballpoint(W, H, seed, ss=2, pen_width=2.5)`; `paper(tone, tooth=0.75, fibres=260, mottle, cockle=1.0, folds=[((x0, y0), (x1, y1))], vignette=0.09)` a grey-beige copy paper: fine tooth, sparse fibres, mottling, a low cockle and folds (lit by an upper-left light), photographic corner vignette
- Ballpoint: `line(pts, pen='blue'|'red'|'black'|None, pressure, width, closed, smooth, wobble, bow, hook, gloop, skip, twice, start, clip, groove)`; `bow` is a freehand straight line's side bow (about 0.003–0.006 of its length), `hook` / `gloop` / `skip` the probabilities of the lift-off hook, gloops and skips, `twice` the chance of going over again (offset 0.6–1.6 px, only partly), `pen=None` presses a groove with no ink; `lines(paths, ...)` a batch of short strokes sharing one rasterisation; `emboss(pts)` a blind indentation
- Little pieces: `dot(x, y, r, pen)` a solid dot circled in; `eye(cx, cy, rx, ry, pen, look, shine)` an eye circled in from outside, leaving one round highlight; `face(cx, cy, size, mood='smile'|'grin'|'oh'|'dizzy'|'sleep'|'worry', pen, blush, look, rot)`; `dashes(pts, pen, dash, gap, pressure, smooth, clip)` dashes (returns the resampled path, for adding an arrow); `arrow(tip, direction, size, pen)`; `ring(cx, cy, rx, ry, pen, turns, rot)` a casual ring, unclosed and drifting outward
- Coloured pencil: `pencil(mask, colour, angle, pressure, spacing, length=(40, 90), overshoot, width=2.9, jitter, bow, cross, clip, groove)` slanted hatching (`length` one wrist's stroke, seams staggered row by row; `overshoot` overshoot px; `cross` a second layer at another angle); `pencil_line(pts, colour, pressure, width)` one pencil line; colours from `PENCILS` (leaf green lime mint teal sky blue violet lilac pink rose red orange peach yellow ochre brown tan grey cream) or '#rrggbb'
- Ballpoint hatching: `pen_hatch(mask, pen, angle, spacing, pressure, length, overshoot, zigzag=0.85, cross, clip)` a continuous zigzag without lifting, gloops at the turns (shadows, shaded sides, dark chess pieces)
- Handwriting: `write(s, x, y, size, pen, pressure, slant=7, spacing=1.04, jitter, anchor='l'|'m'|'r', rot, width, blind, hook, gloop)`, y is the baseline, returns the written (x0, y0, x1, y1); glyphs come from a thin hand-font skeleton (Zhang-Suen thinned and traced to pen paths, cached per letter), each letter scaled, tilted and floating separately, with touch-downs, gloops and hooks; CJK uses a thin hei skeleton; `blind=True` is the inkless indentation pressed through from the page above; `strike(box, pen, passes)` scribbles a word out; `underline(x0, x1, y, pen, wavy)`
- Masks and points: `poly(pts)`, `ellipse(cx, cy, rx, ry, rot)`, `band(pts, width)`, `tube(pts, w0, w1)` a tapering body (snakes, tails), `Ballpoint.tube_sides(pts, w0, w1)` its two sides (one stroke down, around the tail tip and back); `circle_pts`, `star_pts`, `cloud_pts(cx, cy, w, h, bumps, seed, flat)`, `spiral_pts(cx, cy, r, turns, a0)`, `heart_pts`, `Ballpoint.resample(pts, step)`
- `stage(name)`, `save(path, stages_dir)`; `save('x.jpg')` defaults to quality 88, 4:4:4; constants `PENS PENCILS PAPER`; fonts overridable with `INKPAINT_FONT_BALLPOINT` / `INKPAINT_FONT_BALLPOINT_CJK` (defaults: macOS Noteworthy Light / PingFang Light, Linux Kalam / Caveat / Noto Sans CJK, Windows Segoe Print / Microsoft YaHei)

## Overlay sheets — `Overlay`
- Idea: separations on a light box. The light box glows warm white from below and the picture is one chain of transmittances multiplied: the base sheet (bond paper) printed in black line and grey tints, then translucent sheets each printed in ONE ink. An ink's transmittance comes from its screen coverage c (c ≤ 1 a flat screen 1 − c(1 − ink), c > 1 a deepening double hit ink^(c−1)); different sheets multiply together — blue over yellow green, magenta over yellow red, blue over magenta violet; one plate does not stack with itself (the maximum coverage wins). Every sheet has its own paper tint, cloudy fibre formation visible only against the light, stray fibres, a thin dark cut edge and punch holes; tracing paper frosts the darks beneath it slightly even lying flat. A lifted sheet is a little larger, casts a wide soft warm shadow to the lower right (visible through the paper), and being a diffuser off the picture it blurs everything beneath; laid down it is crisp, the shadow a thin contact line, registration crosses landing on the base sheet's crosses
- Construction: `Overlay(W, H, seed, keep_stages=True)`; `desk(colour)`, `lightbox(x0, y0, x1, y1, radius, bezel)`, `lamp(level)` (0 off: dull grey acrylic; 1 on: warm white, brightest a little left of centre; in between dim and warm); `peg_bar(x0, x1, y0, y1, pegs=[('round', x, y, r), ('slot', x, y, w, h)])` a steel peg bar (opaque, drawn under all sheets, pegs and rings of light showing through the punched holes)
- Sheets: `sheet(x, y, w, h, kind='base'|'tracing'|'drafting'|'film', ink, tint, fibre, curl=('br', 70), edge)` returns a `Sheet`. Coordinates are always the canvas coordinates of the sheet lying down, so several sheets drawn with the same points register automatically. `place(sheet, lift, dx, dy, rot)` shows it, lifts it (lift 0–1, offset dx / dy px, rotated rot degrees) or lays it down (all 0); the first `place` fixes the stacking order; `remove(sheet)`; `tape(cx, cy, w, h, rot)` crepe drafting tape with torn ends, pressed on top, translucent
- Printing on a sheet: `fill(mask or polygons, cover, ink)`; `line(pts, width, cover, ink, closed, smooth, dash=(on, off))`, `lines(paths, ...)` a batch of lines rasterised at once; `dots`, `circle(cx, cy, r, width=None)`, `hatch(mask, spacing, angle)`, `stipple(mask, n, r)`; `text(s, x, y, size, font_kind, cover, ink, anchor, spacing, rot, halo, knock)`: `halo` clears the ink around the letters first, `knock` knocks them out of ink (a stop number in a solid disc); `knock(mask)`, `cross(x, y)` a registration cross, `arrow(tip, direction, size)`, `punch(x, y, r)` / `punch_slot(x, y, w, h)` punch holes
- Masks (canvas sized): `poly(pts, smooth)`, `circle`, `ellipse`, `rrect`, `band(pts, width)`; `edge(mask, width)` a contour line, `offset(mask, d)` grow / shrink, `contour(mask, d, width)` a ring at d px from the edge (a coast's waterline), `Overlay.inset(convex polygon, d)` blocks inset from streets, `circle_pts`
- Fonts `font(kind, size)`: map / map_bold / map_heavy / map_italic / map_light (a Gill Sans family), title (Avenir Next Heavy), label, cjk / cjk_light (hei), core style names accepted; overridable with `INKPAINT_FONT_OVERLAY_<KIND>`
- Constants `INKS` (black blue yellow magenta cyan orange green red), `PAPERS`; `stage(name)`, `save(path, stages_dir)`; `save('x.jpg')` defaults to quality 88, 4:4:4 chroma

## Pastel UI — `PastelUI`
- Idea: one frame of a product launch film. A flat pastel field (blue / pink / yellow / lilac / mint / peach, `FIELDS`) with one big pool of soft light and a few white bokeh discs; on it macOS window cards and side panels, pills, a heavy black headline pressed over a translucent marker, status chips, ✓ / ✗ badges, text highlights, a magnifier, and three flat companions peeking over the card edges. Every rounded rectangle, disc, ring, capsule and line icon is a signed-distance field with 1 px analytic anti-aliasing (pixel centres at i + 0.5, so integer coordinates give crisp edges); only free polygons (a callout's point) are 4× supersampled and box-filtered. Shadows follow CSS box-shadow semantics (offset, blur radius sigma = blur / 2, spread, colour, opacity, painted only outside): cards stack two or three — a hairline ring, a wide shadow tinted with the field colour, a tight neutral near shadow — so cards float without a grey halo
- Layers: everything is drawn into named layers (premultiplied RGBA, cropped to what was drawn at the end). `layer(name, z)` starts drawing into one (calling again reopens it), lower z sits behind: a companion's z below the card's means only the part peeking over the edge shows; after `end()` drawing lands on the field. Each layer has a pose `pose(name, dx, dy, scale, alpha, reveal, origin, feather)`: offset, scale about origin, opacity, a left-to-right reveal (reveal 0–1, soft edge feather px); `show / hide` only change opacity; `bounds(name)`. Two states of the same thing (a check row before / after) are two layers, switched with show / hide. `stage(name)` composites with the current poses (PNG-compressed in memory)
- Construction: `PastelUI(W, H, seed, keep_stages=True)`; `field(colour='blue', glow=(x, y), glow_size, glow_alpha, bokeh)` the field (a `FIELDS` name also sets the accent `accent`, the shadow tint `tint` and the headline marker colour `mark_colour`); `wipe(cx, cy, r, prev='lilac', rim, rim_width)` a circular wipe: everything inside the circle, the previous section's field outside, an accent ring leading the edge, `wipe()` clears it
- Shapes: `rrect(x0, y0, x1, y1, r, fill, alpha, grad=(c0, c1), angle, ring, inset)` (ring=w draws only the border, `inset` decides inside or outside), `circle(cx, cy, r, fill, alpha, ring, grad)`, `line(pts, width, fill)`, `poly(pts, fill)`, `shadow(x0, y0, x1, y1, r, dx, dy, blur_px, spread, colour, alpha, shape=(cx, cy, r))`, `soft_shadow(cov_fn, box, ...)` (a drop shadow for any shape), `with clip(x0, y0, x1, y1, r):` clips what is drawn inside into the rounded rectangle (pane fills clipped to the window's corners)
- Text: `text(s, x, y, size, fill, weight='regular'|'medium'|'bold'|'heavy', anchor, alpha, spacing, family='sans'|'mono')`, returns width; Latin and CJK split automatically (macOS: PingFang for CJK, Lantinghei Heavy for the heavy headline, SF Pro variable weight for Latin, SF Mono for mono; elsewhere Noto Sans CJK / Microsoft YaHei; overridable with `INKPAINT_FONT_PASTELUI_<CJK|HEAVY|LATIN|MONO>`); in `anchor` the second letter is s = baseline / m = middle of the body / t = top of the body; `measure(...)` measures width; `marker(x0, x1, base, h, colour, alpha, drop)` the translucent stroke under the words (a text highlight is a marker with h covering the full letter height)
- Widgets: `window(x, y, w, h, title, icon, sub, split, bar=50, radius=22, rim=9, tint)` returns a Box (`.bar`, the `.left` and `.right` panes): a 55 %-white frosted rim, two shadows, a white body, the title bar's red / yellow / green lights, a centred app icon + bold title + mono subtitle, a pale left pane and hairline dividers; `card(x, y, w, h, r=26)` a side panel; `pill(x, cy, text, dot)` a pill with a dot, returns its right edge; `steps(x, cy, labels, lit, current)` a step strip joined by arrows; `headline(x, base, text, size, mark='keywords', draw_text, draw_mark)` the heavy headline and its marker (both drawable as two layers, the marker layer behind in z); `chip(x, cy, text, colour, size, solid, glyph, anchor)` a status chip; `mono_tag`; `callout(x, cy, text, colour, glyph)` a solid little tag with a pointed left edge; `ref_chip(x, base, text, glyph, ok)` a reference chip inside a text line (a green ✓ dot top-right); `badge(cx, cy, 'pass'|'fail', size)`; `spinner`; `bar(x0, x1, cy, frac)`; `tile(x, y, size, fill, glyph, grad)` an app-icon tile; `avatar`; `glyph(name, cx, cy, size, fill, sw)` 24-cell line icons (`GLYPHS`: more user code lens spark book inbox tag check x suitcase umbrella plug passport calendar pin sun bolt leaf); `weather('sun'|'partly'|'cloud'|'rain', cx, cy, size)`; `check_row(x, y, w, h, label, tag, sub, glyph, tone, state='idle'|'active'|'pass'|'fail', result, status)` a check row (with `status=False` the right end is left empty — draw it into another layer with `row_status(x1, cy, state, text)`, so the badge can pop separately)
- Magnifier: `magnifier(cx, cy, r, mag, colour, name='lens', z, handle=45)` its own layer; at composite time the **real** pixels beneath are bilinearly magnified by `mag` into the lens first, then the white rim, a pale violet inner ring, the glint and the violet-gradient handle are drawn on top; move it with `pose('lens', dx, dy, scale)` and the magnified content follows
- Companions (flat vector, faceless): `spark(cx, cy, size, spin)` an orange starburst in a cream circle (a white ring, a warm shadow); `cloud(cx, cy, size, rot)` a white >_ in a violet gradient cloud (eleven circles smoothly unioned, a radial gradient, gloss, a shadow); `critter(x, y, px=9, blink)` an orange pixel critter (10 × 6 cells, integer-pixel aligned)
- Palette constants `INK BLUE LILAC GREEN PINK YELLOW MINT PEACH CLAY LINE GREY FIELDS BOKEH`; `render()`, `composite()`, `save(path, stages_dir)`; `save('x.jpg')` defaults to quality 88, 4:4:4 chroma

## Exam grading — `Grading`
- Idea: an answer sheet on a dark green felt desk being marked. Every paper is a posed object: `Paper` stores its paper colour (mottled clouds, fibres, a slight cockle, stray fibres), alpha (punched holes cut out) and all printing in its own frame; coordinates are always given in the canvas frame *as if unrotated*, the paper's own `rot` and the pose from `place()` applied only at composite time. A lifted paper is slightly larger with a wide soft offset shadow; laid down the shadow tightens to a contact line. Printing is immediate (ink multiplied into the paper; reversed type with `mode='knock'` shows the paper itself). Everything written after printing is a retained `Mark` with `progress` (0–1, how far it has been written, settable back and forth): each Mark holds a density map (Beer's law: paper colour × exp(−density × ink absorbance), deepening naturally over print and other ink) and a time map (at what progress each pixel was reached), so stage snapshots can show strokes half written. Finally the whole frame is multiplied by an upper-left warm lamp's falloff, a vignette and fine grain; desk, papers and pen share the same uneven light
- Construction: `Grading(W, H, seed, keep_stages=True, stages_dir=None)` (with `stages_dir` given, `stage()` writes PNGs immediately instead of keeping them in memory); `desk(colour=FELT, nap)` the felt: two scales of mottling, fine nap, stray fibres
- Papers: `sheet(x, y, w, h, tint, rot, stack=2)` the answer sheet (`stack` more sheets beneath, offset a few px and slightly darker); `card(x, y, w, h, tint, rot)` a card / slip (110 px left around it for the clip's upright handles); `place(paper, lift, dx, dy, rot)` lays down (all 0) or lifts (the first place fixes stacking order), `remove(paper)`; `paper.to_canvas(x, y)` paper coordinates → canvas coordinates under the current pose; `paper.width(s, size, kind)` measures text width
- Printing: `text(s, x, y, size, kind='body', colour, anchor='ls', spacing, alpha, rot, mode='ink'|'knock'|'over')` (returns the end x), `rect(x0, y0, x1, y1, fill, line, width, radius)`, `line(pts, width, colour, alpha, dash=(on, off))`, `ellipse`, `hatch` diagonal hatching, `grid` a faint grid, `rules(x0, x1, ys)` answer lines, `band` a colour band, `checkbox`, `circled(n, cx, cy)` a circled number
- Answer-sheet parts: `timing_track(x, y0, y1, step=30)` a row of registration blocks, `corner_mark`, `holes(x, y0, y1, step, r)` binder punch holes (really cut out, the desk and the paper's shadow showing through), `title(s, x, y, size, sub)` a heavy title + letter-spaced Latin subtitle, `field(label, value, x, y, x1)` a grey field name + bold content + underline, `ticket(x, y, digits)` the ticket number + a 0–9 bubble column per digit (the matching bubble filled), `score_box(x, y, w, h, split, head, titles, note, lines)` the score / grader table (returns the score cell's centre), `fill_example(x, y)` the marking legend, `section(x, y, num, title, sub, meta, x1)` a numbered section head (the numeral knocked out of a dark square), `question(x0, y0, x1, y1, s)` the question in a tinted box with a left bar, `answer_frame(x0, y0, x1, y1, col, rows=[(caption, y, separator y or None)], lines)` the answer box: a faint grid and ruled lines on the left, x ≥ col the hatched grading column (the header "Grading · Candidate: leave blank", dashed row separators); `clip(x, y, w, handle)` the black binder clip: a bright black steel clip on the paper's edge, two upright silver wire handles each spanning the whole clip (the rear one slightly lower and in shadow), casting shadows on the paper and the desk
- What gets written (returns a Mark or Group, both with `progress` and `tip(p)`):
  - Red pen: `stroke(pts, width, colour, pressure, blob, feather, smooth, density)` any stroke (a gel pen: an ink bead where it lands, pressure drifting along the line, slower and darker in the turns, slightly darker rims, bleeding a little into the fibres, paler on tooth peaks); `wave(x0, x1, y, amp, period)` a wavy line with drifting period and amplitude; `ring(cx, cy, rx, ry, tilt, a0, a1, grow)` a hand-drawn ellipse ring (overshooting its start and growing a little); `leader(p0, p1, bend)` a bowed leader; `underline`; `cross(cx, cy, s)` a two-stroke cross; `check(cx, cy, s)` a tick; `digits(s, x, y, h)` digits written from stroke skeletons (0–9, tilted, slightly irregular); `score(s, cx, cy, h, ring=True)` the score: digits written then circled
  - `note(s, x, y, size, kind='bold', jitter)` a note: a bold sans (no handwriting font), each character nudged and turned, inked as red pen (density inside, pooled edges, a faint bleed), revealed character by character
  - `answer(s, x, y, size)` the candidate's blue-black handwriting, appearing character by character
  - `stamp(cx, cy, label, w=None, h=64, rot, size, check=True, press=(0.64, 1.0))` a vermilion rubber-stamp impression: a double rounded border, heavy lettering, a ✓; ragged displaced edges, clustered pin-hole voids, one end pressed lighter (the thin border breaking dry there), ink pooled at the rims, a wet bleed just after pressing (`mark.wet` 0–1), a slight tilt, the paper dented round the rubber; `progress = 0` before it is pressed
- Props: `pen(x, y, lift, angle=28)` the red ballpoint, tip on canvas (x, y), shaded as a cylinder: a metal cone and ball, a ribbed rubber grip, chrome rings, a lacquered barrel with a window highlight, the clip and rivet; the shadow starts at the tip and spreads and softens along the body (the back held up); `pen(None)` takes it away. `write(mark, progress, lift)` sets progress and puts the tip at the writing front (used frame by frame in the GIF). `stamper(mark, height)` the stamp hovering `height` (0–1) above the impression: a varnished beech block (along the grain, chamfered), a turned wooden knob (turning marks, highlight), the shadow widening and softening with height; height 0 is pressed down — the impression appears with its wet bleed; `stamper(None)` takes it away
- Fonts `font(kind, size)`: heavy (Lantinghei Heavy: titles, stamps), bold / medium / body / light (PingFang), latin / latin_heavy (Avenir Next), digits (DIN), mono (Menlo: the ticket number); Lantinghei and PingFang are on-demand macOS fonts, falling back to Hiragino Sans GB / STHeiti, Linux to Noto Sans CJK, Windows to Microsoft YaHei; overridable with `INKPAINT_FONT_GRADING_<KIND>`
- Palette constants `INK GRAY RULE ANSWER PEN STAMP NAVY FELT PAPERS SHADOW`; `render()`, `composite()`, `stage(name)`, `save(path, stages_dir)`; `save('x.jpg')` defaults to quality 88, 4:4:4 chroma

## Shadow puppetry — `ShadowPuppet`
- Idea: every point seen on the screen is lamp light that came through it. The screen is cloth lit from behind by an oil lamp: brightest and palest around the lamp, deepening to amber outward, with thread slubs, a fold or two and a vignette. A puppet is scraped, oiled donkey hide, translucent and dyed with transparent colour: each piece (`Leather`) keeps coverage and an optical density per channel; what reaches the eye is lamp light × exp(−density), so vermilion, teal, ochre and green glow, black dye is nearly opaque, and overlapping pieces add densities and darken naturally (joints, a far-side hand on the body — order never matters). A puppet pressed to the screen (`d = 0`) is crisp; the larger `d`, the larger and blurrier its shadow, and because the lamp has two wicks it splits into two offset shadows — the soft double edge of a figure not quite pressed. Rods are sharp where they are tied and soft lower down, further off the screen. Finally a little glow, a soft-shouldered tone curve, a vignette and fine grain; the carved proscenium and lanterns stand in front, lit by the light the screen spills onto them
- Construction: `ShadowPuppet(W, H, seed, keep_stages=True, stages_dir=None)` (with `stages_dir`, `stage()` writes PNGs immediately); `screen(colour, edge, hot, weave, folds)` the cloth (`hot` = where the lamp is behind it); `lamp(level, radius, flicker)` the lamp: brightness, how far the glow spreads (0.1 = just lit, a small pool), a flicker (±); `proscenium(plaque='Cuju', top=150, valance=54, side=64, sill=80, bracket=170, plaque_w=420)` the stage: a key-fret carved lintel + a red lacquer plaque with gilt characters in the middle, an openwork key-fret valance, openwork brackets at the two corners, pillars, a sill, and the screen opening set to the frame's inside; `frame(dy, alpha)` the lintel group's pose (it drops in from above in the GIF, the lanterns following); `lantern(x, top, r, drop, tassel, lit, swing)` a red paper lantern: a bamboo frame, candlelight inside, a black-lacquered gold-trimmed hat, a gold knot and red tassel, a warm halo; `set(lit, swing, shown)`
- Leather `Leather(x0, y0, x1, y1, scale, ss=3, seed)` (coordinates in design units, × scale = px, the origin is the pivot; `sp.leather(...)` seeds it automatically): `fill(shape, dye)` cuts / glues a piece, `dye(shape, dye)` re-dyes existing leather, `edge(width)` inks along the cut edge, `contour(region, width)` outlines a region, `punch(shape, rim)` punches holes (an ink rim round each), `line(points, width)` a leather line, `carve(points, width)` cuts a slit, `pattern(region, motif, size, angle, margin, origin)` hollows a region leaving a solid margin of `margin`: `dots`, `rings`, `flower` plum blossoms, `coins` old coins, `scales` fish-scale armour, `cloud` ruyi cloud heads, `lattice` a square grid (angle=45 diagonal), `stripes` bar holes, `ice` ice cracks; round motifs (dots / rings / flower / coins) with `angle=90` leave only a centred column in narrow strips (the gate posts are done this way); shape helpers `poly ellipse ring stroke ribbon(a tapering band) text(vertical text) below / above / left_of / right_of inset grow`; `finish()` folds in the hide's uneven thickness (30 px mottling, 90 px patches, fine fibres) as "how much light each channel blocks". Shapes may be points or a same-size mask
- The puppet `Puppet`: `add(name, leather, parent, joint)` a joint tree (a child's pivot hangs on the parent's `joint`, a thread knot at each joint; add parents first); `pose(**angles)`: angles measured counter-clockwise with the figure facing right; a hanging limb at +90 points forward, head +10 tilts up, torso +6 leans back; `set(x, y, rot, d, flip, alpha)`; `state()` / `load(st)` / `Puppet.tween(a, b, t)` store, load and interpolate whole poses (used frame by frame in the GIF); `rod(part, point, lean, width)` control rods; `ground(floor y, [parts])` puts the lowest sole on the ground; `away[part] = distance` lifts one piece off the cloth on its own; `point(part, point)` a part point's canvas coordinates
- `figure(robe, trim, accent, trousers, face, skin, facedye, headdress, plume, pom, pattern, hands, scale)` builds a standard figure (facing right, `flip=True` left): `pelvis` the short skirt and belt as root, `torso` cloud collar, breastplate mirror, scale armour, belt, `head` a profile face (`face='hollow'` positive cut: the face cut away, a profile line, one long eye, a long brow; `face='solid'` negative cut: the face left as `facedye`-dyed leather with the features cut out), `arm_f / arm_b` upper arms (only the near side carries the shoulder guard), `fore_f / fore_b` forearms and cuffs, `hand_f / hand_b` ('open' / 'fist'), `thigh_f / thigh_b`, `shin_f / shin_b` leggings and thick-soled boots; headdress 'plume' a helmet + two barred pheasant plumes, 'crown' a three-pronged crown + pompoms + side tassels, 'bun' a hair knot and ribbon; at scale 1, about 520 px from sole to helmet top
- Props (a one-piece prop, movable and liftable like a puppet): `gate(w, h, net_h, eye, eye_r, post, beam, roof, net, trim)` a pailou goal: posts (a column of coin holes), a beam, a tiled roof with upturned eaves, ridge curls, a finial, a lattice net and the round red-eyed eye, pivot at the floor's middle; `ball(r)` the leather ball: six seams, a red core, one lens-shaped hole per panel; `border(y0, y1, dye, trim)` the floor band (a dot strip, an openwork key-fret band, a black edge; canvas coordinates, placed at 0, 0); `nameplate(text, dye, size, string)` a small vertical plaque hanging on a thread (pivot at the thread's end); your own: `sp.prop(leather)`; `place(p, x, y, **kw)` places or updates on the screen, `hide(p)`
- Fonts: `plaque` / `label` (plaque, small plaques): Wei stele (an on-demand macOS font) → Libian → Kaiti → Songti, Linux Noto Serif CJK, Windows STXinwei / Kaiti; overridable with `INKPAINT_FONT_SHADOWPUPPET_PLAQUE` / `_LABEL`
- Dyes `DYES`: plain red crimson teal green ochre yellow blue brown pink black; palette constants `SCREEN SCREEN_EDGE HIDE WOOD LACQUER GOLD SPILL ROD LAMPS`; `render()`, `composite()`, `stage(name)`, `save(path, stages_dir)`; `save('x.jpg')` defaults to quality 88, 4:4:4 chroma

## Pen & wash — `PenWash`
- Idea: each card is an independent sheet of cold-press watercolour paper (its own buffers, drawn in card coordinates = canvas px as if unrotated, 2× internal resolution), placed on the table with a position and a small angle only when the photo is composed. Paper: bumps of about 1 mm (2–4 px here) over a softer undulation, lit by the window's upper-left light; the cut edge not quite straight, with fuzz. The ink is a fineliner: near-uniform width, every stroke resampled then given a low-frequency wobble and a little tremor, width drifting ±9 %, a small blob at the landing, some strokes ending in a thinning flick; drawn quickly, a closed shape is 1–3 strokes that either stop short leaving a gap or overshoot the join along the tangent, box corners drawn separately and overshooting each other, the odd stretch gone over again offset 1–2.5 px; ink stacks by Beer-Lambert optical density, with a little white showing in the deepest tooth valleys; parts occlude by z, an outline automatically breaking where a nearer part covers it. The wash (after the ink dries) first computes "the region it meant to cover" (the part minus anything in front), then paints like a quick hand: the whole region slipped 2–5 px (spilling over the line on one side, leaving a white gap inside the line on the other); the edge re-found on a blurred copy with a wandering threshold (negative spilling out, positive shrinking, plus patches of whole-edge shrink), crisp but shaky, following the tooth; a dried rim (pigment gathering at the edge as the water dried); granulation (pigment sinking into the tooth's valleys); flat brush streaks; density mottle; optional dry-brush breaks; wet-in-wet `bleed` (dropping into a still-wet shape, soft edges, stopping at the wet rim); the paper cockles where it got wet. Pigments also stack by optical density, deepening and mixing where they overlap. A background block that does not fill the card is a rough flat rectangle painted around the figure with a few px of white halo. Opaque white gouache goes on last
- Construction: `PenWash(W, H, seed, ss=2, keep_stages=True, stages_dir=None)`; `table(kind='oak'|'pale'|'walnut'|'linen', plank=176)` the table (oak planks: cathedral-grain arches, vessels, plank gaps and butt joints, a satin sheen); `card(cx, cy, w, h, rot, tone, corner, tooth, seed)` places a white card and makes it current (`rot` in clockwise degrees), `use(card)` switches; subsequent drawing goes to the current card (or pass `card=`)
- Parts (ink drawn only at `ink()`): `part(pts, z, ink=True, strokes=None, corners=False, smooth=True, width, reserve=True, nseg)` a region plus its outline (`strokes` to outline only given open paths; `corners` splits the corners into straight lines that overshoot each other; `reserve=False` lets background blocks paint over it; `nseg` sets how many strokes a closed contour takes); `limb(pts, w0, w1, z, caps=(False, True))` sleeves, legs, braces (only the two sides and the given end caps outlined); `stroke(pts, z, width, closed, smooth, corners, wobble, over=(0, 3), double, flick, nseg)` one stroke (not drawn where a higher-z part covers it); `lines(paths, ...)`; `dot(x, y, r, z)` dotted eyes; `ink()` inks all pending strokes on the current card
- Faces and bits: `glasses(cx, cy, r, gap, z, temple=(x, y))` big round glasses (both lenses are parts — skin painted behind stops at the frames, the lenses left white), returns both lenses; `brows(paths)`; `tufts(x, y, n, length, spread, angle)` a few sticking-up hairs; `frame(x0, y0, x1, y1)` a hand-drawn frame (one stroke per side, corners overshooting); `ground(x0, x1, y)` a two-stroke ground line; `signature(x, y, size)` a little spiral signature in the corner
- Shapes (static methods, card coordinates): `PenWash.oval(cx, cy, rx, ry, rot, egg, wob)` (`egg` > 0 a wide-bottomed egg), `rrect`, `box(cx, cy, w, h, rot)`, `turn(pts, cx, cy, deg)`, `smooth(pts, closed)`, `tube(pts, w0, w1)` → (outline, [left, right, end, start]), `hand(x, y, angle, size, kind='mitten'|'open'|'fist', flip)` → (outline, finger lines)
- Pigments: `wash(target, colour, strength, shift, slip=(2, 4.8), warp=1.6, gaps=2.2, soft=2.2, edge=0.35, gran=0.14, streak=0.08, angle, var=0.13, dry)`, target is a part, a list of parts, points or `('mask', sl, m)`; for regions with an equivalent radius under 16 px the slip, gaps and jitter shrink proportionally; `bleed(target, x, y, r, colour, strength)` wet-in-wet (blush, toast-brown); `background_block(x0, y0, x1, y1, colour, strength, rough, corner, halo, angle, edge, streak, dry)`; `spatter(colour, n, box, size)` flicked paint dots; `highlight(pts, width, alpha)`, `glint(x, y, r)` white gouache; colours from `PAINTS` (coral red pink rose peach skin tan ochre yellow brown terracotta teal mint sage green cobalt sky bluegrey grey warmgrey black lilac) or '#rrggbb'
- Props on the table: `pen(x, y, angle, length=430, r=10.5, cap=True)` the fineliner (tip at (x, y), the body at `angle` degrees: a fibre tip, a metal collar, a grey cone, a matte barrel printed with the line width, a glossy cap over the tail and a clip); `water_brush(x, y, angle, length=320, r=12, stain='teal', cap=(x, y, angle))` the water brush (a nylon tip stained a little with pigment, a ribbed grip, a slightly misted transparent barrel refracting what is beneath as a cylinder, a bubble at the tail; `cap` places a clear cap separately); both lit as cylinders with shadows (the transparent part's shadow faint, with a little caustic). Scale: with cards about 9 cm wide the fineliner is longer than a card (640 px, r 16 in the example), the water brush 600 px, r 24
- Constants `PAINTS INK PAPER WOODS LINEN SHADOW`; `render()`, `composite()`, `stage(name)`, `save(path, stages_dir)`; `save('x.jpg')` defaults to quality 88, 4:4:4 chroma; barrel lettering uses core's sans_bold (overridable with `INKPAINT_FONT_SANS_BOLD`)

## Retro gouache — `GouachePortrait`
- Idea: opaque gouache in coats, dark to light and big to small (toned ground → umber underdrawing → background → flats → value modelling → texture → features → highlights → print grain).
  - Paint: every stroke replaces the colour beneath (C ← C + (colour − C)·A); a wet brush picks up a little of the layer below (`pickup`).
  - Tooth: the board has a tooth height field `G`.
  - One stroke: a flat brush dragged along a short arc. Across it, each bristle carries a different load, so streaks run along the stroke with slight value shifts; along it, the paint runs out. Fully loaded it covers; as the load drops, colour lands only where loaded bristles cross tooth peaks, so the tail breaks into dry specks and points; ragged sides, a rounded head.
  - Edges: shapes are blurred one pixel, then re-thresholded with a low-frequency wobble and the tooth — crisp, but never a vector edge.
  - Modelling: a mask is blurred, normalised to its inner edge and square-rooted as a dome height (or pass `height`; spheres use an exact ellipsoid). One upper-left, slightly frontal light; the lit value quantised into 3–5 soft steps mapped onto a five-colour ramp (shadows toward purple-red, lights toward warm white). Wet-in first, then covered with short strokes that follow the form (along iso-lines, down the fall line, or around the sphere centre), each stroke taking its colour where it lands.
  - Knit: one V per stitch (two slanted ellipse yarns of cylindrical cross-section with twisted-ply streaks, neighbouring rows overlapping, valley occlusion), lit and multiplied onto the existing paint so the big value steps survive. Rows can arch, droop, rotate or follow a path; cables are two tubes alternating in front; ribbing is raised knit columns and sunken purl columns with small chevron stitches on each.
  - Hair: dark masses first, then hundreds to thousands of tapered strands along a flow field, banded dark to light by a lit field, dark painted first, a few flying off the silhouette.
  - Felt: mottle at two scales, short fibres and fuzz outside the silhouette.
  - Finally `grain()` adds the feel of a printed book page: tooth grain, a warm paper tone in the lights, ink never reaching pure black, saturation slightly reduced.
- Construction: `GouachePortrait(W, H, seed, keep_stages=True, stages_dir=None)` (total pixels capped at 4096²).
  - `panel(x0, y0, w, h)` makes a new picture and makes it current; subsequent coordinates are local pixels of this panel. `use(p)` switches.
  - `ground(tone='#c88f5a', paper, alpha=0.5)` paints the beige board, each panel getting a thin warm imprimatura.
  - `sketch(mask, colour, width=2.2, alpha=0.8, breaks)` draws the mask's outline in diluted umber — the underdrawing, later covered by colour.
- Shapes: return float masks the size of the panel, combined with `np.maximum`, multiply, `1 −`.
  - `ellipse(cx, cy, rx, ry, rot)`, `poly(pts, smooth=True)`, `blob(cx, cy, rx, ry, rough, seed, rot)`
  - `band(path, w0, w1)` a heavy path; `GouachePortrait.tube(path, w0, w1)` gives outline points.
  - `rough(mask, amount)` a painted ragged edge.
- Brushes: angles in degrees, 0 right, 90 down.
  - `stroke(x, y, angle, length, width, colour, bend, dry, alpha)` one stroke.
  - `strokes(mask, colour or a colour field, angle or an angle field or f(x, y), length, width, density, dry, alpha, clip, load)` scatters many strokes in the mask, each taking colour and direction where it lands. `load` < 1 lands half-dry — a scumble.
  - `dry_brush(path, width, colour, dry, alpha, taper)` one long drag along a polyline (steam, long sweeps).
- Painting:
  - `scumble_background(colour, bottom=None, avoid=figure mask, angle, light, dark, scratches, flecks, blend)` a flat or top-to-bottom gradient background: fully loaded long strokes first, then lighter and darker scumbles half-dry, short pale scratches and splashes, avoiding the figure.
  - `block_in(mask, colour, angle)` a flat block, returns the ragged mask.
  - `model_form(mask, colours five steps, steps=4, height=None, angle='contour'|'fall'|number|field|function, relief=0.55, round_=0.5, bias, ambient=0.08)` value modelling in steps, returns the lit field.
  - `form_light(mask, height, relief)` → (lit 0–1, height, gradient).
  - `sphere_shade(cx, cy, rx, ry, colours, mask, rot)` sphere modelling: strokes around the centre, a reflected light at the lower rim.
  - `glaze(mask, colour, alpha, mode='multiply'|'screen'|'normal', soft)` a glaze.
  - `cast_shadow(caster, dx, dy, soft, alpha, colour, on)` a soft shadow under a brim, a nose or a beard, landing only on `on`.
  - `dry_highlight(mask, colour, above=0.62, angle)` dry-brush highlights in the light, 'fall' for domes.
  - `blush(cx, cy, rx, ry, colour, strength)` powdery blush.
  - `dab(x, y, r, colour)` a small dot (highlights, buttons).
  - `line(path, width, colour, alpha, taper, dry)` a thin round-brush line.
  - `spatter(mask, colour, n, size)` splashes.
- Faces:
  - `eye(cx, cy, r, ry, iris, iris_r, look=(dx, dy), lid, lid_colour, lashes, lash_dir, ring, lower, iris_light)` round eyes: a milky-white ball, a shadow under the lid, a dark or teal iris (brighter below), a black pupil, a white highlight, lids and lid lines, a fine warm outer ring. `look` = 1 touches the edge; both eyes inward and down is cross-eyed.
  - `nose(cx, cy, rx, ry, colour, on, cast)` a big nose: a soft shadow on the face, sphere modelling, a reflected light on the lower rim, nostrils, a soft pink-white highlight with a touch of white.
  - `ear(cx, cy, rx, ry, colour, side)` big ears.
- Texture:
  - `knit(mask, stitch=(width, height), origin, curve, angle, path, cables=[(x, width)], strength, highlight)` V-shaped flat stitches. `curve` > 0 droops rows downward, < 0 arches them up; with `path` they follow a scarf.
  - `rib(mask, path, period)` ribbing (necks, cuffs, hat brims).
  - `hair_strands(mask, flow, colours dark→light, n, length, width, shade, curl, jitter, roots, clip)` hair, brows, beards, bobbles, curls.
  - `felt(mask, strength, fibres, fuzz)` felt.
- Colours and constants:
  - Colours from `PAINTS` (vermilion red orange mustard yellow ochre cobalt cerulean navy teal seaglass green olive peach skin ruddy nose rose blush pink umber sienna cream white grey ink brown cocoa) or '#rrggbb'.
  - `ramp(base, deep, shadow, light, pale, cool, warm)` gives the five steps; give skin a warm `cool`.
  - Constants `PAINTS PAPER LIGHT`; `grain(amount)`, `render()`, `composite()`, `stage(name)`, `save(path, stages_dir)`; `save('x.jpg')` defaults to quality 88, 4:4:4 chroma.

## Blue picture book — `BlueBook`
- Idea: draw one page in the illustrator's order, then print it (blank paper → blue colour blocks → ink line → scribbled dark masses → accents and blush → white chalk → rain, splashes, spatter → print).
  - Tooth: a histogram-equalised tooth height field `T` (uniform 0–1), so "the tool reaches where tooth > 1 − contact" turns pressure straight into coverage. The tooth is also pre-dragged along 8 directions (`TD`); each stroke reads the version along its own direction, so the specks a dry tool skips stretch into short streaks along it.
  - One stroke (shared by ink lines, coloured crayon lines, chalk lines and rain): the path is splined and resampled at 2 px, given a slow hand wobble and a fine tremor. Pillow paints each segment's index into a 32-bit integer map — which pixels the stroke covers and which segment they belong to — then the arc length and across-offset are projected exactly per pixel.
    - The pressure lands fast and lifts slowly (`taper`), swelling and thinning along the way (`swell`).
    - Across the stroke, a few hairs carry different loads and run dry along it (`dry`); where contact × load falls below the tooth the line breaks into white streaks along its direction.
    - The edge wanders with the tooth, never a vector edge.
  - Colour is dry crayon, pastel or dry gouache:
    - The region slips 1.5–3.5 px first (`slip`); its edge re-found on a blurred mask with a wandering threshold (`loose`), pressure dropping near the edge and breaking dry (`edge`, `dry_edge`).
    - Coverage depends only on pressure and tooth (`pressure` 0.95 leaves only fine white specks); value comes from low-frequency mottle and parallel lanes (`mottle`, `lanes`).
    - Wax and pastel are semi-transparent: new = old + (colour × old^0.55 − old) × coverage. Over white it is the colour; over ink it stays dark; blue over blue deepens; `medium='gouache'` and `'chalk'` are opaque.
  - Ink on top: ink coverage is kept in `K`, so later colour barely catches on ink (`over_ink` default 0.1, chalk 0.5, eye highlights 1) — the line always sits over the colour, like a printed book.
  - Finally `printed()`: the print's slight softness, only the black lifted to a deep ink (whites never greying), fine paper specks, an optional gutter (a soft shadow, a fine crease and a line of light).
- Construction: `BlueBook(W, H, seed, keep_stages=True, stages_dir=None)` (total pixels capped at 4096²), starting with `paper(tone='paper', tooth, drag)`. Coordinates are pixels of the whole canvas.
- Shapes: return canvas-sized float masks, combined with `np.maximum`, multiply, `1 −`.
  - `ellipse(cx, cy, rx, ry, rot)`, `poly(pts, smooth=True)`, `blob(cx, cy, rx, ry, rough, seed, rot)`, `band(path, w0, w1)`; `BlueBook.tube(path, w0, w1)` gives outline points.
  - `cloud(cx, cy, w, h, bumps, seed)` clouds; `rect(x0, y0, x1, y1, rough, corner, seed)` a hand-drawn ragged rectangle.
- Colour:
  - `block(box or mask, colour='powder', rough, corner, pressure, angle, edge, dry_edge, streaks, avoid, halo)` the big background blocks: ragged edges, stopping short of the page edge, border strokes following the edge and dragged dry, then dozens of dry strokes hugging the edge (confined near it).
  - `fill(mask, colour, pressure=0.9, angle, slip, loose, edge, dry_edge, mottle, lanes, lane_var, passes, medium='crayon'|'gouache'|'chalk', avoid, halo, clip, streak, over_ink)` fills a region, returning what it actually covered. `angle` is a number or a canvas-sized angle field; `avoid` + `halo` goes around what is in front, leaving a white rim; `slip=0` does not slip.
  - `crayon(paths, colour, width, pressure, dry, medium)` dry coloured strokes along paths (hair strands, far trunks, wet ground, reflections).
- Ink:
  - `ink(path, width=4, dry, taper, wobble, swell, overshoot, clip)` one brush / ink-pencil line.
  - `outline(pts, width, closed=True, open_=0.45, overshoot, strokes=(1, 3), twice=0.35)` quick contours: closed shapes in 1–3 strokes, stopping short before the join or running past it along the tangent; `twice` the chance a stretch is gone over again, offset one or two px.
  - `scribble(mask, width=5, spacing=4.5, angle=80, reach, overshoot, dry, colour, clip)` dark masses: back-and-forth zigzag scribbles in one direction, overshooting or falling short of the outline at the turns, one wrist-sized patch at a time.
  - `hatch(mask, angle, spacing, length, width, colour, medium, density)` one quick short parallel hatch after another (a sleeve's shaded side, shadows on white fur).
  - `blades(x0, x1, y or f(x), n, height, lean, width, clip)` tapering grass blades growing from a baseline.
  - `dot(x, y, r, ry, colour, medium)` solid little dots with irregular edges (eyes, noses, buttons).
- White and weather:
  - `chalk(mask or a list of paths, width, pressure=0.62, angle, over_ink=0.5)` opaque white chalk / white pastel: a mask is filled (clouds, a little bus), paths are drawn (highlights, glass reflections); only on tooth peaks, stretched into long streaks along the stroke.
  - `rain(region, n, angle=100, length, width, colour='deep', avoid)` short dry rain strokes.
  - `splash(x, y, size, colour, n, up)` the little ring of short lines and droplets a raindrop throws up; `ripples(cx, cy, rx, ry, rings)` broken concentric rings on a puddle.
  - `spatter(region, colour, n, size, satellites)` flicked paint (power-law sizes, slightly stretched, with satellite dots); `speckle(region, colour, n, size)` finer dust and glints. `region` is a mask or (x0, y0, x1, y1).
- Characters:
  - `head(cx, cy, rx, ry, width, ears=[(x, y, r)])` a big round head drawn open, ears drawn only where they show, returns the head's (with ears) mask.
  - `eyes(pts, r, look=(dx, dy), glint)` dot eyes with a white glint; `smile(x, y, w, depth, tilt)`; `blush(cx, cy, r)` stippled blush (a very pale crayon base + sparse pink dots); `freckles(cx, cy, n, spread)`.
  - `fluff(mask, 'ink' or 'chalk', length, width, curl, avoid, gaps, curls, reach)` fluff. 'ink' draws the outline as a run of short scalloped arcs (unclosed, with gaps); 'chalk' is white fur growing from inside, crossing the edge over the background blue; `curls` are little curls inside.
  - `flower(x, y, r, colour='coral')` a little flower: opaque petals plus an ink-dot centre.
- Finish: `printed(amount, gutter=x)` printed on the book; `folio(x, y, '14', size)` a small page number; `render()`, `composite()`, `stage(name)`, `save(path, stages_dir)`; `save('x.jpg')` defaults to quality 88, 4:4:4 chroma.
- Colours from `COLOURS` (paper chalk ink mist powder cornflower blue cobalt deep teal navy slate coral tomato cheek flesh freckle mustard butter) or '#rrggbb'; constants `COLOURS`, `INK`.

## Jiangnan garden — `JiangnanGarden`
- Idea: painted back to front in the landscape illustrator's order (sky → far hills → haze → far trees → trees behind the buildings → buildings → the far-bank garden → water and reflections → things on the water → foreground → the hanging frame → the seal); opaque pigment covers what is behind, so paint order is occlusion.
  - Aerial perspective: every painter has `far` (0 near – 1 very far); the farther, the more colours pull toward the haze (a pale blue-green) and the lower the contrast; `haze()` lays a fog band between depth planes, densest at the hills' feet.
  - Strokes: one stroke is one small polygon (a pointed leaf, a round petal, a sword blade, a dot), each with its own size, angle and colour. Thousands laid at once: Pillow fills every polygon with its id into a 2× supersampled integer id map (the id is the order — later covers earlier), numpy looks ids up in a colour/alpha table and box-averages back to 1×; edges anti-aliased, a stroke costs well under a microsecond. A fixed fine canvas texture is multiplied into the pigment, shifting values a few percent.
  - Light: the sun upper-left, slightly frontal. A tree crown is a set of clumps (ellipses, those behind placed first); each stroke finds the clump it lands in (the frontmost containing it) and treats the clump as a dome: the normal gives diffuse shading, the whole clump darkens downward, plus low-frequency noise to break it up. That 0–1 value picks a colour from a ramp of mixed greens (a deep teal-green core → mid green → yellow-green → pale sunlight green).
  - Crowns painted as a gouache painter paints: a dark underpaint (the clump cores + 1.7× larger dark leaves, the base colour's edge itself made of leaves) → the full crown's mid-dark leaves → leaves breaking out across the silhouette → clusters (5–14) of bright leaves where the lit value is high → the few palest strokes. Leaf tips point outward and droop slightly; near shrubs take `veins`, a pale vein on the bright leaves. Tray pines use the same model: a gnarled trunk plus tiers of flat needle pads, needles very fine and barely drooping.
  - Blossom trees: branches, a stroke underpaint (dark petals + deep green leaves, not one flat block), twigs, a few young leaves, then hundreds of blossom clusters sorted by light (bright over dark), petals facing the sun whiter inside a cluster, branches showing through the gaps between clusters; `blossom_branch()` is a near branch: five-petal flowers one by one, stamens, buds and young leaves.
  - Weeping willow: trunk and a few curving boughs first, then a crown of hanging leaves covers them; hundreds to a thousand strands in tresses (3–9 dropping together at one spot, gaps between tresses), emerging from boughs or the crown, arcing slightly outward before hanging, with a little sway; each strand is a hair-thin stem with small narrow leaves hung randomly on both sides, slightly splayed and drooping. Painted back to front (rear rows darker and bluer, front rows lighter and yellower), the lit side and the tips' new leaves brightest.
  - Roofs: a front-view hip-and-gable roof is a surface S(u, v) (u across −1..1, v from the ridge 0 to the eave 1): steeper above, gentler below (the eave stretch flattened by perspective), widening downward, the wings thrown outward, the eave ends kicking up sharply. The surface is broken into small quads rasterised into the id map and each pixel's exact (u, v) recovered by two Newton steps, so tiles shade analytically: pan tiles (lit on the left, a dark seam beside) and flat tiles row by row, a bright lip per course with a shadow below, each tile with its own slight value, the odd one greened; a row of end caps and drip tiles at the eave, rafter ends and the soffit beneath, an eave shadow on the wall; the main ridge openwork with curled ridge ends, the hip ridges running up into the flying corners.
  - Buildings: plaster walls are flats with low-frequency mottling, rain stains under the eaves, a greened wall foot and a layer of faint strokes; `dapple()` is leaf shade cast on lit surfaces (thresholded cold blotches, heavier nearer the casting tree). Lattice doors and windows (square, diagonal, lantern brocade, cracked ice) draw their bars on a 3× map, lit bright on the upper-left edge and dark on the lower-right, casting a little shadow into the dark interior or the window paper; columns are lacquered cylinders with stone bases; lanterns are ribbed red ellipsoids with gilt caps and tassels; plaques are framed boards with outlined letters; platforms, steps and bridges are jointed stones, each with its own value. The zigzag bridge is real geometry: spans, slabs, piers, posts, pierced panels and handrails all projected through a pinhole camera (`camera()`, `project()`), every face shaded by its normal, back faces culled.
  - Water: the lake is jade, paler far off where it mirrors the sky. Reflections mirror the picture above the bank line, pushed sideways by ripples, blurred vertically (blurrier the farther from the bank), darkened, and mixed toward the jade body colour with a Fresnel that increases toward the viewer — matching what stands on the bank, only softer and darker. Things standing in the water carry their own: the bridge reflects its geometry heights inverted and re-projected, the boat and the egret flip their own layer about the waterline. Last: horizontal strokes sampling the water itself, faint ripple lines (bright mirroring sky, dark mirroring banks) and fine vertical streaks. Lily pads lie on the plane through the camera (ellipses flattened by eye height / distance), notched, veined, a bright far edge, a dark near half, and a little shadow.
  - Finish `grain()`: slight softness, a spring halo round the lights, shadows toward teal, fine grain, the faintest canvas weave and vertical streaks; `seal()` a small worn red seal, white characters.
- Construction: `JiangnanGarden(W, H, seed, keep_stages=True, stages_dir=None)` (total pixels capped at 4096²); `camera(horizon, eye, focal, cx)` sets the pinhole camera on the lake, with `project(X, Y, Z)`, `ground_y(Z)`, `depth_at(y)`, `px_per_m(Z)`. Canvas coordinates are pixels; world coordinates metres (X right, Y up from the water, Z away).
- Base: `dabs(xs, ys, length, width, colours, angle, shape='leaf'|'round'|'blade'|'dot', alpha, far)` lays many strokes at once (angles in radians); `stroke(path, w0, w1, colour)`; `fill(mask, colour, alpha)`; `poly(pts)`, `ellipse(cx, cy, rx, ry)` return canvas-sized masks; `crown(cx, cy, w, h, n, size)` generates clumps (cx, cy, rx, ry); `with g.clipped(mask):` draws only inside (the view through the moon gate).
- Sky and distance: `sky(top, bottom, y1, clouds)`; `hills(ridge, base, colour, far)` a ridge spline, relief-noise lighting, crown bumps on the silhouette, fading at the foot; `haze(y0, y1, y2, colour, strength)` a fog band rising from y0 to y1, gone by y2.
- Plants: `foliage(clumps, ramp_='spring', leaf=(5, 9), density, far, light, droop, aspect, veins, edge, highlights)`; `blossom_tree(branches, clumps, kind='white'|'pink'|'azalea', petal, density, far, leaves, gaps)`, branches = [(path, w0, w1), ...]; `blossom_branch(path, w0, w1, kind, flowers, spread, size)`; `willow(trunk, boughs, strands, length, ramp_, leaf, far, sway, crown, tips, gap, bundle, splay, aspect, crown_anchor)`; `pine(trunk, limbs, pads)`; `branch(path, w0, w1)`; `reeds(x0, x1, y or f(x), n, height, lean, width, bend)` curved blade leaves; `petals(region, n, kind, water)`; `rock(cx, cy, rx, ry)`; `pot(x, y, w, h, colour, band)` returns the pot mouth's centre.
- Buildings: `wall(x0, y0, x1, y1, colour, stains, damp)`; `dapple(x0, y0, x1, y1, amount, scale, cover, falloff=(x, y, r), mask)`; `roof(cx, y_ridge, y_eave, ridge_w, eave_w, upturn, flare, rows, courses, ridge_h, soffit, shadow)` returns the surface function; `coping(x0, x1, y, h)` wall-top tiles; `column(x, y0, y1, w)`; `beam(x0, x1, y0, y1)`; `lattice(x0, y0, x1, y1, pattern='lantern'|'grid'|'diamond'|'ice', cell, bar, frame, back='interior'|'paper'|colour|None, kind='window'|'door')`; `fretwork(x0, x1, y0, h)` the hanging frieze; `round_window(cx, cy, r, ring, pattern or inside=function(mask), ground, reveal)` the openwork window / moon gate; `stonework(x0, y0, x1, y1, course, block, damp)`; `steps(x0, x1, y, n, rise, tread, grow)`; `bridge(path=[(X, Z), ...], width, deck, thick, rail, post, rails='both'|'rear'|'none', reflection)`; `lantern(x, y, h, cord)`; `plaque(cx, cy, w, h, text)`.
- Water: `lake(y0, y1, far_colour, near_colour, bank)`; `reflect(bank, strength, blur, ripple, darken, fresnel, src)` (`src` takes the canvas as it was before the water); `layer()` + `reflect_layer(before, axis, box)` give the boat and the egret their own reflections; `ripples(box, n, light, dark, alpha, brush)`; `lily_pads(pads=[(X, Z, radius m)], flowers=[(X, Z, size m)])`; `lotus(x, y, s)`; `boat(x0, x1, y, h)`; `egret(x, y, s, pose='stand'|'fly', facing)`.
- Finish: `seal(x, y, w, h, text)`; `grain(amount)`; `render()`, `composite()`, `stage(name)`, `save(path, stages_dir)`; `save('x.jpg')` defaults to quality 88, 4:4:4 chroma.
- Colours from `PAINTS` (sky sky_low haze cloud plaster coral jade jade_dark jade_light lacquer beam interior paper stone tile board gold wood lantern seal bark bark_light water water_deep water_far terracotta porcelain egret) or '#rrggbb'; ramps from `RAMPS` (spring jade deep olive willow far white pink azalea tile stone rock pad) or your own colour list; constant `LIGHT`.

## Dry-brush blocks — `DryBrushBlocks`
- Idea: following the reference poster: lay the biggest blocks first, build the figure from a few planes, then details and one small red square; no outlines, no gradients — form comes entirely from blocks' edges and overlaps.
  - Brush frame: each block is brushed in its own frame: the mask is rotated (Pillow) so the stroke direction runs along +u, coverage, value and colour mix computed there with axis-aligned arrays, then rotated back — so bristle streaks, lanes and dry ends always follow the stroke.
  - Textures: a few periodic textures are generated once per canvas, each block taking a random window: bristle streaks (about 40 px along the stroke, 1–3 px across); dry-brush teeth (bristle streaks + heavier drag marks + tooth, histogram-equalised to 0..1, so "colour where teeth > 1 − contact pressure" turns pressure straight into coverage); roller teeth (clusters of isotropic fine dots); comb-edge noise; lane value shifts; stretched mottle. All noise is white noise blurred along a direction (wrap-around edges), never an upscaled coarse grid.
  - Lanes: a big block is parallel passes of a wide brush or roller (`lane` wide, each pass slightly wider or narrower), each starting loaded and drying toward its end at its own rate (`dry`, `dry_from`); `stroke_len` breaks each pass into strokes of different lengths with their own values and dry tails; `passes=2` lays a second pass so the block's body closes while both ends stay ragged; `direction` sets the run (+1 / −1 one way, 0 random, 2 back and forth).
  - Edges: across the stroke only slightly softened (`side`, both sides slightly wavy straight edges), along it much more (`fringe`), then thresholded with the bristle texture: sides near-straight, both ends combed into tines of varying length, longer at the dry end (`tines`), tines growing only out of the soft band.
  - Scumble: where mottle is high the contact pressure drops (`scumble`), paint catching only the tooth's teeth, paper or the colour below showing through; `edge` is a few dry-rubbed stretches along the outline, only on the stroke-direction sides. `load` > 1 is a fully loaded brush that stays solid even where it dries fast (the porcelain bowl).
  - Colour: opaque gouache by default (new = old + (colour − old) × coverage); a dry stroke over another colour lets the lower one show through the gaps; `mode='multiply'` like screen-print overprint (the glass, the tea); `mix=(colour, fraction)` puts a second colour's stripes in the same brush.
  - Finish `printed()`: like this picture's offset print: slight softness, fine paper grain, scattered paper-coloured dots knocked out of the darks, scattered dust on the paper (added in render).
- Construction: `DryBrushBlocks(W, H, seed, keep_stages=True, stages_dir=None)` (total pixels capped at 4096²); `paper(tone)` lays the cream paper and builds the textures. Canvas coordinates are pixels, angles degrees (0 right, 90 down).
- Shapes (canvas-sized float masks): `rect(x0, y0, x1, y1, rot)`, `poly(pts, smooth)`, `ellipse(cx, cy, rx, ry, rot)`, `tube(path, w0, w1)`, `quad(pts)`.
- Blocks: `slab(shape, colour, angle, load, dry, lane, fringe, side, tines, scumble, streak, vary, direction, passes, seam, edge, stagger, dry_from, stroke_len, tooth='brush'|'roller', mix, mode, alpha, avoid, clip)` returns coverage; shape may be a mask, (x0, y0, x1, y1) or polygon points; `piece(shape, colour, torn, cut, rim, ...)` a torn / cut paper piece: only part of the outline tears (`cut` the fraction kept as a straight cut), the tear may carry a pale fibrous rim; `backdrop(box, colour, angle, rough, scratches)` the roller slab behind the subject (nearly flat, clustered speckle, a crisp loaded edge, dry sides, a few fine scratches); `accent(x, y, size, colour, rot)` the small square (x, y its top-left); `scratches(mask, colour, n, length, width)`.
- Brushes: `stroke(path, width, colour, load, dry, taper, wobble, square, mode, alpha, clip)` one dry stroke along a path (every pixel knows its arc length and across-offset, texture following the curve, a flat head, drying as it goes); `scumble(mask, colour, n, length, width, angle, spread, load, dry)` short dry strokes scattered in a mask (a beard, hair, highlights), angle may be a function f(x, y).
- People: `head(cx, cy, w, h, tilt, turn, light, skin, lit, shade, warm, ear, hair, crown)` returns an anchor dict (eyes, nose, mouth, chin and masks); `glasses(head, colour, lens, frame)`; `beard(head, colour, shade, length, width)`; `jacket(neck, shoulders, bottom, lit, shade, dark, shirt, collar, light)`; `sleeve(path, w0, w1, colour)`; `hand(x, y, size, angle, grip, cuff)`. All in local unit coordinates (centre, size, tilt) — a pose is a few numbers; `light=-1` means the light comes from the viewer's left.
- Objects: `bottle(x, base, w, h, colour, shoulder, neck, neck_w, dark, light, label, label2, cap, glass)`; `bowl(cx, rim_y, w, h, colour, band, inside, shade)` returns (body, bowl interior); `cup(cx, base, w, h, colour, glass, level, tea)`.
- Finish: `printed(amount)`; `render()`, `composite()`, `stage(name)`, `save(path, stages_dir)`; `save('x.jpg')` defaults to quality 88, 4:4:4 chroma.
- Colours from `PAINTS` (paper cream bone white ink black forest green slab sage leaf ash celadon mint khaki teal lagoon tealblue navy deep sky ice vermilion tomato coral blush ochre tan skin skin_light terracotta umber brown mustard) or '#rrggbb'.

## Twilight storybook — `TwilightStorybook`
- Idea: painted in storybook order: dusk sky → night → hills → the flower slope → characters and the kite → glowing things → the plate and handwriting; a "nightness" field is fixed first and every later lit thing shades by it.
  - Dusk sky: a six-stop gradient (lavender → lilac → rose → peach → apricot → gold, positioned by the horizon), stop positions warped by horizontally stretched low-frequency noise, so the colours lie in soft layers, not ruler-straight bands, plus a few percent of fine mottle. Clouds are lens-shaped bands: one flat lens plus a few rounded masses (higher in the middle), blurred outlines, flatter and crisper bottoms, denser downward, semi-transparent; thin light streaks under the clouds; the sun a pale disc with two halos, painted before the hills so they cut it.
  - Night: `night_split()` fixes the whole picture's nightness field N (0 dusk – 1 night) from one wandering boundary line, a wide feathered transition; the night gradient, the Milky Way, stars and moon are all painted with N as their alpha, so stars thin and fade across the boundary instead of stopping at a line; near the boundary the night leans indigo, the transition band re-saturated (`richness`) — dusk → mauve → indigo → black without greying.
  - Every later "lit" thing (hills, flowers, figures) shades by the same N: colour passes through a linear 3×3 grade (desaturate, then cool) mixed by N — a white daisy on the night side turns moonlit blue, grass a deep slate. Emissive things (stars, fireflies, lanterns, shooting stars) and the plate go in `with t.unlit():`, unaffected.
  - Milky Way: an analytic band between two points with a bend and a wandering centreline, density a cross-sectional gaussian × two scales of cloud noise, a narrower dark rift in the middle; thousands of faint stars sampled from the band's density. Stars: uniform positions, power-law brightness (most dim, a few bright), a few warm or blue, splatted with sub-pixel accuracy (large stars a disc coverage kernel, small ones mass-preserving gaussians, bincount accumulation); the brightest few get four-point sparkles (long tapering rays plus short diagonals), only deep in the night, far from the horizon.
  - Hills: far hills are filled ridges (per-column analytic anti-aliased edges), fading toward the feet (fog in the valleys); striped hills are bands following the ridge, widening toward the viewer (perspective), each band cut into slanted patchwork fields, some fields with parallel crop rows, a darker hedge line between bands and fields, a pale lit rim on the crest; `trees()` places little round trees (shadow, trunk, crown, an upper-left lit face).
  - Flower slope: the ground is a sage-to-olive gradient with mottle and fine specks, a bright rim on the crest (gold crossing into silver past the boundary). Flowers and grass placed by perspective with rejection sampling: size grows as a power of the depth below the crest (default t²), density ≈ coverage / (π · size²), so the crest is thousands of colour specks and the bottom a few dozen big flowers; species drift in patches by low-frequency noise (a stretch of daisies, a stretch of cornflowers); distant flowers sink into the hazy ground colour. By size: a colour speck, a small flower with a centre, or a full flower head (daisy, cornflower, poppy, cosmos, marigold, buttercup, lupine, forget-me-not), each with a fine stem, the big ones a leaf; heads flattened by perspective, backward petals drawn first. All polygons sorted by their base y, rasterised in one pass into the supersampled integer id map (painter order = id order), looked up and averaged back — tens of thousands of anti-aliased shapes in under a second.
  - Characters: flat, cute storybook characters assembled from smooth polygons: `wanderer()` the small seated hooded figure (a felt-textured cloak, a soft sagging hat tip and pompom, a cream round face, dot eyes with highlights, blush, mittens and boots, a warm soft rim on the sunset side, a broken dark fine outline); `jar_lantern()` a jar of fireflies that glows and lights the grass around it; `kite()` a four-panel diamond kite with spars, a bridle and a tail of bows; `kite_string()` slightly sagging, turning from dark (dusk) to silver (night) with the sky behind it. `fireflies()`, `butterfly()`, `dragonfly()`, `seeds()` (dandelion seeds), `birds()` are small things left for the closer look.
  - The plate: fine ink lines drawn as polylines at 3× (slight hand jitter, tapers) and averaged back — even 0.8 px hairlines are smooth; `hatch()` hatches parallel hand lines in a shape (call again at another angle for cross-hatching), `dimension()` extension lines, dimension lines with architectural slash ticks and small labels (`across=True` spans a member sticking out of the frame, leading out to "ø 6"), `ruler()` a small scale bar, `wash()` a flat watercolour tint with a darker edge; `inset()` the circular magnified detail: a paper disc with a soft shadow, a `draw(t, cx, cy, r)` callback clipped inside, a fine double ring, a marker ring and leader on the target, a label.
  - Handwriting: a thin handwriting face (Noteworthy Light on macOS, or set `INKPAINT_FONT_HAND_LIGHT`) set letter by letter: each letter with its own rotation, baseline wobble, spacing and weight, drawn at 2× and reduced, over a faint pencil texture.
  - Finish `finish()`: slight softness, a bloom in the lights, fine grain with a little more soft mottle, a plum-tinted light vignette, optionally a cream torn-paper edge at the bottom; parameters recorded and applied in render, so call it before the first stroke and every stage snapshot matches the final.
- Construction: `TwilightStorybook(W, H, seed, keep_stages=True, stages_dir=None)` (total pixels capped at 4096²); `with t.clipped(mask):` draws only inside (inside the inset), `with t.unlit():` skips nightness shading. Canvas coordinates are pixels; angles in radians unless noted (`hatch()` uses degrees).
- Base: `lines(paths, width, colour, alpha, wobble, taper)`; `fill(mask, colour, alpha)`; `shapes(polys, colours, alpha)`; `poly(pts)`, `ellipse(cx, cy, rx, ry)` return masks; `glow(x, y, r, colour, strength)`; `splat(xs, ys, rad, colours, alpha)`.
- Sky: `dusk_sky(stops, horizon, mottle)`; `cloud(x0, x1, y, h, colour, alpha)`; `streak(x0, x1, y)`; `sun(x, y, r)`; `mist(y0, y1, y2, colour, strength)`; `birds(pts, size)`; `seeds(pts, size)`.
- Night: `night_split(edge, soft, ground)` (`edge` a top-to-bottom polyline, night to its right; call after `dusk_sky()`); `night_sky(top, low, indigo, richness)`; `milky_way(p0, p1, width, bend, rift, stars)`; `stars(n, region, sparkles, dusk)`; `sparkle(x, y, size)`; `moon(x, y, r, phase, tilt)`; `shooting_star(p0, p1)`.
- Ground: `hills(ridge, colour, base, fade, rim)`; `striped_hill(ridge, colours, stripe, grow, fields, rows, hedge)` returns info (ridge, bounds, for placing trees); `trees(pts, size)`; `meadow(crest, size, density, grass, kinds, drifts, haze)` returns info (crest); `grass_heads(x0, x1, base, n, height, lean)` tall tasselled grass on the crest against the sky.
- Characters and little things: `wanderer(x, y, s, facing, reach)` returns the hand position; `jar_lantern(x, y, s)`; `kite(cx, cy, size, tilt, tail)` returns the bridle knot; `kite_string(p0, p1, sag)`; `nightline(path, width)` a thin line that changes with the sky; `fireflies(pts, size, trails)`; `butterfly(x, y, s, colour)`; `dragonfly(x, y, s)`.
- Plate and text: `hatch(shape, angle, spacing, width)`; `dimension(p0, p1, text, offset, across, lead)`; `ruler(x0, y, x1, labels)`; `wash(pts, colour)`; `leader(p0, p1)`; `inset(cx, cy, r, draw, target, label, sub, leader_colour)`; `caption(x, y, text, size, colour, anchor, rot)`.
- Finish: `finish(grain, vignette, bloom, soft, edge)`; `render()`, `composite()`, `stage(name)`, `save(path, stages_dir)`; `save('x.jpg')` defaults to quality 88, 4:4:4 chroma.
- Colours from `COLOURS` (sky lavender lilac rose peach apricot gold sun cloud cloud_low streak; night night night_low indigo star moon milk; ground mauve mauve_dark haze rim silver meadow meadow_far meadow_near grass tree tree_light trunk field_sage field_lav field_wheat field_green field_rose; lines and paper ink pencil caption paper pale; flowers daisy cornflower poppy cosmos marigold buttercup lupine forgetmenot eye stem; characters cloak cloak_dark face cheek boot mitten kite kite_cream spar string lantern firefly glass) or '#rrggbb'; species in `SPECIES`.

## Hatched nocturne — `Nocturne`
- Idea: a small night print painted in the pastel / coloured-pencil artist's order (paper and dark ground → sky → distant land → cool clouds → add light → glowing filaments and sparks → sea → reflection → the little figure → mounting and captions); the whole print is built from short strokes alone, and the warm light is the last strokes "added" layer by layer.
  - Paper: a histogram-equalised tooth `T` (uniform 0–1), low-frequency mottling and a few hundred fibres; the picture area is underpainted dark first and every later stroke clipped to it (like taped edges), the margins staying clean. The tooth is also pre-dragged along 8 directions, each stroke reading its own, so skipped tooth stretches along the stroke as short streaks rather than isolated dark dots.
