  - One stroke: the centreline is integrated through an orientation field (angles mod π, so it never flips midway), bent a little, blunt landing, tapering tail. Tens of thousands at once: Pillow fills each stroke's outline with its id into a 2× supersampled integer map (later over earlier), numpy looks up each covered pixel's stroke colour, start, direction, length and width, recovers its position on the stroke (u along, v across), and from v the bristle lane: six lanes per stroke, each with its own value and load, drying toward the tail (`dry`), pigment landing only where tooth < pressure (`pressure`) — every stroke streaky and grainy, the dark ground showing in hairline gaps; averaged back to 1×. Within a batch the topmost stroke wins (never a smeared mush); depth comes from layered passes.
  - Colour: one stroke takes one colour — a pigment, a ramp indexed by a lit field, or any f(x, y); plus per-stroke value jitter (`value`) and hue jitter (`hue`, a true rotation about the grey axis); `lit` adds the light map to strokes, so strokes in the light are themselves warm.
  - Orientation fields: `flow(angle, perturb, scale)` a region's wind; `radial(cx, cy)` radiating from a point (a cone's slopes, light rays); `contour(density)` following iso-lines (around cloud puffs, along ridges); `blend(f1, f2, w)` mixes with doubled angles (0 and π are the same direction). Strokes in a steam column follow the rising column and curl only at the rims — curling everywhere turns into a whirlpool.
  - Light: `light()` point lights and `light_from()` emissive masks (lava rivulets) sum into a light map. `glow()` lays light strokes: landing probability follows light × receptivity, colour from the light map, so the glow is warm strokes thinning outward, not a smooth halo halo. Air and glare are added (screen); lit surfaces are painted directly by light × receptivity with `blend='paint'` — a blue-grey cloud under orange light turns pink; painted as the lit colour it is red-orange; `whiten` pushes the strokes nearest the hot source toward warm white (white steam).
  - The hot source `source()`: a small fan of yellow-white strokes, rings of orange and red radiating outward, a tiny white core and a small bloom.
  - Filaments: `threads()` a walk with momentum that keeps turning toward its end (and down the fall line off a summit), so braided rivulets rather than zigzag lightning; strands drift apart toward the tips, small-angle branches fade out or stop in a bright bead, brightness crusts into dark stretches, the front hottest. `filaments()` paints them into a 2× float emission buffer: fine lines, a tight halo, light strokes falling on nearby things, bright beads at tips and along the way. `sparks()` ballistic sparks: a short fading streak and a hot head.
  - Water: `reflect()` lays horizontal strokes on the sea, colour from the mirror of the picture above the waterline (stretched by waves, pushed sideways by ripples, darkened and mixed with the sea), landing only where the mirror is bright, so the reflection is broken; `glitter()` the column of light the source pulls toward the viewer on the waterline: hot and narrow near, widening, reddening and breaking up downward.
  - Figures and silhouettes: `figure()` a seated figure a few dozen px tall (head, torso, tucked legs, hands on knees) in silhouette colour with one accent (a scarf, a hat), a warm rim on the lit side; `silhouette()` dark masses in strokes (reefs, islands, hills), an optional rim on the lit side.
  - Mounting: `mount()` presses a plate mark outside the picture area (the groove's upper and left edges dark, lower and right bright); `caption()` pencil small type set glyph by glyph in a thin plain face (Helvetica Neue Light / Thin class, no handwriting font), baseline and angle jittering slightly, graphite blurring on the tooth. Finish `bloom()` a restrained glow only on the brightest light in the picture, `grain()` fine grain.
- Construction: `Nocturne(W, H, seed, keep_stages=True, stages_dir=None)` (total pixels capped at 4096²), starting with `sheet(box=(x0, y0, x1, y1), paper, ground)`. Coordinates are pixels of the whole canvas; orientation fields in radians, `flow()` angles in degrees (screen y down, positive angles slanting to the lower right).
- Masks: `poly(pts, smooth)`, `ellipse(cx, cy, rx, ry, rot)`, `blob(cx, cy, rx, ry, rough)`, `below(xs, ys, invert)` (below a spline) return canvas-sized float masks; `value(top, bottom, y0, y1, mottle, scale)` a vertical value field with low-frequency mottle.
- Strokes: `hatch(region, field, colour, length, width, density, jitter, bend, value, hue, alpha, pressure, dry, lanes, lit, mode='paint'|'light', taper, clip)`. `region` is a landing-probability mask or (x0, y0, x1, y1); `density` is roughly how many passes cover the region; `length` / `width` take (min, max), a number or f(x, y) (the sea's strokes longer nearer); `colour` takes a pigment, (ramp, lit field or f(x, y)) or f(x, y) → (N, 3).
- Light: `light(x, y, r, colour, power, squash)`, `light_from(emission, spread, colour, power)`, `lightmap()`; `glow(receptivity, field, length, width, density, power, reach, blend='light'|'paint', ambient, whiten)`; `source(x, y, r, power, field, squash, core)`.
- Filaments and dots: `threads(start, ends, wander, strands, spread, branches, branch_len, width, fall, heat)` returns paths; `filaments(paths, gain, halo, light, receptivity, field, beads)`; `sparks(x, y, n, spread, speed, streak)`; `embers(region, n)`; `stars(region, n, size, bright)`.
- Water: `reflect(waterline, region, src, stretch, ripple, darken, mixw, thr, length, width, density)` (`src` takes the canvas before the sea, `waterline` may be a per-column array); `glitter(x, y_water, y_end, w0, w1, power)`.
- Silhouettes and figures: `silhouette(mask, ramp_, field, val, density, rim, rim_from)`; `figure(x, y, h, facing, accent, accent2, legs='knees'|'dangle', lean, rim_from, rim, hat)` returns the figure's mask.
- Finish: `mount(plate, depth)`, `caption(text, x, y, size, anchor, weight='light'|'thin', alpha, jitter)`, `bloom(amount)`, `grain(amount)`; `render()`, `composite()`, `stage(name)`, `save(path, stages_dir)`; `save('x.jpg')` defaults to quality 88, 4:4:4 chroma.
- Colours from `PAINTS` (paper pencil ground night indigo navy dusk haze starlight mist slate steam steam_lit rock rock_lit sea sea_deep glint blood ember lava flame core scarf star warmstar) or '#rrggbb'; ramps from `RAMPS` (fire ember sky sea steam land isle) or your own colour list.

## Obsidian UI — `ObsidianUI`
- Idea: one frame of a note app in an explainer film (default dark theme). A near-black page (#161616) with one or two soft violet glows; the window a whole set of dark greys (note area #1e1e1e, sidebars and title bar #202020, hairline dividers #363636), with exactly one accent (violet #8a5cf5 by default, pale text #a88bfa): the short bar under the H1, #tags, internal links, the switch's selected segment, the lit flowchart node, the graph's linked notes. Every rounded rectangle, disc, ring, line and line icon is a signed-distance field with 1 px analytic anti-aliasing (pixel centres at i + 0.5, integer coordinates crisp); free polygons (the pointer, arrowheads, diamond nodes) 4× supersampled and box-filtered. Shadows follow CSS box-shadow semantics (offset, blur radius sigma = blur / 2, spread, colour, opacity, painted only outside); with zero offset that is the violet glow of lit cards and the H1 bar; `halo()` a soft radial glow (graph highlights)
- Layers: as in Pastel UI, everything is drawn into named layers (premultiplied RGBA, cropped to what was drawn), `layer(name, z)` starts one (calling again reopens it), lower z behind, `end()` lands drawing on the page. Each layer has a pose `pose(name, dx, dy, scale, alpha, reveal, mode, origin, feather)`: offset, scale about origin, opacity, a reveal (reveal 0–1, soft edge feather px) — `mode='x'` left to right (typing), `'y'` top down, `'r'` growing from origin as a circle (the graph growing out of the current note); `show / hide` only change opacity; `bounds(name)`. Two states of the same thing (the Source / Reading switch, grey / lit graph, plain / lit node) are two layers, switched with show / hide. `stage(name)` composites with the current poses (PNG-compressed in memory)
- Construction: `ObsidianUI(W, H, seed, keep_stages=True, accent='violet'|'blue'|'teal'|'amber'|'rose'|(accent, pale text))`; `page(colour, glows=[(cx, cy, rx, ry, alpha), ...])` the page and glows (closest-side linear falloff, in the accent colour)
- Shapes: `rrect(x0, y0, x1, y1, r, fill, alpha, ring, inset)` (ring=w border only), `circle(cx, cy, r, fill, alpha, ring)`, `line(pts, width, fill, alpha, closed)`, `poly(pts, fill, alpha)`, `shadow(x0, y0, x1, y1, r, dx, dy, blur_px, spread, colour, alpha, shape=(cx, cy, r))`, `soft_shadow(cov, box, dx, dy, blur_px, colour, alpha)` (a drop shadow for any mask), `halo(cx, cy, r, colour, alpha, power)`, `with clip(x0, y0, x1, y1, r):` clips into a rounded rectangle
- Text: `text(s, x, y, size, fill, weight='regular'|'medium'|'semibold'|'bold'|'heavy', anchor, alpha, spacing, family='sans'|'mono', halo, halo_fill)`, returns width; Latin and CJK split automatically (macOS: PingFang for CJK, Lantinghei Heavy for the heavy H1, SF Pro variable weight, SF Mono for code; elsewhere Noto Sans CJK / Microsoft YaHei; overridable with `INKPAINT_FONT_OBSIDIAN_<CJK|HEAVY|LATIN|MONO>`); in `anchor` the second letter is s = baseline / m = middle / t = top; `halo=px` strokes a halo_fill ring first (graph labels: links stop short of the words); `measure(...)` measures width; `rich(x, base, runs, size, fill)` sets one line of note text: runs is `[(text, style), ...]`, style None body / 'b' bold brightened / 'link' internal link (accent colour) / 'code' inline code (mono red on a rounded dark chip) / 'hl' accent highlight / 'faint' / 'accent' / 'tag', returns the line's end x
- Window: `window(x, y, w, h, tabs, active, side=300, right=0, bar=52, ribbon=52, radius=14, ribbon_icons, lit_icon)` returns a Box (`.bar`, `.explorer`, `.note`, `.right`, `.tabs`): shadow and a 1 px ring, the title bar's red / yellow / green lights, tabs (the active tab's top corners rounded, its bottom open, joining the note; a + behind it), the icon ribbon (help and settings at the bottom), the explorer pane (`side` includes the ribbon; 0 = none) and the right sidebar (`right` wide, 0 = none) grounds and dividers; `explorer(win, items, active, size, row)` the file list: a row of tool icons on top, items `(name, depth, 'open'|'folder'|'file')`, an indent line under an open folder, the `active` row highlighted; `view_header(win, crumbs)` back / forward arrows, a centred breadcrumb (the last crumb brightened), more at the right; `toggle(x, y, w, h, labels=('Source', 'Reading'), on)` the segmented switch; `sidebar_tools(win, icons, lit)` the sidebar's top icons, returns the y below; `status_bar(win, items)` the status bar floating at the window's bottom right
- Note: `h1(x, base, s, size, bar, bar_w, bar_gap, draw_text)` (the heavy title + a glowing violet bar; `bar=False` / `draw_text=False` draws one part only, for two layers); `heading(x, base, s, size, marker)` an H2 (`marker=True` a grey ##); `tag(x, cy, s, size)` a #tag pill, returns its right edge; `prop(x, cy, glyph, key, key_w)` a property row's icon and key, returning where the value starts; `code_block(x, y, w, lines, size, lang='mermaid', pad, lh, draw)` the dark code block (a 1 px border, radius 10, the language top-right), returns a Box with `.bases` (each line's baseline), `.tx`, `.lh`; `draw=False` draws only the frame; `code_line(x, base, toks, size)` one code line, toks `[(text, 'kw'|'str'|'num'|'op'|'cm'|'fence'|'text' or '#rrggbb'), ...]`, returns the end x; `caret(x, base, size)` a block caret; `callout(x, y, w, title, lines, size, title_size, colour, glyph='quote')` a quote callout (a 16 % ground, a 4 px left edge, an icon and bold title, each body line rich runs), returns a Box; `checkbox(x, y, size, checked)` a task checkbox
- The reading-view diagram: `dnode(cx, cy, w, h, label, shape='rect'|'round'|'stadium'|'diamond', hi, size, focus)` flat dark nodes (a 2 px grey border, centred text; `hi` the accent one, `focus` adding an outer ring and glow), returns a Box; `dedge(pts, width, colour, head, label, size, label_at)` a grey line with a small filled arrowhead, optionally a dark label chip at `label_at` (mermaid's `-->|label|`)
- The graph: `graph(nodes, links, lit, label_size, only='grey'|'lit', bg)`: nodes `(x, y, r, name)`, links `(i, j)`; nodes in `lit` glow in the accent with a radial halo, links lit at both ends turn violet and thicken; `only` draws just the grey or the lit half — two layers for fading in; singly `gnode(x, y, r, label, lit, label_size, glow, bg)`, `glink(a, b, lit)`
- Pointer: `cursor(x, y, scale=1.4, click)` a white mouse pointer (a dark outline, a soft shadow), `click` 0–1 draws an expanding click ring
- Line icons: `glyph(name, cx, cy, size, fill, sw)` (`GLYPHS`: menu search file files plus grid graph canvas calendar terminal gear help back fwd chev_d chev_r edit folder_plus sort collapse list layout bars link tag hash book eye quote more check x star)
- Palette constants `PAGE BG BG2 BG3 SIDE BORDER BORDER2 TEXT BRIGHT MUTED FAINT ACCENT ACCENT2 CODE_BG CODE_TEXT INLINE SYNTAX NODE_FILL NODE_EDGE NODE_LINE LINK GNODE ACCENTS`; `render()`, `composite()`, `save(path, stages_dir)`; `save('x.jpg')` defaults to quality 88, 4:4:4 chroma

## Canvas board — `CanvasBoard`
- Idea: the whiteboard view of a dark note app (the film's Canvas look, no official logos drawn). Unlike other styles, nothing is rasterised when you call the drawing methods: every shape is recorded in **board coordinates** into a named node (a group, a card, an edge ...), and `render()` redraws every node through the camera, so any pan and zoom is crisp vectors and type. The dot grid lives on the board (moving and scaling with the camera); the window, tab bar, icon ribbon and zoom buttons do not. Rounded rectangles, discs, rings and lines are signed-distance fields with 1 px analytic anti-aliasing; polylines compute only their own per-segment bounds — a 64-segment edge or a hand-drawn sketch of hundreds of segments stays fast; arrowheads and the pointer are 4× supersampled polygons; shadows follow CSS box-shadow.
- Nodes and animation: `node(name, kind, z)` starts recording into a node (calling again reopens it; z defaults to groups 0, edges 5, cards 10, other 20). `pose(name, dx, dy, scale, alpha, origin)`: offset (board px), scale about the centre, opacity (below 1 the node draws into its own layer and composites); `set(name, **vars)` sets progress vars, `reset(name)` clears them (unset vars default 1 = done; a card's `lift` defaults 0); `show / hide`. Every shape can take `var='name'` or `var=('name', a, b)` (the var mapped from a..b onto its own 0–1), and `mode` says what progress does: fade / draw along its length / reveal left to right / pop with overshoot.
- `tween(tag, n, ease, moves=[(node, from, to)], cam=(from camera, to camera), cam_ease='io', between, last)` writes n stages: in-betweens `mv_<tag>1 ...` and the final `<tag>`; the from/to dicts mix pose keys (dx dy scale alpha) with vars; `to=None` means settled and complete; camera x, y interpolate linearly, zoom geometrically. Easings `EASE`: linear out io back drop (drop accelerates the fall then settles with a 6 % bounce — card drops use it).
- Construction: `CanvasBoard(W, H, seed, keep_stages=True, stages_dir=None, window=(x, y, w, h), tab='xx.canvas', ribbon=True, controls=True, glow=True)` (with `stages_dir`, `stage()` writes PNGs immediately); `camera(x, y, z)` the board point (x, y) centred in the pane at zoom z; `fit(x0, y0, x1, y1, pad, right)` returns the camera that just fits the region (`right` the extra px for the zoom buttons); `to_screen(x, y)` board → canvas px; `control(i)` the i-th zoom button's centre (0 plus, 1 minus, 2 fit, 3 help), `hover = i` highlights it; `cursor(x, y, click, alpha, press)` the pointer (click 0–1 a violet click ripple), `cursor()` hides it.
- Board objects: `group(name, x, y, w, h, label, colour)` a group frame (radius 20, a 2 px line, a very faint white wash, the group name floating above its top-left; vars frame / label); `card(name, x, y, w, h, colour, label, selected, pad=(24, 22), fill, tint=0.05)` a card: radius 12, a 2 px border in a card colour (`CARD_COLOURS`: red orange yellow green cyan purple, or 'accent' or hex, None grey) with a 5 % tint of it, a soft shadow; `label` the file name written above the card; `selected=True` a violet border and a pale violet halo (var sel); the var `lift` adds the "held above the board" big shadow. `image_card(name, x, y, w, h, label, bg, grid)` an image card (no padding, dark grid paper) — draw inside: `cb.node(name)` then `with cb.clip(card):`. `edge(name, a, b, colour, width=4, label, dashed, arrow, bend)` an edge: anchors are (card name, 'l' 'r' 't' 'b'[, position along the side 0–1]) or (x, y, side); a cubic leaves and enters along the sides' normals (control distance = endpoint distance × 0.45, clamped 50–240), a solid arrowhead at the end, a board-coloured label pill at the middle; the var p draws it, the arrowhead growing over the last 14 %, the label fading in past half.
- Markdown in cards (`card()` returns a `Card`, laid out top to bottom, `Card.y` the next block's top): `h1`, `h2(glyph=)`, `p(s, size, fill, weight, hl, x)` inline Markdown lines (`==highlight==` a violet ground, `` `code` `` mono on a dark chip, `**bold**`; `hl=` a var sweeping the highlight through), `task(s, done, key, lh)` a checklist (the key var 0 → 1: the violet box pops, the check draws on, a strike runs through, the text greys), `quote(lines, size, by)` a violet-bar quote, `tags([...])` violet tag pills, `chips([(name, code, colour)])` colour-code chips, `bar(frac, label)` a progress bar, `rule()` a divider, `gap(px)`. These are also drawable anywhere with `cb.rich / task / tag / chip` (laying out a timeline card yourself, for instance).
- Shapes (board coordinates, recorded into the current node): `rrect(x0, y0, x1, y1, r, fill, alpha, ring, inset)` (zoomed out, thin lines never thinner than 1 screen px), `circle`, `line(pts, width, fill, dash=(on, off))`, `poly`, `shadow`, `text(s, x, y, size, fill, weight, anchor, family='sans'|'mono'|'hand')` (in `anchor` the second letter is s = baseline / m = middle / t = top, returns width), `glyph(name, cx, cy, size)` (`GLYPHS`: menu search file plus minus grid fit help check x link image), `measure`, `with clip(...)`. Hand-drawn: `rough(pts, width, fill, closed, rough, passes=2, dash)` each segment a slightly bowed line overshooting a little at both ends, drawn twice (the second pass fainter); `rough_rect`, `rough_ellipse(cx, cy, rx, ry, a0, sweep)` (a circle or arc, the lift-off lapping slightly), `arrow_head(tip, direction angle)`.
- Fonts: PingFang + SF Pro variable weight + SF Mono, Latin and CJK split automatically; family='hand' uses a hand-note face (Hannotate SC, falling back to PingFang); sizes are floats, so type scales smoothly with zoom; overridable with `INKPAINT_FONT_CANVASBOARD_<CJK|BOLD|LATIN|MONO|HAND>`.
- Palette constants `PAGE BG BG2 BG3 SIDE BORDER BORDER2 TEXT BRIGHT MUTED FAINT ACCENT ACCENT2 BOARD DOT EDGE CODE_BG CODE_FG DONE CARD_COLOURS`; `render()`, `composite()`, `stage(name)`, `save(path, stages_dir)`; `save('x.jpg')` defaults to quality 88, 4:4:4 chroma.

## Flexoki paper — `FlexokiPaper`
- Idea: a docs-site-style diagram sheet, themed Flexoki ("ink on paper"): warm paper #FFFCF0, ink-black #100F0F type, a ladder of warm greys (50 #F2F0E5 card ground, 100 #E6E4D9 tracks and table heads, 150 #DAD8CE hairlines, 200 #CECDC3 borders, 300 #B7B5AC faintest text, 500 / 600 muted text), eight accents in two shades (600 for text, 400 for fills). On the paper, borderless shadowless light-grey rounded cards (radius 28) carrying a heavy CJK title, a grey English tag and a mono index; inside, diagrams drawn the way a lightweight renderer (the Mermaid class) draws them: boxes one step off their ground with a 2 px grey border, 2.4 px ink lines ending in a small filled triangular head, plain semibold labels. Accents only where they mean something.
  - Shapes: rounded rectangles, discs, rings, capsules, diamonds (an exact rhombus distance field), cylinders (a body plus top and bottom ellipses, the top's lower edge also stroked), pie sectors and polylines are all signed-distance fields with 1 px analytic anti-aliasing (pixel centres at i + 0.5); arrowheads and other free polygons 4× supersampled and box-filtered.
  - Lines: polylines carry their arc length — `p` draws to a fraction of it (the cut end keeps its round cap), `dash` dashes along the arc (flat caps), trimmed under the arrowhead, the head's tip being the last point. `curve(a, b, da, db, k)` is the renderer-style cubic leaving and entering along tangents, `rounded(pts, r)` a polyline with filleted corners (loop edges), `bezier(p0, p1, p2, p3)` explicit control points.
  - Groups: a node (ground, border, label) is composited as one group then placed by opacity, so popping in (0.85 → 1, fading) scales and fades label and box together, like SVG's `<g opacity>`.
  - Type: PingFang for CJK (the heavy weight Semibold plus a faux-bold stroke of size/50 px, close to a Source Han Black heading), SF Pro variable weight for Latin (big numbers tracked tight −0.03 em), SF Mono for mono; elsewhere Hiragino / Noto Sans CJK / Microsoft YaHei and Helvetica / DejaVu; overridable with `INKPAINT_FONT_FLEXOKI_<CJK|HEAVY|LATIN|MONO>`. Weights regular / medium / semibold / bold / heavy.
  - Layers: as in Pastel UI, everything drawn into named layers (premultiplied RGBA, cropped to what was drawn), each with a pose `pose(name, dx, dy, scale, alpha, reveal, origin, feather)`, `show / hide`, `bounds(name)`, `with clip(...)`; `layer(name, z, fresh=True)` clears first and redraws, for progress states (a line half drawn, a number mid-roll, a pie mid-sweep, a bar half grown) — the edge-by-edge draw-on animation is made this way. `fresh=True` does not change the pose: a layer hidden with `hide()` must be `show()`n to be seen.
- Construction: `FlexokiPaper(W, H, seed, keep_stages=True, theme='light'|'dark')`, starting with `paper()`. `ground` is what the drawing sits on (label chips at the edges, pie gaps, node default grounds all follow it): `paper()` sets paper colour, `panel()` / `window()` set the card colour; a node's default ground is one step off `ground` (pale grey on paper, paper colour on a pale grey card).
- Page parts: `chip(x, cy, text, fg, bg, size, anchor, weight, glyph=None|'dot'|'check')` a pill (black on paper by default), returns a Box; `heading(x, base, zh, en, size, en_size)` a heavy title with a grey English line on the same baseline; `number(x, base, s, size=104, fill, anchor, track=-0.03)` a big number (counts, timers, measures); `panel(x, y, w, h, r=28, tone='surface'|'paper'|'dark', title, tag, num, title_size, pad)` a card and title row, returns a Box with `.inner` (the content area under the title); `focus(box, colour=ORANGE4, width=4, r, out)` an inset focus ring; `step(x, cy, label, lit, size, accent, mute)` a step pill (dark → lit, the check drawn with lit); `meter(x0, x1, cy, frac, colour, h)` a pill track and fill; `window(x, y, w, h)` a paper window (a 3 px grey border, a pale grey title bar, three grey dots), returns a Box with `.body`; `dashed_rect(x0, y0, x1, y1, r)` a dashed rounded frame; `dimension(x0, x1, y, label)` a width annotation (dashed ends, the label on a ground chip in the middle); `code(x, base, tokens, size, mark)` a mono code line ((text, colour) fragments, `mark` grounded in pale orange).
- Renderer: `node(cx, cy, w, h, label, shape='rect'|'round'|'stadium'|'diamond'|'cyl'|'circle', size=22, p, fill, stroke, sw=2, fg, weight)`, w × h the whole outer box (a diamond spans its diagonals), `\n` breaks lines in the label, `p` the pop-in progress; returns a Node (`side('l'|'r'|'t'|'b', gap)` the side's midpoint / the diamond's vertex, `toward(x, y, gap)` where an edge toward a point leaves). `edge(pts, p, head=True, dash, width=2.4, colour, head_len=11, head_w=10.4, label, label_t, label_size, label_fill, label_bg)`: the label appears only past `label_t`, the arrowhead lands only past p 0.94. Also `head(x, y, deg)`, `line(pts, width, fill, alpha, p, dash, cap)`, `rrect`, `circle`, `poly`, `shadow` (CSS box-shadow, for one lifted card only), `text`, `measure`.
- Sequence diagrams: `lifeline(x, y0, y1, p)`, `activation(x, y0, y1, w=14, p)`, `message(x0, x1, y, label, p, reply)` (the label centred above the line, `reply` dashed), `note(x0, y0, x1, y1, text, p)` (a pale yellow sticky).
- Charts: `pie(cx, cy, r, values, colours, sweep, gap=3, labels, label_r)` clockwise from 12 o'clock, `sweep` 0–1 opening it, `gap` px of ground between wedges; `legend(x, y, items=[(colour, name, value)], size, pitch, value_x, alpha)` (alpha may be per row); `bars(x0, y0, x1, y1, values, labels, colours, vmax, ticks, tick_fmt, p, value_fmt, bar_w, value_colours)` (p may be per bar, top values rolling with p); `table(x, y, widths, n_rows, row_h, head_h, header, dots)` draws the frame and header, returns a Table (`cell(i, j)`, `col(j)`, `box`), `table_row(t, i, cells, colours, weights)` draws one row (each row may take a layer).
- Palette constants `PAPER BG2 UI UI2 UI3 TX3 TX2 TX1B INK`, `BASE50 ... BASE950 BLACK`, `RED RED4 ORANGE ORANGE4 YELLOW YELLOW4 GREEN GREEN4 CYAN CYAN4 BLUE BLUE4 PURPLE PURPLE4 MAGENTA MAGENTA4`, `ACCENTS SERIES THEMES PALETTE`; colours may also be accent names ('orange' the 400 shade, 'orange6' the 600); `mix(a, b, t)`; `render()`, `composite()`, `stage(name)`, `save(path, stages_dir)`; `save('x.jpg')` defaults to quality 88, 4:4:4 chroma.

## Warm pencil storybook — `PencilStorybook`
- Idea: one page painted in the coloured-pencil storybook illustrator's order (blank paper → reserve the subject, lay the sky → distance → water → the subject's pale under-layer → modelling → details and fur → the figure → reflections → lamplight and foreground), every surface built from fine pencil strokes layered along the form's own direction — no ink lines, no black.
  - Paper: a histogram-equalised tooth `T` (uniform 0–1, mostly a 1 px grain), so "wax catches where tooth > 1 − pressure" turns pressure straight into coverage; the tooth pre-dragged along 8 directions, each stroke reading its own, skipped pits stretching into very short streaks. A little wax settles in the pits too (`floor` 0.34); a wax buffer `F` accumulates, so the more layers, the deeper later strokes press — the same pits show through every layer as fine, increasingly tinted specks, closing only where the work is heavy.
  - One stroke: the centreline integrated through an orientation field (angles mod π, never flipping midway) with a slight wrist arc. Tens of thousands at once: Pillow paints each stroke as a polyline with its id into a 2× supersampled integer map, numpy looks up only covered sub-pixels' colour, start, direction, length, pressure and taper, recovers the position along the stroke for the pressure curve (heavy landing, light flicked lift-off), lays wax only on the tooth (dragged along the stroke's own direction) the pressure reaches, then bincounts back to pixels. Within a batch the topmost stroke wins; depth comes from multiple passes (`passes`).
  - Landing sites use a jittered grid (even hatching, never clumps); the region is both the landing probability and, pixel by pixel, the pressure (`fade`), so shadows are smooth gradations, not scattered dark dashes; `avoid` holds the shapes coming later — when laying the sky, the tortoise, tree and lantern post are blanked first so colour never mixes into the blue.
  - Wax mixing: new = old + coverage × (target − old), target = (1 − opacity) × old × colour / paper + opacity × colour. Over white it is the colour; yellow over blue goes green; the same colour deepens with layers; pale colours (white, cream, yellow) over dark need higher `opacity` (burnishing).
  - Modelling: `shading()` inflates a contour into a soft dome (blurred layers summed), giving 0–1 light by normal and light direction (upper-left by default); `form()` models as a pencil artist does: a pale under-layer over the whole shape, the local colour, a darker same-family tone in the shade, the deepest tone in the core, then a warm glaze on the lit side and a cool one in the shadow — each layer crossing the form's own direction field by ±`cross` degrees so strokes cross.
  - Orientation fields: `flow(angle, perturb, scale)` slowly drifting directions (sky, water); `contour(mask)` following a shape's iso-lines (around domes, stones, each scute's growth lines); `radial()` radiating from a point; `blend()` doubled-angle mixing (0 and π are the same direction); `rotate()`.
  - Fur: `fur()` locks at a time: each lock 4–12 fine strands, roots spreading within the lock, tips converging, all bending the same way, heavy landing and flicked lift-off, along the lie; the strands on a lock's shaded side a little darker (`side`), so every lock has a lit and a dark face. `pelt()` the whole coat: a pale under-layer, darker shorter locks in the shade, the local colour, pale tips where the light falls. `grass()` the other way round, blades spreading from the root up; `leaves()` clusters of short bent leaf strokes in a crown.
  - Light: `glow()` layers of warm strokes following the surrounding surfaces (yellow-white near the source, orange outward, thinning out — never radiating as rays) over a toothy lift of the paper (never a smooth halo); `window()` a lit window (orange near the frame, cream-yellow in the middle, a few bars); `lift()` a kneaded eraser / white pencil highlight.
  - Water: `reflect()` horizontal strokes on the water, colour from the mirror of the picture above the waterline (pushed sideways by ripples, blurred, mixed with the water colour) — the reflection is strokes too; `ripples()` broken elliptical rings around things standing in the water.
  - Storybook parts: `tree()` trunk, a few boughs and clumps of separately lit crowns (`tree_plan()` gives the masks first, for blanking the background); `cottage()` a cottage (walls hatched vertically, the roof along the slope, lit windows, a door, a chimney); `stone()` a stone with a slightly flat top and contour-following strokes, optional moss; `scales()` staggered little scales with a crease on the shaded side; `flowers()` wildflowers, round-headed petal strokes around a centre, optional stems.
  - Characters: `child()` a storybook child a few hundred px tall: a hat under a raincoat hood, boots, a bob falling in locks, dot eyes, blush, one hand carrying a lantern forward (`paint=False` gives masks only — the same seed draws the same child); `lantern()` a round paper lantern; `eye()` a soft dot eye with a highlight; `blush()` blush.
  - Finish: `finish()` records parameters and applies them in render (every stage snapshot matches the final; call before the first stroke): slight softness, fine grain, a very faint warm vignette.
- Construction: `PencilStorybook(W, H, seed, keep_stages=True, stages_dir=None)` (total pixels capped at 4096²), `finish()` first, then `paper(tone, tooth, mottle, fibres)`. Coordinates are pixels of the whole canvas; a number passed as an orientation field is degrees (0 right, 90 down), field arrays are radians; `LIGHT` is the default light (upper-left, slightly frontal).
- Masks: `poly(pts, smooth)`, `ellipse(cx, cy, rx, ry, rot)`, `blob(cx, cy, rx, ry, rough)`, `tube(path, w0, w1)`, `below(xs, ys, invert)`, `rect(x0, y0, x1, y1)` return canvas-sized float masks; `line_y(xs, ys)` the spline's y per column; `value(top, bottom, y0, y1, mottle)` a vertical 0–1 field.
- Strokes: `hatch(region, field, colour, length, width, density, pressure, opacity, alpha, jitter, bend, value, hue, taper, passes, clip, avoid, fade)`. `region` is a landing-probability mask or (x0, y0, x1, y1); `density` roughly the passes covering it; `length` / `width` take (min, max), a number or f(x, y); `colour` takes a pigment, a list of pigments (random pick), (ramp, field or f(x, y)) or f(x, y) → (N, 3). `strokes(paths, ...)` draws given polylines; `line(path, colour, width, pressure, seg, wobble, clip, gaps)` a soft pencil line of a few overlapping strokes; `edge(mask, colour, ..., where)` a very faint, broken same-colour contour.
- Modelling: `shading(mask, light, sigma, wrap, flat)`; `form(mask, colours=(pale, local, dark[, deepest]), field, light, cross, length, width, density, pressure, warm, cool, edge, lit, avoid)` returns the lit field.
- Fur and plants: `fur(region, field, colour, length, width, density, tuft, spread, curl, converge, pressure, side, light)` (`converge` 1 tips converge, −1 spread); `pelt(mask, colours, field, light, length, density, lit)`; `grass(region, colour, height, lean, sway, density, tuft, fan)`; `leaves(region, colour, size, density, field)`; `tree(x, y, h, r, colours, trunk, clumps, leaf, light, lean, seed)`, `tree_plan(x, y, h, r, clumps, lean, seed)`; `flowers(region, kinds, n, size, stems)` (species in `FLOWERS`: daisy buttercup poppy forgetmenot pink clover cornflower marigold).
- Objects: `cottage(x, y, w, h, wall, roof, windows, door, chimney, roof_h, light, glow, avoid)`; `stone(cx, cy, rx, ry, colours, moss, flat, light, warm, cool, avoid)`; `scales(mask, size, colours, groove, field, light, lit)`; `dots(region, colours, n, size)`.
- Light and water: `glow(x, y, r, colours, strength, density, halo, squash, clip, field)`; `window(x0, y0, x1, y1, panes, frame, light, sill, glow)`; `lift(mask, colour, amount)`; `reflect(waterline, region, src, ripple, stretch, darken, tint, mixw, soft, length, width, density, field)` (`src` takes the canvas before the water, `waterline` may be a per-column array); `ripples(cx, cy, rx, ry, rings, colour, grow)`.
- Characters: `child(x, y, h, facing, coat, hair, boots, legs, skin, look, light, arm, paint, seed)` returns hand / head / eye / mask; `lantern(x, y, size, body, ribs, cap, handle, glow)` returns the lantern's centre; `eye(x, y, r, colour, lid, look, glint)`; `blush(x, y, r, colour, amount)`.
- Finish: `finish(soft, grain, vignette, warmth)`; `render()`, `composite()`, `stage(name)`, `save(path, stages_dir)`; `save('x.jpg')` defaults to quality 88, 4:4:4 chroma.
- Colours from `PAINTS` (paper white cream ivory; sky_top cobalt sky cornflower sky_pale powder periwinkle lavender mauve violet ultramarine indigo slate teal aqua; lemon butter sun apricot peach glow lantern flame orange tangerine; meadow yellowgreen grass leaf sage moss olive forest pine lichen; ochre gold amber sienna brown umber sepia tan sand stone warmgrey pebble; terracotta brick rose coral vermilion red cheek pink; skin skin_shade hair hair_light raincoat raincoat_shade raincoat_deep denim boot eye) or '#rrggbb'; ramps from `RAMPS` (dusk_sky day_sky glow water meadow hills shell skin stone autumn) or your own colour list.

## Colour town — `ColorTown`
- Idea: one street painted far to near in the gouache illustrator's order (blank paper → sky and clouds → the far town and clock tower → rows of houses approaching → the steps and square-edge houses → the near walls either side → roses, flowers and signs → near bunting → the wet street and reflections → people); every colour is an opaque flat, and almost everything carries a fine, slightly shaky dark-brown ink line.
  - Blocks: each is a polygon, later covering earlier. Tens of thousands at once: Pillow fills each with its id into a 2× supersampled integer map (the id is the paint order), numpy looks each id up in a table (colour, opacity, texture weights) and box-averages back to 1×, so edges are anti-aliased and a block costs a few microseconds. Three texture fields are precomputed for the whole canvas — vertical brush streaks, horizontal streaks and an isotropic scumble — plus a low-frequency mottle layer; each block mixes by its own weights, values differing only a few percent: walls take `PLASTER` (scumble 0.7 plus a little vertical brush 0.5), sky and paving lean horizontal, roofs, leaves and flowers scumble. Pale colours scale texture and mottle down to about 45 % by brightness, so a white wall stays clean, not grey. Polygon edges resample every few px and are pushed along their normals by a smooth random wobble — long edges are hand-drawn straight, not ruler-straight, and colour never registers exactly with its line.
  - Ink: a deep warm brown, drawn after each group's colour (`flush()`). Every line has its own id in a 3× map (Pillow drawing round-joined polylines), so crossings never bead. Long lines split into pieces of slightly different width; each resampled with a little low-frequency hand wobble; a box's four sides drawn separately, about three in ten corners overshooting 0.3–1.3 px. The ink is semi-transparent: 60 % ink + 40 % ink × base — deep brown on cream, deep red-brown on a red wall, near-black on glass.
  - Depth: `depth(far)` (0 near ... 1 far; usable as a context or an argument) pulls colour toward the sky's pale blue-grey and desaturates a little, lines thinning and paling; the rest of the depth is scale (near windows tens of px, far ones under ten) and back-to-front occlusion.
  - Perspective walls: `plane(src, dst)` sets a homography mapping a flat rectangular layout onto a quad, so a whole facade (windows, boxes, balconies, roses) recedes to the vanishing point in one-point perspective; ink width follows the local scale.
  - Houses: `house()` builds a tall narrow townhouse from a few numbers. Walls are plaster with outlined bare-stone patches, string courses and a cornice, a thin multiplicative glaze toward the foot and a soft shadow band under the eaves. The ground floor is a door, a shopfront (lettered sign, pilasters, a small-paned window, a striped awning) or an archway. The upper windows are each a little different: cream frames, dark teal glass with one angled pale reflection, glazing bars, sills, slatted shutters on some (`shutter_p`), curtains on others, plus round and arched windows. Window boxes overflow with leaves topped by flower dots, vines trailing in front; iron balconies hold pots. Roof slates laid tile by tile in staggered courses, each slate its own blue-grey, clipped to the roof with joints inked; mansard, hip, side-gable and pointed-gable roofs, with dormers, terracotta-capped chimneys and TV aerials. Returns window, roof-line and chimney-top coordinates, for hanging bunting and training roses.
  - Plants: roses are stems winding up a path with side shoots and paired leaflets; blooms irregular cups with a darker heart, three dark petal arcs, a mixed pale rim on the upper edge, more and clustered toward the top. Trees are trunk and branches (inked) with crowns of leaf clumps, each lit as a dome from the upper left: lower clumps painted first, pale colour only on the lit tips, leaves breaking out of the dark base so the silhouette is leaf-edged.
  - Bunting: the rope sags as a parabola between two points, pennants hung at even spacing following the rope's slant, drooping more than the rope's normal; each pennant one half darker (the fold), colours cycling through a cheerful list, outlined. Small far, large near, strung at several depths.
  - Wet street: flagstones laid in perspective first, joints running to the vanishing point; then the canvas as it was before the street is mirrored about each column's base line, rippled and pushed sideways with per-column smooth offsets, stretched vertically more the farther from the base, darkened and mixed into the flagstone colour, multiplied by vertical brush streaks; finally the joints re-inked faintly and a few horizontal glints. Things drawn after (people, lamps, café tables) wrap in `mirror()`: whatever is drawn inside mirrors onto the ground automatically, a vertical tail fading downward.
  - Finish: `finish()` records parameters and applies them in render (every stage snapshot matches the final; call before the first stroke): slight softness, fine grain, a little warmth, a very light vignette.
- Construction: `ColorTown(W, H, seed, keep_stages=True, stages_dir=None)` (total pixels capped at 4096²), `finish()` first, then `paper(tone)`. Coordinates are pixels of the whole canvas; `t.lw` is the default ink width — lower it before drawing far things.
- Base: `fill(pts, colour, tex, dir, mot, hand, alpha, vary)` (`dir` 0 vertical / 1 horizontal / 2 scumble, or a three-weight tuple like `PLASTER`), `ink(pts, width, closed, alpha, colour, hand, corners, over)`, `box(x0, y0, x1, y1, colour)`, `flush(clip)` (`clip` a clipping polygon), `dab(x, y, r, colour)`, `leaf(x, y, length, width, angle, colour)`, `glaze(P, colour, top, bottom)`, `mask(P, soft)`, `paint_mask(mask, colour, alpha, tex, dir, sl)`, `text(cx, cy, s, size, colour)`; contexts `depth(far)`, `plane(src, dst)`; shape functions `rect()`, `arch()`, `ellipse_pts()`, `leaf_pts()`.
- Sky: `sky(top, mid, bottom, y1)`, `clouds([(cx, cy, w, h), ...])`, `birds(region, n)`.
- Houses: `house(x, base, w, h, wall, roof, roof_h, roof_col, floors, cols, frame, glass, shutters, shutter_p, curtains, boxes, flowers, balconies, ground, door_col, shop, sign, letters, awning, stones, boards, bands, chimneys, chimney_col, dormers, aerials, win_kind, win_w, win_h, arch_top, arch_w, top_window, cornice, lw, far, seed)` returns windows / roof / ridge / chimneys etc. (`ground` 'door' | 'shop' | 'arch' | None, `roof` 'mansard' | 'hip' | 'side' | 'gable' | 'pyramid' | 'flat' | None, `roof_col='tile'` terracotta, `balconies` a list of (floor, column), column −1 the whole floor). Parts: `window(x0, y0, x1, y1, kind, frame, glass, panes, shutters, curtains, sill, lintel)` (kind std / arch / round), `door(..., kind, panels, fanlight, step)`, `shopfront(..., text, letters, door, goods)`, `awning(x0, x1, y, depth, colours)`, `balcony(x0, x1, y, h, pots, style)`, `flowerbox(x0, x1, y, h, box, flowers, mound, trail)` (flower sets in `FLOWER_SETS`: pink red sunny violet white), `roof(kind, x0, x1, y, h, colour, dormers)`, `dormer()`, `slates(P, tile, ramp)`, `chimney()`, `aerial()`, `vane()`, `patches()`.
- Clock tower and steps: `clock_tower(cx, base, w, h, body, quoins, dome, time, flag)` (dome 'slate' or 'verdigris'); `stairs(bottom, top, n, ratio)` (bottom / top each (left x, right x, y), steps shrinking upward); `parapet(top polyline, bottom polyline, course)`; `railing(path, h)`.
- Street: `street(y0, vp, poly)`; `reflect(src, base, y0, strength, smear, ripple, darken, region)` (`src` the canvas before the street, `base` may be a per-column array); `joints(alpha)`; `mirror(base, x0, x1, top, strength)` a context; `glints(y0, y1, n, region)`.
- Plants and props: `roses(path, n, colour, dark, light, size)`, `tree(x, base, h, rad, clumps, leaf)`, `pot(x, base, w, h, plant, flowers)` (plant bush / tall / flowers), `bunting(p0, p1, sag, size, colours)`, `lamp(x, base, h, wall)`, `sign(wall_x, y, r, colour, icon, side)` (icon cup / key / loaf / flower), `person(x, base, h, coat, legs, hair, umbrella, balloon, basket, facing)`, `cat(x, base, s)`, `bistro(x, base, s)`, `poster(x0, y0, x1, y1, colour)`.
- Finish: `finish(soft, grain, warmth, vignette)`; `render()`, `composite()`, `stage(name)`, `save(path, stages_dir)`; `save('x.jpg')` defaults to quality 88, 4:4:4 chroma.
- Colours from `PAINTS` (paper ink ink_soft iron; sky_top sky sky_low haze cloud cloud_shade; tomato red brick coral salmon pink blush rose_wall mustard ochre yellow butter pale_yellow orange apricot teal teal_deep jade mint pale_mint aqua turquoise blue cobalt powder cerulean green sage lavender cream ivory white stone stone_mid stone_dark warmgrey; slate slate_dark slate_light slate_blue tile tile_dark verdigris copper; glass glass_light glass_dark curtain wood wood_dark gold; leaf_dark leaf leaf_mid leaf_light leaf_pale trunk rose rose_dark rose_light rose_pink rose_pink_dark blossom_white blossom_yellow blossom_orange blossom_purple blossom_pink terracotta pot_dark; street cobble cobble_dark wet; skin hair hair_light) or '#rrggbb'; bunting colours `BUNTING`, slates and tiles `SLATE_RAMP`, `TILE_RAMP`.

## Pastel vignette — `PastelVignette`
- Idea: a white-ground vignette painted in the pastel illustrator's order (blank paper → a pencil sketch → a background sprig and a very pale pencil townscape → flat masses → modelling → fur → hair → features → dry highlights and flyaway strands); it simply stops at the edge: no background block, no frame, colour dissolving into the white in dry strokes.
  - Paper: white paper with a histogram-equalised tooth `T` (uniform 0–1), so "pastel catches where tooth > 1 − pressure" turns pressure straight into coverage; the tooth pre-dragged along 8 directions (stored as uint8), each stroke reading its own, skipped pits stretching into streaks. A pigment buffer `F` records how much pastel is already in each pit, so later layers press a little deeper. On blank paper the tooth varies only 0.6 % — that is why the white stays clean.
  - One stroke: the centreline integrated through an orientation field (angles mod π), a slight wrist arc. Tens of thousands at once: Pillow paints every segment of every stroke with its own id into a 2× supersampled integer map (each segment slightly lengthened so bends never gap), numpy looks up each covered sub-pixel's segment, recovers the position along the stroke (a heavy-landing, flicked lift-off pressure curve, both ends rounded — never a ruler-square cap) and across it (soft sides plus a few bristle lanes opening as the stroke runs dry), pastel landing only where pressure reaches the tooth, bincount back to pixels. Within a batch the topmost stroke wins; depth from layered passes; an over-large batch splits into y-bands (memory).
  - Pigment: pastel nearly opaque: new = old + coverage × (target − old), target = (1 − opacity) × old × colour / paper + opacity × colour. Pale dry strokes press over dark (white strokes on powder blue); a thin coat only tints.
  - Dissolving: a vignette has no frame. A coat of rubbed colour is closed off by a smooth fade-out field (never breaking into tooth specks at the edge), with dry strokes running on beneath it, per-pixel pressure and dryness dropping along the fade (`fade`), stopping at different places; `ragged()` gives a colour a low-frequency wandering edge (wandering only near the original shape, never growing far-off islands).
  - Hair and fur: `strands()` hundreds to thousands of fine strands along a flow field, each with its own offset and a stable curl (`curl`), banded dark to light by a sheen field, dark first; `lock=(count, width)` groups them into locks (a lock sharing its root area, offsets, bend and shade) — hair curls in locks, never combed straight; a few fly off the silhouette unclipped. `fur()` tufts: roots spreading, tips converging, the shaded side darker. `fluff()` plants short strands inside a contour growing outward, colour taken from the pigment a few px inside (brightened on the lit side), so a furry edge melts into what is behind it.
  - Features: `eye()` big round eyes: a milky-white ball, a blue-grey shadow under the upper lid, a dark iris (brighter below, darker rimmed, with radial fibres), a pupil, the upper lid covering a little iris, a dark soft line flicking out at the outer corner, faint double lid and lower line, two white highlights; `blush()` a big powdery circle, outward only catching the tooth; `nose()` a soft shadow on the bridge's shaded side plus a red tip and a touch of highlight; `mouth()` a small mouth; `brow()` a soft pastel band with a few fine hairs.
  - Finish: `finish()` records parameters and applies them in render (every stage snapshot matches the final; call before the first stroke): slight softness, fine grain, a touch of warmth where colour was laid, the white paper untouched.
- Construction: `PastelVignette(W, H, seed, keep_stages=True, stages_dir=None)` (total pixels capped at 4096²), `finish()` first, then `paper(tone, tooth)`. Coordinates are pixels of the whole canvas; a number passed as an orientation field is degrees (0 right, 90 down), field arrays radians; `LIGHT` the default light (upper-left, slightly frontal).
- Masks: `poly(pts, smooth)`, `ellipse(cx, cy, rx, ry, rot)`, `blob(cx, cy, rx, ry, rough)`, `tube(path, w0, w1)` (`w0` may be a list of widths along the path; `tube_pts()` gives outline points), `rect(x0, y0, x1, y1)`, `below(xs, ys, invert)` return canvas-sized float masks; `ragged(mask, amp, scale, edge)` gives a colour an unfinished dry edge.
- Orientation fields: `flow(angle, perturb, scale)`, `field(f(x, y) → degrees)`, `radial(cx, cy, offset)`, `contour(mask, sigma, offset, fallback)` (computed only near the shape), `blend(f1, f2, w)` (doubled angles), `rotate()`; `value(top, bottom, y0, y1)` a vertical 0–1 field; `shading(mask, light, sigma, wrap)` a soft dome's light.
- Strokes: `strokes(region, field, colour, length, width, density, pressure, opacity, alpha, streak, soft, taper, passes, clip, avoid, fade)`. `region` a landing-probability mask or (x0, y0, x1, y1); `colour` a pigment, a list (random pick), (ramp, field or f(x, y)) or f(x, y) → (N, 3); `streak` the bristle streaks' strength, `soft` the soft sides' share of the half-width; `fade` defaults to region^0.65 — where the region fades, strokes dry and break. `lines(paths, ...)` draws given polylines; `pencil(path, colour, width, alpha)` a very pale pencil line (a few overlapping strokes, some broken).
- Colour layers: `wash(mask, colour, alpha, grain, edge, dry, clip, opacity)`; `model(mask, colours, light, lit, sigma, wrap, gamma, lo, hi)` returns the lit field; `glaze(mask, colour, alpha, mode)` (multiply / screen / normal); `dab(x, y, r, colour, alpha, soft)` a small dot (highlights); `line(path, width, colour, alpha, taper, dry)` a smooth fine round line (eyelines, moustaches, soft contours).
- Hair and fur: `strands(region, flow, colours dark→light, n, length, width, curl, jitter, shade, spread, alpha, clip, roots, lock)` (`flow` degrees, a radian field or f(x, y) → degrees); `fur(region, field, colour, length, width, density, tuft, spread, curl, converge, side, light)`; `fluff(mask, length, n, width, out, field, alpha, lift, light)`.
- Features: `eye(cx, cy, rx, ry, iris, iris_light, iris_r, look, lid, lid_skin, line_colour, line_w, flick, lashes, crease, lower, rot, side)` (`side` which way the outer corner faces, `look` −1..1); `brow(path, width, colour)`; `blush(cx, cy, rx, ry, colour, strength)`; `nose(x, y, size, side, tip, bridge, shade)`; `mouth(x, y, w, smile, colour, dark)`.
- Plants: `leaf(base, tip, width, colours, bend)` a leaf (lit side pale, pale veins, a few dry strokes); `sprig(path, n, size, colours, width, round_, spread, fade)` a leafy sprig (`fade(y)` fading leaves toward the root).
- Finish: `finish(soft, grain, warmth)`; `render()`, `composite()`, `stage(name)`, `save(path, stages_dir)`; `save('x.jpg')` defaults to quality 88, 4:4:4 chroma.
- Colours from `PAINTS` (paper white chalk pencil pencil_light graphite; skin skin_light skin_pale skin_shade skin_deep blush nose lip lip_dark lid brow crease; eye_white eye_shade iris iris_light pupil amber amber_light olive_eye; hair_black hair_dark hair hair_blue hair_grey hair_light; powder_deep powder_shade powder powder_light powder_pale cream cream_shade cream_deep ivory; ginger_deep ginger_dark ginger orange tangerine apricot peach butter mustard fur_cream fur_white ear_pink; eucalyptus_deep eucalyptus eucalyptus_light sage olive ochre stem mist umber) or '#rrggbb'; ramps from `RAMPS` (skin neck powder cream ginger hair eucalyptus ochre mist, all dark → light) or your own colour list.
- The example's scene script precomputes every shape's mask (`plan()`), then computes each layer's visible part from front-to-back occlusion (fringe > cat > face > neck > the hair behind the cat > sweater > coat > the hair behind), painting each layer only into what it can see; the 37 retained canvas-sized masks and lit fields are stored as float16.

## Poetic Halloween — `PoeticHalloween`
- Idea: a melancholic autumn-night storybook portrait painted in the illustrator's board order (board → a chalk sketch → night sky and stars → dark foliage → flat masses → minimal volumes (the porcelain face, one bob mass, the sheen) → cloth (burlap, tartan, knit) → features (flush, eyes, nose, mouth) → the little ghosts and their warm light → foreground leaves and the last strokes); the frame dark throughout, one place glowing.
  - Board: a dark toned board with a histogram-equalised tooth `T` (uniform 0–1), so "pigment catches where tooth > 1 − pressure" is coverage; the tooth pre-dragged along 8 directions (uint8), dry strokes reading their own. A separate one-pixel speck texture `ST` (uint8) serves the flush's stipple; the print grain and the coarser printed tooth are computed in `ground()` (while memory is low) and merely consumed in `render()`.
  - Strokes: the same per-segment id-map rasterisation as the pastel vignette (Pillow paints each stroke's segments with ids into a 2× supersampled integer map, numpy looks them up for the pressure curve, soft sides and bristle lanes opening as the stroke dries, pigment landing where pressure reaches tooth, bincount back): wide strokes are opaque gouache dry brush, thin and long ones pencil hatching. `colour='canvas'` makes each stroke take the canvas colour under its centre — repainting a computed base (a tartan, a modelled face, the night sky) by hand without changing its colour. Mixing is white-referenced multiplicative: target = (1 − opacity) × old × colour + opacity × colour, which also holds on dark grounds. An over-large batch splits into y-bands (`band_px` sub-pixels each, for memory).
  - Volumes: `wash()` one smooth opaque coat (colour may index a ramp by a field; computed row-band by row-band, so even a full-canvas fill never builds a full-canvas RGB temporary), the deepest pits left a touch lighter, edges breaking on the tooth; `model()` kneads a dark → light ramp up a soft dome light — the reference's minimal volumes come from this. `soften()` blurs only near the shape.
  - Hair: the bob is one mass: shade the whole hair (fringe, the far sheet, the back) up one lit ramp (near-black → slate blue); `sheen()` lays a soft sheen only where the light falls in a narrow band (lo, peak, hi), then a few long faint strokes along the flow, so the sheen stretches along the hair and the mass never breaks into strands; `strands()` adds a few strands at very low contrast; the blunt fringe is the mask's straight edge plus a few fine tips.
  - Face: `eye()` a heavy-lidded sad eye: an almond milky-white ball, the upper lid covering about half the iris and drooping at the outer corner (`droop`), the lid a puffed skin kneaded pink, a dark lid line, a wet red rim on the lower lid (`rim`), a faint double lid above, one white highlight; `reflect` dots warm light into the iris's lower half (the lamp she looks down at, mirrored). `flush()` the red flush: a smooth red layer plus a single-pixel speck stipple, denser as the flush deepens; `spot()`, `ring()` give it soft fields (cheeks, round the eyes, denser on the lower half). `nose()` a long simplified nose that is its own small volume: modelled by the light, a soft violet-grey on the shaded side, a small shadow under the tip, reddening downward to a red tip, a hint of nostril, a faint lit line down the bridge; `mouth()` closed and unsmiling, corners down.
  - Cloth: `burlap()` plain weave: each row and column its own thickness, slubs along the threads, rows and columns wandering a little, over-and-under interlacing, lit as a height field and multiplied onto the original colour, black holes between threads, loose fibres on the surface, a fuzzy silhouette; `coords=(U, V)` makes the weave follow a shape (`along(path, mask)` gives coordinates bending along a tube — a brim weaves along its arc). `knit()` chunky V stitches (`path` making the stitch columns follow a scarf), `rib()` ribbing. `tartan()` tartan: half a sett mirrored into a symmetric repeat, a 2/2 twill deciding which thread shows in each cell, each thread slightly round, multiplied by the folds' light, `coords` letting the tartan ride the folds. `fuzz()` grows fibres outward from inside the contour, colour from a few px in.
  - Foliage: `leaf()` a palmate, doubly serrated leaf (gooseberry / maple-wide lobes, shallow notches), randomly squashed (seen from the side), each lobe folded along its midrib with one side lit, brighter toward the tip, mottled with pale veins, casting a soft shadow down-right first — that is where a hedge's depth comes from; `foliage()` scatters hundreds back to front, smaller and darker farther; `lit` lights leaves by the light map (leaves near a ghost warming).
  - Light: `light()` registers warm point lights (falloff 1 / (1 + (d / r)²)², steep — a ghost lights only its own surroundings); `illuminate()` lights what is already painted by the light map: C ← 1 − (1 − C)·exp(−L × receptivity × (base + albedo × C)), so pale wool and porcelain warm a lot, dark leaves near a ghost turn olive-orange, black hair barely changes; receptivity is a field (leaves take all, the sky a little, the face only its downward parts). `emit()` writes the emission buffer, `render()` blurring it in three tiers and stacking the halo.
  - Little ghosts: `ghost()` a translucent dome-and-bell sheet made of hundreds of silver strands, falling from the crown like hair to a hem gathered in locks; a pair of glowing eyes (a cream core, an amber ring, a halo), a stretched warm bead of light at each lock's end, the hem lit warm, the whole sheet under a very faint halo.
  - Finish: `finish()` records parameters and applies them in render (every stage snapshot matches the final; call before the first stroke): slight softness, a halo on the glow, fine print grain plus a coarser paper tooth showing in the darks, black lifted a touch; computed in row bands.
- Construction: `PoeticHalloween(W, H, seed, keep_stages=True, stages_dir=None)` (total pixels capped at 4096²), `finish()` first, then `ground(tone, tooth)`. Coordinates are pixels of the whole canvas; a number as an orientation field is degrees (0 right, 90 down), field arrays radians, or pass f(x, y) → degrees directly (evaluated only where used); `LIGHT` the default light (upper-left, slightly frontal).
- Masks: `poly(pts, smooth)`, `ellipse(cx, cy, rx, ry, rot)`, `blob(cx, cy, rx, ry, rough)`, `tube(path, w0, w1)` (`w0` a width list along the path), `rect(x0, y0, x1, y1)`, `below(xs, ys, invert)` return canvas-sized float masks; `spot(cx, cy, rx, ry, rot, solid)`, `ring(cx, cy, rx, ry, width, rot, below)` soft fields; `soften(mask, sigma)` blurs only near the shape.
- Orientation fields: `flow(angle, perturb, scale)`, `field(f(x, y) → degrees)`, `radial(cx, cy, offset)`, `contour(mask, sigma, offset, fallback)`, `blend(f1, f2, w)`, `rotate()`; `value(top, bottom, y0, y1)` a vertical 0–1 field; `shading(mask, light, sigma, wrap)` a soft dome's light.
- Strokes: `strokes(region, field, colour, length, width, density, pressure, opacity, alpha, streak, soft, taper, passes, clip, avoid, fade)` (`colour='canvas'` takes the canvas colour); `lines(paths, ...)`; `sketch(path, colour, width, alpha)` a faint chalk sketch line on the dark ground (a few overlapping strokes, some broken).
- Colour layers: `wash(mask, colour, alpha, grain, edge, dry, clip, opacity)`; `model(mask, colours, light, lit, sigma, wrap, gamma, lo, hi)` returns the lit field; `glaze(mask, colour, alpha, mode, soft)` (multiply / screen / normal); `dab(x, y, r, colour, alpha, soft, ry, rot)` a small dot (highlights, glowing eyes); `line(path, width, colour, alpha, taper, dry, clip)` a smooth fine round line (eyelines, the wet red lower lid, veins, twine).
- Hair: `strands(region, flow, colours dark→light, n, length, width, curl, jitter, shade, spread, alpha, clip, roots, lock)`; `sheen(mask, flow, lit, band, colour, alpha, strokes)`.
- Features: `eye(cx, cy, rx, ry, iris, iris_light, iris_r, look, lid, lower, droop, fold, rim, crease, reflect, rot, side, clip)` (`side` the outer corner's direction, `look` −1..1, dy > 0 looking down); `flush(field, colour, strength, speckle, clip)`; `nose(path, width, tip_r, side, shade, tip, tip_strength, clip)`; `mouth(x, y, w, droop, colour, rot)`.
- Cloth: `burlap(mask, angle, period, coords, thread, slub, holes, rust, fibres, fuzz)`; `knit(mask, stitch, angle, path, origin, curve)`; `rib(mask, path, period)`; `tartan(mask, sett, scale, angle, coords, lit, lo, hi)`; `fuzz(mask, length, n, width, out, field, lift)`; `along(path, mask)` → (S, T).
- Foliage: `leaf(x, y, size, angle, colours, value, fold, lit, veins, lobes, squash, shadow)`; `foliage(region, n, size, colours, value, angle, fold, lit, veins, squash, shadow)`.
- Light and ghosts: `light(x, y, r, colour, power, squash, fall)`, `lightmap()`, `illuminate(receptivity, gain, base, albedo)`, `emit(x, y, r, power, ry)`; `ghost(cx, top, w, h, look, tips, strands, glow, lean, eye_r, body_alpha, sheet, clip)`; `stars(region, n, size, bright, warm)`.
- Finish: `finish(soft, grain, tooth, bloom, lift, warmth)`; `render()`, `composite()`, `stage(name)`, `save(path, stages_dir)`; `save('x.jpg')` defaults to quality 88, 4:4:4 chroma.
- Colours from `PAINTS` (board chalk chalk_warm; night_deep night indigo dusk haze star warmstar; porcelain porcelain_light porcelain_shade porcelain_deep flush flush_light flush_deep rim lip lip_dark lid_line crease; eye_white eye_shade iris iris_light pupil; jet hair_black hair_dark hair hair_sheen hair_shine silver silver_light; burlap_dark burlap burlap_light burlap_hi burlap_rust oxblood_deep oxblood_dark oxblood oxblood_light oxblood_pale navy bottle tar rust ochre mustard; leaf_black leaf_deep leaf leaf_olive leaf_moss leaf_maroon leaf_rust leaf_orange; ghost_dark ghost ghost_light ghost_white glow glow_core glow_ring ember) or '#rrggbb'; ramps from `RAMPS` (night porcelain hair silver burlap oxblood leaf leaf_olive leaf_maroon leaf_rust ghost glow, all dark → light) or your own colour list.
- The example's scene script precomputes every mask (`plan()`), then computes each layer's visible part from front-to-back occlusion (mittens and cuffs > jar and cloth cover > sleeve > scarf > coat; hat > fringe > the far hair sheet > face > the hair behind), painting each layer only into what it can see; retained canvas-sized masks convert to float16 on assignment.

## Oil-pastel portrait — `OilPastel`
- Idea: a white-paper figure painted in oil-pastel order (white paper → the big masses: coat, scarf, hair → skin → props and gloves → stripes → black crayon accents → features → steam and flying hair strands); it stops at the edge: no background block, no frame, the hem finished by the strokes themselves as dry comb tines.
  - Paper: white cold-press paper with a histogram-equalised tooth `T` (uniform 0–1), so "wax catches where tooth > 1 − pressure" is coverage; the tooth pre-dragged along 8 directions (uint8, dragged longer than pastel's), each stroke reading its own, skipped pits stretching into short white streaks. Blank paper keeps about four steps of tooth value (3.6–4.3 in the reference) — not a flat digital white.
  - One stroke: the centreline integrated through an orientation field (angles mod π), the stick's width constant (`taper` narrows it toward the tail — hair uses that), both sides wobbling slightly. Thousands at once: Pillow paints each segment with its id into a 2× supersampled integer map, numpy looks it up for the along and across positions. The stick's worn face splits into lanes: each lane its own pressure (value streaks along the stroke), its own slight value (`mix` can even turn some lanes a second colour) and its own end — breaking one by one through the last `dry` stretch, so the tail is a comb of tines of uneven length, never a soft fade; the landing blunt and full. Wax lands only where pressure reaches the tooth, the threshold steep (white specks are crisp, not a soft grain); waxed paper takes more wax (a second layer covers the first) but bare pits stay bare. bincount back to pixels; over-large batches split into y-bands (`band_px` sub-pixels each).
  - Mixing: wax nearly opaque: new = old + coverage × (target − old), target = (1 − opacity) × old × colour / paper + opacity × colour; `smear` lets a stroke carry a little of the wax beneath (by the amount `F`) into its own colour.
  - Edges: when a colour is clipped by a mask, the mask's soft edge is thresholded by meso-scale noise plus tooth (`edge`), breaking like a dragged stick; `spill=(dx, dy)` lets strokes run out past the shape in that direction, each edge stretch running out a different distance (26 px-scale noise) — the hem a ragged comb, never a ruled line.
  - Accents: `crayon()` a slightly furry black crayon line: the path jittering a little, split into segments of uneven length overlapping each other (the hand lifting and pressing again), each segment its own width and pressure, occasionally breaking, occasionally a finer line added beside. `blush()` parallel hatching in a ragged ellipse; `dashes()` short bold colour bars along a path (knit stripes, ribbing, fringes); `smear()` a dry-brushed shadow or a smear of sky; `wisp()` a wisp of steam curling up.
  - Features: `eye()` a black crayon eye: the opening between a lowered upper lid (`lid`, looking down) and the lower lid, a large black iris visible only inside it, a small patch of paper white as highlight, a heavy upper-lid line thickening and flicking out at the outer corner, a faint peach double lid and a light lower line; `brow()` one heavy crayon stroke plus a few short hairs; `lips()` flat orange-red lips built from short dense strokes plus a darker seam; `hair()` long strokes from a root line (or region), following a flow field, bending strongly, tapering to the tail.
  - Finish: `finish()` records parameters and applies them in render (every stage snapshot matches the final; call before the first stroke): the tooth showing beneath the wax, a little upper-left-lit thickness at wax edges (pale wax weakest), a very faint grain, the slightest softness.
- Construction: `OilPastel(W, H, seed, keep_stages=True, stages_dir=None)` (total pixels capped at 4096²), `finish()` first, then `paper(tone, tooth, drag)`. Coordinates are pixels of the whole canvas; a number as an orientation field is degrees (0 right, 90 down), field arrays radians, or f(x, y) → degrees directly (evaluated only where used).
- Masks: `poly(pts, smooth)`, `ellipse(cx, cy, rx, ry, rot)`, `blob(cx, cy, rx, ry, rough)`, `tube(path, w0, w1)` (`w0` a width list along the path), `rect(x0, y0, x1, y1)`, `below(xs, ys, invert)` return canvas-sized float masks; `spot(cx, cy, rx, ry, rot, solid)` a soft field; `soften(mask, sigma)` blurs only near the shape.
- Orientation fields: `flow(angle, perturb, scale)`, `field(f(x, y) → degrees)`, `radial(cx, cy, offset)`, `contour(mask, sigma, offset, fallback)`, `blend(f1, f2, w)`, `rotate()`.
- Strokes: `fill(region, field, colour, length, width, density, pressure, alpha, dry, streak, smear, opacity, mix, spill, edge, passes, clip, avoid, fade)` fills a region with parallel dry strokes (`region` a landing-probability mask or (x0, y0, x1, y1); `colour` a pigment, a list (random pick), {pigment: weight}, 'canvas' or f(x, y) → (N, 3); lower `pressure` more white specks; `dry` the tail stretch that breaks into tines; `streak` lane-to-lane value; `avoid` what to steer around; three passes by default); `lines(paths, colour, width, pressure, ...)` draws given polylines; `stroke(path, width, colour, ...)` one stroke.
- Accents: `crayon(path, width, colour, pressure, wobble, seg, gap, double)`; `blush(cx, cy, rx, ry, colour, angle, strength)`; `dashes(path, colour, spacing, length, width, angle, along)` (`angle=90` crosses the path, `along` hangs the bars to one side — fringes); `smear(region, colour, angle, length, width)`; `wisp(x, y, height, sway, colour, width)`.
- Features: `eye(cx, cy, w, h, look, lid, side, rot, lower, lash, iris, crease)` (`side` the outer corner's direction, `look` −1..1); `brow(path, width)`; `lips(cx, cy, w, h, smile, rot, side, parting)` (`side` biases the lip peaks to one side — for three-quarter views); `hair(region, flow, colours, n, length, width, bend, pressure, dry, taper, roots, clip)`.
- Finish: `finish(tooth, relief, grain, soft)`; `render()`, `composite()`, `stage(name)`, `save(path, stages_dir)`; `save('x.jpg')` defaults to quality 88, 4:4:4 chroma.
- Colours from `PAINTS` (paper white black charcoal warmgrey grey; navy_deep navy navy_light indigo cobalt cool_deep cool cool_light mist; saffron_deep saffron marigold yellow butter orange ochre; skin_pale peach_light peach peach_deep skin_tan skin_brown skin_deep blush blush_light vermilion lip lip_dark; olive moss kraft kraft_dark rust) or '#rrggbb'.
- The example's scene script precomputes every mask (`plan()`), then computes each layer's visible part from front-to-back occlusion (fingers > the near glove > thumb > cup and lid > the far glove > both sleeves > the scarf's hanging end > the two scarf coils > face > ear > neck > hair > coat); the hem and sleeves run out with `spill` while `avoid`ing what is in front of them (or they would run into the gaps left by the gloves and the cup); masks store as uint8 on assignment and whole shapes no longer needed are dropped once occlusion is computed.

## Sketchbook doodle — `Sketchbook`
- Idea: a sketchbook drawing painted in order (blank paper → a hair-thin ink line drawing → hair strand by strand → colour-pencil / marker stripes → the other colours → props → the face → floating bits); the drawing occupies one small patch of the page and the rest stays empty: no ground, no background, no frame.
  - Paper: warm off-white with a faint blush (`#f2e9e1`), a histogram-equalised fine tooth `T` (colour pencil catches on it; ink breaks slightly in the deepest pits), a very faint large-scale mottle and a few stray fibres; the blank corner's value range about 1.9 steps (0.1–3.0 in the reference), paper mean (242, 233, 225) — inside the reference's range.
  - Ink: the path is splined, resampled at 1 px, then pushed along its normal by a slow hand wobble (`wobble` px, `wscale` its length scale) plus a fast tremor (`tremor`); the landing eases in over a short stretch (`taper[0]`), the lift-off a long pointed flick (`taper[1]`), width drifting ±12 % slowly in between, ink flow drifting too (`flow`); `gaps` lifts the pen now and then, breaking a few px. Many lines at once: grouped by colour, each line cut into constant-width, constant-darkness runs, Pillow drawing run by run into a 4× supersampled layer (`joint='curve'`), box-filtered back, breaking slightly in the deepest pits (`grain`), then multiplied in — ink is transparent, the colour beneath survives, and lines over colour stay black. `clip` / `avoid` confine lines to a region or steer them around what is in front (`grow()` expands the front things' masks a few px so lines stop just short). `contour()` draws a closed shape as one to three strokes, joins either leaving a small gap or overlapping — never a seamless vector ring.
  - Colour pencil / marker: given a coverage target (a colour band, a shape), build (u, s) coordinates along the stroke and read a tileable noise texture: streaks stretched long along the stroke (each its own pressure) plus a layer of along-stroke fine grain; pigment catching the tooth peaks (`grain` high: colour pencil, white specks left) or lying flat (`grain` low: marker, streaks only); `hatch` adds a visible layer of parallel lines (one every `spacing` px). Colours multiply, so red over yellow turns orange, like real pencil.
  - Stripes: `stripes()` lays colour bands along the coordinate s — `across` gives the angle (90 = horizontal bands top to bottom), `bend` bends them into a smile (+) or a frown (−), `path` gives a centre line (a sleeve: stripes wrap the arm, s the arc length projected onto it); each band takes the next colour (`order='cycle'` or 'random'), a width in `band` (or a `widths` list), a paper gap in `gap`, its own tilt (`tilt`), grain and pressure; both edges wander on their own noise (`rough` px); both ends re-found on the clothes mask's blurred copy by their own threshold, so each band overshoots the ink a little or falls short (about `overrun` px), the whole layer shiftable a few px (`shift`, mis-registration).
  - Props: `leaf()` a leaf: an ink or coloured outline (a small gap at the tip), a midrib, pairs of diagonal veins toward the tip (herringbone), optional offset pencil fill, or veins left white in the fill (`vein='white'`); `flower()` a little flower: 'rose' a doodled spiral plus two loose petals, 'daisy' a ring of petals round a yellow dot, 'tulip' a pencil-filled three-pointed cup, optional stem and leaves; `tree()` a thin-line tree: a trunk of a few roughly parallel wobbling lines, branching upward with twigs, a small fork at each tip, optional blossom dots; `steam()` a lazy S rising, a loose loop at the top (`curl` the loop count), `strands` > 1 adds fainter shorter uncurled wisps; `ticks()` short furry ticks in a region (cat fur, grass); `squiggle()` a little doodle on blank paper: a spiral, a four-point star or a small ring — never fake writing.
  - Features: `eye_closed()` a downward arc (a closed eye) plus lashes at the outer corner heading down and out; `eye_dot()` a small black dot; `mouth()` a marker-red little mouth (two peaks on the upper lip, a rounder lower, a darker line between); `blush()` a round pink blush: a clear but soft edge, fine grain, darker at the rim (`rim`); `hair()` a lock: n fine lines interpolated between two or more guide curves (root to tip, ordered across the lock), each starting near the root (`start`), ending at its own length (`length`), waving by `wave` (amplitude px, period px) — neighbours nearly in phase (`cohere` turns per crossing), so they wave together instead of tangling — the wave growing from root (`wave_root`) to tip, the tip tapering over a long stretch; `stray` the few that fly.
  - Finish: `finish()` records parameters and applies them in render (every stage snapshot matches the final; call before the first stroke): the faintest paper grain and the slightest softness.
- Construction: `Sketchbook(W, H, seed, keep_stages=True, stages_dir=None)` (total pixels capped at 4096²), `finish()` first, then `paper(tone, tooth, mottle, fibres)`. Coordinates are pixels of the whole canvas, angles degrees (0 right, 90 down).
- Masks: `poly(pts, smooth)`, `ellipse(cx, cy, rx, ry, rot)`, `blob(cx, cy, rx, ry, rough)`, `tube(path, w0, w1)` (`w0` a width list along the path) return canvas-sized float masks; `tube_pts()`, `ellipse_pts()`, `closed_pts()` return outline points (for `ink()` / `contour()`); `soften(mask, sigma)`, `grow(mask, px)`.
- Lines: `ink(path, width, colour, alpha, wobble, wscale, tremor, taper, wvar, flow, grain, gaps, smooth, closed, clip, avoid)`; `inks(paths, ...)` many at once (`width` / `alpha` may be (lo, hi) or per-line lists, `colour` a per-line list of names or a random group); `contour(pts, pieces, gap, overlap, p_gap, smooth, closed, ...)`.
- Colour: `stripes(mask, colours, across, path, bend, band, gap, tilt, rough, overrun, grain, streak, pressure, shift, start, order, widths, clip, avoid)` returns each band's (s0, s1, colour); `tint(mask, colour, angle, strength, grain, streak, pressure, hatch, spacing, overrun, shift, edge, clip, avoid)`; `blush(cx, cy, r, colour, strength, ry, grain, rim)`; `dots(pts, r, colour, strength)`.
- Features: `eye_closed(cx, cy, w, sag, lashes, side, rot, width)` (`side` the outer corner's direction); `eye_dot(cx, cy, r)`; `mouth(cx, cy, w, h, colour, rot, smile)`; `hair(guides, n, width, colour, wave, wave_root, length, start, spread, stray, alpha, taper, jitter, cohere, clip, avoid)`.
- Props: `leaf(base, tip, width, fill, line, veins, vein, vein_colour, bend, asym, stem, lw, fill_shift, strength, grain, hatch, rib)` returns the leaf's mask; `flower(x, y, r, colour, kind, stem, stem_colour, leaves, lw)`; `tree(x, y, height, colour, lines, branches, lean, spread, width, trunk, blossoms, n_blossom)`; `steam(x, y, height, colour, sway, curl, width, alpha, strands, lean)`; `ticks(mask, n, length, angle, spread, width, colour, alpha)`; `squiggle(x, y, size, colour, kind)`.
- Finish: `finish(grain, soft)`; `render()`, `composite()`, `stage(name)`, `save(path, stages_dir)`; `save('x.jpg')` defaults to quality 88, 4:4:4 chroma.
- Colours from `PAINTS` (paper paper_light white; ink ink_soft sepia graphite grey forest_ink; red scarlet vermilion coral orange yellow gold mustard teal turquoise aqua blue cobalt navy sky magenta pink rose violet; cheek blush lip; leaf olive sage moss forest terracotta ochre tan wood cream ginger stone) or '#rrggbb'.
- The example's scene script precomputes all masks (`plan()`, stored as uint8) and passes every layer's predecessors' masks `grow()`n a little as `avoid` when inking (book and fingers > sleeve > hair > face > neck > sweater > skirt > legs and shoes > stool), also `avoid`ing them when colouring; hair only avoids the "visible face" (the face ellipse minus above the hairline), so the forehead is covered by hair; when a pale colour must sit on a dark one (a daisy clip in black hair, a yellow sun on a teal book cover), the spot is reserved inside the dark first — colours multiply, so layering would only darken.

## Flat spot illustration — `SpotIllustration`
- Idea: in the Japanese spot-illustration manner, several small scenes scattered on white, each stacked back to front in flat opaque colour (far legs and arms → near legs → neck and top → head and hair → near arms and hands, props slipped between layers with `hooks`), then shared little marks added at the end (rain, steam, surprise dashes, z); no ground and no background, at most a puddle or a sliver of shadow.
  - Shapes: one colour is one closed outline (points). Before cutting, resampled by arc length and pushed along its normal by one period of low-frequency undulation plus a finer layer (`wobble` the amplitude in px × k; `lumps` the wavelength — short for hair, more bumps), so circles carry little lumps and straight edges "breathe"; Pillow fills in a 3× supersampled bounding-box-sized patch (`Patch`), edges thresholded by fine noise, slightly furry. Pigment opaque: new = old + mask × (colour − old); `clip=` / `avoid=` confine a shape inside / outside another.
  - White gaps: `gap=` carves a white seam around a shape before it lands — the paper showing between two same-colour shapes (an arm on a sweater, trousers under a coat, a cushion and armrest on a sofa); the gap is the shape's mask blurred and thresholded by meso-scale noise, so it varies in width, and beyond `gap_keep` that part of the edge has no seam.
  - Lines: fine bands pointed at both ends with round caps, along a slightly wobbling path, 4× supersampled. `fold()` looks at what is beneath: white lines carved into dark and mid cloth (luminance < 0.74), a line one shade darker on pale cloth; `cut()` is just a white line.
