  - People: assembled from simple parts back to front (far legs, far arms and hands, near legs, neck and top, head and hair, near arms and hands; crouching, the near legs in front of the top): `limb()` a sleeve or trouser leg is one thick tube, cuff / hem cut square; `torso()` a loose boxy top, assembled in its own frame (so lying down still works); `head()` a paper-white face, one profile line (nose tip, lips, chin), a dot eye, a lumpy hair mass and two or three white strands cut in; `hand()` a mitten-like white hand, four finger bumps on the front edge, fine outline, red lines between fingers; `shoe()` a sneaker, or a socked foot. `person()` draws a whole figure from a pose preset ('stand' 'walk' 'sit' 'crouch' 'reach'): `rel` overrides any joint in head units, `rot` turns the whole figure (−90 lying on the back, head left), elbows, knees, shins and hems gain folds automatically, `hooks` slips a prop between two layers (an umbrella shaft under the near hand, a book under the near arm, an armchair's armrest over the sitting person).
  - Props and marks: mugs, bowls, pots, tote bags, umbrellas, bicycles, benches, chairs, armchairs (the near armrest returned separately, drawn after the person), sofas, stools, tables, stoves / cabinets, potted plants, books (open, closed, tented), a sitting cat, a curled sleeping cat, windows; steam S-curves, three surprise dashes, z's, raindrops, motion dashes, a small shadow.
  - Finish: `finish()` records parameters and applies them in render (every stage snapshot matches the final; call before the first stroke): a soft mottle and fine grain in the colours, a few pale specks, a very faint tooth on the blank paper (about 0.5 steps in a blank corner, 0.44–0.48 in the reference), the slightest softness.
- Construction: `SpotIllustration(W, H, seed, keep_stages=True, stages_dir=None)`, `finish()` first, then `paper(tone)`. Coordinates are pixels of the whole canvas; `k` = min(W, H) / 1000 scales default widths and wobbles; a person's size `u` is one head height (px), pose-preset joints in u units, origin on the ground between the feet, facing right (`facing=-1` mirrors).
- Outlines: `ellipse(cx, cy, rx, ry, rot)`, `rrect(x0, y0, x1, y1, r)`, `blob(cx, cy, rx, ry, rough, rot)`, `curve(pts)` (closed spline), `tube(path, widths, cap)` returns points.
- Colour: `shape(pts, colour, smooth, wobble, rough, gap, gap_keep, clip, avoid, alpha, lumps)` returns a `Patch`; `knock(pts)` a paper-white shape; `line(path, width, colour, taper, wobble, clip, avoid, smooth, closed, tip)`; `cut(path, width)`; `fold(path, width, colour, clip)`; `dot(x, y, r, colour)`; `union(*patches)`, `grow(patch, px)`, `under(path)` (the average colour beneath a path).
- People: `limb(path, widths, colour, cap, gap, gap_keep)`; `torso(sh_back, sh_front, hem_back, hem_front, colour, facing, collar, bulge, gap, hood)`; `head(cx, cy, r, facing, turn, tilt, hair, hair_colour, eye, ear, strands, skin, line, mouth, fringe, hat)` returns face points (chin, nape, eye, nose, mouth, crown); `hair(head, style, colour, strands, fringe, hat)` ('bob' 'short' 'bun' 'long' 'cap'); `neck(top, bottom, w)`; `hand(x, y, angle, size, pose, facing, finger)` ('grip' 'fist' 'open' 'flat' 'cup'); `shoe(x, y, angle, size, colour, facing, sole, line, laces)` (`line=None` a socked foot); `pose(name, x, y, u, facing, rot, rel, **joints)` returns joints; `person(x, y, u, facing, pose, outfit, hooks, rel, rot, J, order, folds, **joints)`, `outfit` giving top bottom hair hair_style shoes sole laces socks eye turn tilt skin hood sleeve apron hand_n hand_f strands fringe hat (`shoes=None` socked, `hand_n=None` omits that hand).
- Props: `mug(x, y, h)`, `bowl(x, y, w, fill)`, `pot(x, y, w)`, `bag(x, y, w, h)`, `umbrella(x, y, r, angle, handle_len)`, `bicycle(x, y, size, facing)`, `bench(x0, x1, y, h)`, `chair(x, y, h, facing)`, `armchair(x, y, w, h, facing)` (the returned `'arm'` is a function, called after the person), `sofa(x0, x1, y, h)`, `stool(x, y, w, h)`, `table(x0, x1, y, h)`, `stove(x0, x1, y, h)`, `plant(x, y, h, pot, leaf)`, `book(x, y, w, angle, open_)` (`open_` True / False / 'tent'), `cat_sit(x, y, h, facing, head_down, eye)`, `cat_curl(x, y, w)`, `window(x0, y0, x1, y1)`; furniture `y` is the floor.
- Marks: `steam(x, y, h, n)`, `bang(x, y, r, angle)`, `zzz(x, y, size)`, `rain(x0, y0, x1, y1, n, slant)`, `ticks(x, y, r, angle, n)`, `shadow(cx, cy, rx, ry)`.
- Finish: `finish(grain, speckle, soft)`; `render()`, `composite()`, `stage(name)`, `save(path, stages_dir)`; `save('x.jpg')` defaults to quality 92, 4:4:4 chroma.
- Colours from `PAINTS` (paper white ink line black; navy_deep navy blue slate powder powder_light; mustard mustard_light olive green sage; grey_light grey grey_mid grey_dark cream skin wood wood_light; red red_deep red_line pink) or '#rrggbb'.
- Each vignette in the example is a function `f(s, x, y, u)` (origin on the ground, u the head height), so the same five vignettes lay out as a portrait sheet (`--layout sheet`, 1500×2000) or 16:9 (`--layout wide`, 1920×1080); rain, steam, surprise dashes and z's are collected into `MARKS` and added once all five are drawn.

## Grainy picture book — `GrainBook`
- Idea: after the "sweet children's-book illustration" class on Midjourney: a full-bleed page, a cream ground laid first, then big flat shapes back to front (sky → far hills → cottage and trees → snow → figures → animals → accent lines → falling snow), every shape one "dry chalky grain brush" fill; no outlines — shapes meet colour to colour, with a few dark crayon accent strokes; light a lighter dry scumble on one side, shadow a flat darker shape pressed on top.
  - Texture: per-page shared textures computed once — tooth `T` (near-histogram-equalised, uint8), grain `G` (pixel noise plus an about 1 px clustered layer), the meso-scale noise for wandering edges, large mottle, a speck map; the stroke-direction streak texture is built per 7.5° and cached on first use: bicubic-upscaled on very elongated random grids (about 26 × 3.6, 11 × 1.6, 80 × 13 px × k), each cell becoming one streak, three layers summed, rotated to the stroke angle, stored as uint8 tiles larger than the frame, read at a random offset per fill, so two fills at the same angle never repeat their streaks.
  - Shapes: a `Region` is a bounding-box-sized float mask plus an offset (small shapes occupy small arrays), Pillow fills polygons at 2× and averages back; `|` union, `&` intersection, `-` subtract.
  - Fill `fill()`: inside the padded bounds, the mask lightly blurred and thresholded against "streaks + tooth + meso noise", edges fraying into a crumbly fringe along the stroke (whiskers at head and tail, wavy along-stroke edges), the frayed ring a shade darker (`rim`); pressure falling toward the edge (`dry`, `dry_px` the range), against the tooth / streak mix (`skip` the streaks' share) colour skipping sporadically inside and thinning at the edge, exposing the layer beneath; the colour itself biased to a lighter `hi` on bright streaks and a darker `lo` on dark ones (`streak`), plus fine grain and large mottle. Opaque: new = old + A × (colour − old); `mode='multiply'` a pressed-on shadow, `'screen'` light.
  - Sky and ground: `bands()` steps the sky level by level, each level's edge a wavy dry fringe, each colour dry-brushed up into the one above (`blend`) — painted steps, never a digital gradient; `mottle()` loose patches of a second colour; `scumble()` a low-pressure dry scumble skipping along the streaks (lights, roof snow, pale patches on snow); `shadow()` a flat multiply shadow; `glow()` a two-tier screen glow with dry edges.
  - Lines: `brush()` one dry stroke tapering both ends (filled along its direction); `crayon()` dark crayon accents: the path wobbling, width drifting, broken by tooth; `dots()` furry grainy dots (snow, stars, crumbs).
  - Finish: `finish()` records parameters and applies them in render (every stage snapshot matches the final; call before the first stroke): page-wide fine grain, sparse dark and pale specks (more bright on dark, more dark on pale), the slightest softness.
- Construction: `GrainBook(W, H, seed, keep_stages=True, stages_dir=None)`, `finish()` first, then `paper(tone)`. Coordinates are picture pixels, angles 0° right, 90° down; `k` = min(W, H) / 1000 scales default widths and texture scales.
- Shapes: `poly(pts, smooth)`, `ellipse(cx, cy, rx, ry, rot)`, `blob(cx, cy, rx, ry, rough, rot, seed)`, `rect(x0, y0, x1, y1)`, `tube(path, w0, w1, taper)`, `below(xs, ys)` / `above(xs, ys)` (below / above a curve), `full()`, all returning a `Region`; `wavy(x0, x1, y, amp, period, seed, slope)` gives an undulating horizon `(xs, ys)`.
- Colour: `fill(region, colour, angle, streak, grain, mottle, pressure, dry, edge, soft, hi, lo, alpha, clip, avoid, mode, seed, dry_px, rim, skip)`; `scumble(region, colour, angle, cover)`; `shadow(region, colour, depth, angle)`; `glow(region, colour, strength)`; `bands(region, colours, ys, angle, wave, period, blend)`; `mottle(region, colour, scale, cover, angle)`; `brush(path, width, colour, taper)`; `crayon(path, width, colour, pressure, taper, wobble)`; `dots(pts, r, colour)`.
- Faces: `face(cx, cy, r, turn, eyes, look, skin, cheeks, mouth, nose, brows, ears, eye_size)` returns head, chin, eye, nose, mouth positions (`eyes` 'big' 'dot' 'closed'); `eye_big(cx, cy, w, h, look, highlight, lid)`, `eye_dot(cx, cy, r)`, `cheek(cx, cy, r)`, `nose(cx, cy, size, kind)` ('button' 'tri' 'line' 'dot'), `mouth(cx, cy, w, kind)` ('smile' 'lips' 'o' 'open'), `brow(cx, cy, w, tilt)`; `hair(cx, cy, r, style, colour, turn, layer, length)` ('bob' 'long' 'short' 'bun', `layer` 'back' then 'front'); `beanie(cx, cy, r, colour, band, pompom, turn)`.
- People: `limb(path, w0, w1, colour)`, `mitten(x, y, r, colour, angle, thumb)`, `boot(x, y, w, colour, facing)`; `child(x, y, h, facing, pose, outfit, hooks, **joints)` draws a child about h tall (`pose` 'stand' 'walk', origin on the ground between the feet; 'sit' origin on the seat edge under the hips, legs dangling), `outfit` giving coat sleeve legs boots hair hair_style hair_len hat hat_band scarf mittens skin eyes eye_size mouth nose turn look collar buttons brows, `hooks` slipping things in at 'behind' 'hand_far' 'before_head' 'hand_near' 'end', joints overridable in h units, returns the joints (including `face`'s points).
- Props: `house(x, y, w, h, wall, side, roof, snow, depth, window, door, chimney)` (returns ridge, chimney mouth, windows), `pine(x, y, h, w, colour, light, snow, lean)`, `fir_far(x, y, h, w, colour)`, `round_tree(x, y, h, w, crown, light, trunk)`, `bush(x, y, w, h, colour, light)`, `shrub(x, y, h, w, colour, light, snow)`, `cup(x, y, w, colour, inside, rim, handle, light, steam)`, `parcel(x, y, w, h, colour, top, string, bow)`, `bag(x, y, w, h, colour)`, `potted(x, y, h, pot, leaf)`, `reeds(x, y, h, n, colour, head, spread)`, `tuft(x, y, w, h, colour)`, `lamp(x, y, h, colour, light, glow)`, `dog(x, y, s, facing, colour, patch, ear, collar)` (returns collar, nose, head), `duck(x, y, s, facing, colour, wing, beak)`, `fence(x0, x1, y, h, colour)`.
- Sky and marks: `snow(x0, y0, x1, y1, n, r, colour, alpha, avoid)`, `stars(x0, y0, x1, y1, n, colour)`, `moon(x, y, r, colour, halo, crescent)`, `smoke(x, y, h, colour, n, drift, alpha)`, `wisp(x, y, h, colour)`, `ripples(x, y, w, n, colour)`, `footprints(pts, size, colour, depth)`.
- Finish: `finish(grain, speckle, soft)`; `render()`, `composite()`, `stage(name)`, `save(path, stages_dir)`; `save('x.jpg')` defaults to quality 92, 4:4:4 chroma.
- Colours from `PAINTS` (paper cream white cream_dark; navy cobalt_deep cobalt cobalt_bright ultramarine sky_pale dusty dusty_light dusty_dark teal ice; vermilion tomato red_deep brick orange orange_light apricot; pine pine_mid green moss olive sage; mustard window lamp gold; snow snow_light snow_shade snow_blue; skin skin_shade cheek nose lip; hair black ink brown brown_light wood wood_dark kraft coffee shadow shadow_blue) or '#rrggbb'.
- The evening walk keeps all positions in `LAYOUTS`, one `scene()` drawing the portrait page (`--layout page`, 1600×2000) or 16:9 (`--layout wide`, 1920×1080); falling snow `avoid=`s the child's face, snow never landing on the eyes.

## Dreamy hills — `DreamHills`
- Idea: after the "minimal dreamy hills wallpaper" class on Midjourney: a 1:2 portrait, the sky over half, below it giant hills laid one by one far to near (short grass and haze far, long grass near), each hill first a luminous colour field then tens of thousands of slope-following grass strokes; one very small person on a ridge; last the foreground grass band and wildflowers, bloom and film grain.
  - Hills: `ridge(pts)` a Catmull-Rom ridge through control points, sampled per column (`.y`, slope `.dy`, `.at(x)`); `hill(ridge, front=[nearer ridges])` paints back to front, using `front` to know where it is hidden (not painted, no grass there) and where the troughs are.
  - Light: a pseudo normal per pixel — on the ridge the silhouette's outward normal (into the frame), turning toward the viewer down the face by 1 − exp(−d / `foot`) while keeping which side the hill faces (the ridge slope smoothed across the face, a dome's left half facing left all the way); `swell` then bends the normal with a noise height field laid out by (x, distance from ridge), contours parallel to the ridge — the soft bands of light following every hill. Two lights: the key `key_dir` (x right, y up, z toward the viewer; negative z backlit) with a wrapping diffuse, skylight strengthening as the normal rises; a crest rim on the sunward side; above every nearer ridge a trough occlusion that takes the key first (ao²) and the skylight less, so troughs not only darken but cool. Total light becomes a value through 1 − exp(−E × `expo`), the colour the `warm` ramp at that value mixed toward `cool` by the sky's share (a smoothstep, narrow at night) — the two ramps are the luminous surreal colour field: lavender / apricot, saffron / pink, ember / navy.
  - Grass: a base layer a little darker than the grass first (its top edge shrinking from the ridge), then strokes: direction the silhouette normal on the crest (the fringe standing against the sky), turning vertical downward, plus wind and a slowly turning swirl; each blade lit with its own normal (the surface normal jittered by the palette's `jitter`, so grass alternates warm and cool), the root half-occluded from the key (dark, cool), the tip biased toward the key. Strokes are not drawn one by one with Pillow but "splatted": points sampled along each blade (across it too when wider than 1 px), bilinear weights, `np.bincount` accumulating coverage and colour sums; sorted by root y (jittered about one blade length, or chunks would band), composited chunk by chunk from back hills to front, near grass over far.
  - Sky: `sky()` a multi-stop vertical gradient (mixed in linear light, wandering slightly) with an optional sun glow; `cloud()` a cumulus: puffs arrayed on a dome's front, the dome height undulating along x into one or two rounded heads, a row of overlapping large puffs at the bottom holding it up, big below and small above, small puffs breaking out of the upper half (only hugging the body, never floating); each pixel takes the highest ball, the height field lightly blurred and slope-limited (puffs melting together, occlusions soft rather than a dark crease), a wrapping diffuse for the key, crevices darkened, the lower half cooled; coverage the union of each puff's own soft-edged disc, cut flat at the bottom. `stars()` power-law brightness, the brightest with four-point spikes; `milky_way()` a bent cloudy band, brighter in the middle, with a dark rift and star dust; `comet()` a bright spiky head plus a widening tail of fine-grain streaks.
  - Finish: `finish()` records parameters and applies them in render (every stage snapshot matches the final; call before the first stroke): the slightest softness, bloom on the brightest, brightness-weighted fine film grain plus a little chroma grain (dithering the sky too, so the gradient never bands).
- Construction: `DreamHills(W, H, seed, palette='lilac', keep_stages=True, stages_dir=None)`, `palette` 'meadow' 'lilac' 'saffron' 'ember' or a dict in `PALETTES` format (sky stops, `key_dir`, the `warm` / `cool` ramps, rim light, fog, cloud lit and shade colours, foreground grass, flowers, the walker's clothes, the weights); `finish()` first, then paint. Coordinates are picture pixels, y down; `k` = min(W, H) / 1000. The canvas starts as a warm-grey blank (the GIF's first frame).
- Sky: `sky(stops, sun_xy, glow, glow_r, glow_strength, wander)`; `cloud(cx, base, w, h, seed, n, detail, light, lit, shade, warm, warm_k, alpha, wisp, flat, towers)` (`base` the flat cloud base, `h` the height above it, `light` the light direction, `warm` warming the sunward faces); `stars(n, y0, y1, x0, x1, size, bright, spikes, colour, fade, seed)` (`fade=(y_a, y_b)` thinning toward the horizon); `sparkle(x, y, size, colour, strength, angle)`; `milky_way(p0, p1, width, bend, strength, colour, warm, rift, stars, seed)`; `comet(x, y, angle, length, width, colour, head, strength, spread, seed)` (angles 0° right, 90° down — the tail's direction).
- Hills: `ridge(pts)` returns a `Ridge`; `hill(ridge, front, foot, blade, density, warm, cool, haze, swell, swell_scale, rim, rim_w, trough, trough_w, wind, flow, lean, seed, jitter, tip, root, base_dark, fur)` — `blade` grass length (px, 3–6 far hills, 10–18 near), `foot` the distance over which the normal turns to the viewer, `swell` / `swell_scale=(along, down)` the light bands, `rim` the crest rim, `trough` the trough occlusion, `wind` which way grass leans (degrees), `flow` the swirl, `lean` how much the crest fringe follows the silhouette normal, `warm` / `cool` per-hill ramps.
- Else: `trees(ridge, xs, h, colour, light, haze, kind, seed)` (little trees on far hills, 'round' / 'tall'); `foreground(top, bottom, density, blade, colour, tip, flowers, flower_colours, flower_size, wind, blur_px, seed, curve)` (the bottom grass band: dark roots, backlit tips, brighter higher, flowers on the tallest tips, the whole band slightly defocused); `walker(x, y, h, facing, shirt, trousers, skin, hair, stride, rim, shadow, grass, bag, lamp, lamp_glow)` (feet at (x, y), h px tall, head / hair / clothes / striding legs / swinging arms drawn at 8× and reduced, a rim on the lit side, a soft shadow on the grass, `grass=colour` a few blades before the feet, `lamp=colour` a glowing little lantern warming the grass around it).
- Output: `render()`, `composite()`, `stage(name)`, `save(path, stages_dir)`; `save('x.jpg')` defaults to quality 92, 4:4:4 chroma.
- Colours from `PAINTS` (sky: navy night midnight cobalt azure sky sky_pale cornflower lavender lilac mist peach_sky apricot_sky rose_sky; ground: grass grass_deep moss cream peach apricot saffron marigold ember rust violet indigo plum; light: white warm_white gold pink_light ice; people and flowers: shirt_blue shirt_red shirt_white shirt_yellow trousers skin hair cornflower_blue flower_white flower_coral flower_yellow) or '#rrggbb'.
- The long walk keeps three times of day × two layouts in `SCENES` (sky stops, cloud or night sky, ridge control points far to near and each hill's parameters, far trees, which hill the walker is on, the foreground band), one `scene()` drawing the portrait wallpaper (`--layout page`, 1200×2400) or 16:9 (`--layout wide`, 1920×1080).

## Sunlit greenery — `SunlitGreen`
- Idea: after the "summer full of green" of anime background art and digital gouache: everything a hard-edged flat shape, layered far to near (sky, far trees, mid trees, near trees, the house, light, near plants); greenery is thousands of crisp flat leaves layered by light and shade; whites painted lit first then covered with cool leaf shadow (or shaded first, then warm sun patches cut out); water last, reflecting what was already painted.
  - Rasteriser: a batch of polygons filled with ids into a 2× supersampled id map (Pillow), ids looked up for colour and opacity, then 2×2 averaged back: edges hard and anti-aliased; list order is coverage order; a leaf costs microseconds, a picture tens of thousands of leaves. Polygon edges are subdivided then gently jittered (`_wobble`) — painted edges, not ruler edges.
  - Leaf masses `canopy()`: clumps (cx, cy, rx, ry[, shade]) are big domes packed with sub-clumps (radius about `sub` leaves), each sub-clump a small dome bright on its sunward face; sub-clump and big-dome normals mixed, plus noise, darkening downward and occlusion in the gaps, gives a value cut by `levels` into flat greens (`tones`, dark → bright). Each tone painted dark to bright: `fill=1` (far and mid trees) one solid shape, `fill=0` (near trees) leaf-paved only (the darkest layer always a solid base); each layer's whole edge stamped with a ring of outward leaf clusters, so bright masses sit over dark with leafy edges and the silhouette is all leaves; `mottle` swaps some leaves a tone up or down (bright and dark interleaved); `airy` carves the clump's outer zone with coarse noise, edges breaking into sprays, sky showing through the middle; `holes` punches sky holes inside (taking that height's sky colour, brightened), a few leaves pressed over each hole's rim, `twigs` showing twigs in them; `glint` scatters pale highlights on the brightest leaves; `hole_colour` substitutes the wall colour for sky, for indoor plants.
  - Near leaves `spray()`: branches (points, root to tip) carrying big leaves, a rosette at the tip; each leaf split along its midrib into two flat halves, the sunward half a tone brighter, sunlit leaves with a pale midrib and a fine warm edge, shaded leaves a dark midrib; shaded painted first, sunlit over. `grass()` clumps of flat blades; `limb()` trunks and branches with a bright band on the sunward side, `bark` adding along-the-grain bark strokes.
  - Light: `leaf_shadow()` a dappled leaf-shadow mask — a low-frequency field gives the big shadow masses, shadow edges ringed with outward leaf clusters, a few leaf-shadow sprays scattered in the sun, small sun flecks punched inside; `squash` flattens it for floors in perspective. `shade()` lays cool shadow on what is painted (a blue-grey multiply: cream to blue-grey, warm stone to violet-grey, yellow-green grass to teal); `sunlight()` lays warm sun (screen against cream, `tint` multiplying a colour, a deeper amber for evening); `dapple()` does both over a region in one step. Two uses: paint lit colour then cover with leaf shadow (`mode='shade'`), or paint shade first then a sun patch (say a window shape) with leaf shadow cut out of it (`mode='sun'` or `sunlight()` directly).
  - Solids `soft()`: any outline inflated to a height field first (pillowy edges, `dome` rounder, `facets` cutting stone faces with a few random planes), lit and cut into flat tones; `levels='q'` cuts by quantiles so every tone appears; `warm` a thin warm line at the terminator; `clean` merges tone islands too small to read (or they look like holes). Built on it: `rock()` (sun-bleached boulders and moss caps; with `wet=` standing in water: the underwater part dyed the water colour, its own broken reflection and a bright ripple line at the waterline), `cushion()` (puffy cushions, stripable or checkered), `pot()` (vertical bands by cylinder shading); plus `box()`, `panel()` (a recessed door panel), `face()`, `window()` (a white frame: the lit face plus a cool side, returning the glass mask), `curtain()` (sheer curtain folds), `boards()` (one-point-perspective floorboards), `book()`, `mug()`, `gradient()`, `strokes()` (visible strokes in a region).
  - Water `water()`: the pebbled bed first, seen through transparent teal (deeper and bluer the farther); then the picture above `line` flipped (stretched by `reflect_scale`), each thin horizontal band offset a little, smeared sideways, flattened to a dozen-odd colours, then cut by a layer of horizontal pointed strokes: far merging into a mirror, near breaking into sparse clusters showing the bed; finally a few pale sky glints. Paint everything it will mirror first.
  - Sky: `sky()` a three-stop gradient (remembered, so sky holes in canopies take their colour by height); `cloud()` a small cumulus: puffs on a flat-bottomed dome, the height field melted, the noise-twisted boundary cut into three flat tones, edges hard and slightly broken, small holes filled.
  - Finish: `finish()` records parameters and applies them in render (every stage snapshot matches the final; call before the first stroke): bloom on the brightest, a lift toward pale air, a very faint tooth and fine grain.
- Construction: `SunlitGreen(W, H, seed, keep_stages=True, stages_dir=None, light=(-0.55, -0.7, 0.45))`, `light` the direction toward the sun (x right, y down, z toward the viewer); `finish()` first, then paint. Coordinates are picture pixels, y down; `k` = min(W, H) / 1000. The canvas starts as warm-white paper (the GIF's first frame).
- Sky: `sky(top, mid, bottom, y0, y1, mid_at)`, `sky_at(y)`; `cloud(cx, base, w, h, seed, light, tones, n, levels, rough, merge)` (`base` the flat cloud base).
- Plants: `canopy(clumps, tones, leaf, light, holes, far, sub, levels, noise, bottom_dark, blend, haze, edge_leaves, sprinkle, width, shape, hole_size, fill, glint, mottle, twigs, twig_colour, hole_colour, airy, seed)` — `leaf` leaf length (× k, 7–10 far trees, 20–24 near), `far` mixing toward haze; `spray(stems, leaf, width, tones, rosette, along, droop, bend, light, rim, rim_w, midrib, stem, stem_w, far, seed, lit_bias)`; `grass(x0, x1, y, h, n, tones, lean, width, far, curve, seed, ys)`; `limb(path, w0, w1, colour, lit, lit_w, alpha, bark, seed)`.
- Light: `leaf_shadow(box, cover, leaf, squash, scale, flecks, seed)` returns a mask; `shade(mask, tint, strength, add, add_k)`; `sunlight(mask, colour, strength, tint)`; `dapple(region, cover, leaf, squash, scale, flecks, mode, strength, tint, colour, seed)` returns the mask used.
- Solids: `soft(pts, tones, light, bevel, levels, noise, wobble, warm, warm_w, facets, flat, dome, clean, seed, clip)`, `rock(cx, cy, rx, ry, tones, facets, rough, flat_base, moss, warm, bevel, wet, seed, light)`, `face(pts, colour, alpha, wobble, seed)`, `box(front, top, side, tones)` (returns the three faces' masks), `panel(x0, y0, x1, y1, colour, lit, shade, bevel)`, `window(x0, y0, x1, y1, cols, rows, frame, bar, colour, lit, shade, depth)` (`rows` may be a list of rail positions), `cushion(cx, cy, w, h, tones, round_, tilt, bulge, stripes, light, bevel, seed)` (`stripes=(colour, count, width, angle[, True adds a crossed set])`), `curtain(x0, x1, y0, y1, folds, tones, alpha, sway, seed)`, `boards(y0, y1, vp, n, x_span, tones, gap, gap_w, seed)`, `pot(cx, top, w, h, colour, rim, light)`, `book(cx, cy, w, h, open_, cover, tilt)`, `mug(cx, base, w, h, colour, handle, tea)`; plus `fill`, `poly`, `mask`, `gradient`, `strokes`.
- Water: `water(box, line, mask, deep, mid, shallow, reflect, near_reflect, stroke, density, pebbles, pebble_size, ripple, sky_glints, reflect_scale, seed)` returns the water mask.
- Output: `render()`, `composite()`, `stage(name)`, `save(path, stages_dir)`; `save('x.jpg')` defaults to quality 92, 4:4:4 chroma.
- Colours from `PAINTS` (sky: sky_top sky sky_low sky_hole haze cloud cloud_half cloud_shade; leaves: leaf_deep leaf_shade leaf_mid leaf_light leaf_sun leaf_glint; bark: bark bark_light; whites: white_sun white white_half white_shade white_deep; wood: wood_sun wood wood_shade wood_deep; stone: stone_sun stone stone_half stone_shade stone_deep warm_edge; moss: moss_sun moss moss_shade; water: water_deep water water_shallow pebble; accents: blue_cloth orange_cloth terracotta book_red book_teal book_mustard paper) or '#rrggbb'; leaf ramps by `LEAVES` name ('summer' 'gold' 'moss') or a dark-to-light colour list.
- Both examples keep their layouts in `LAYOUTS` (`page` 1152×2048, `wide` 1920×1080: the window, the room's things, each tree clump's position), one `scene()` drawing both; the window seat adds `PRESETS` (`noon`, `late` evening gold: sky, sunlight colour and `tint`, wall colour, the leaf ramp).

## Order
Everything is far to near and big to small, with text composited last. Ink-wash mist must be painted after the things it will swallow; watercolour leaf stalks go first and are painted only outside the leaves.
